  "detected_pester_activity": "The YARA rule \"detected_pester_activity\" is designed to identify the execution of PowerShell scripts utilizing the Pester testing framework, specifically targeting the invocation of Pester with output formatting options that may indicate automated testing or script execution, which aligns with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001 for PowerShell-based scripting.   "ps_taskmodification_monitor": "The YARA rule \"ps_taskmodification_monitor\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell scripts that query Windows event logs for modifications to scheduled tasks, specifically targeting events associated with Event ID 4702, which indicates changes to task properties.   "suspicious_powershell_activity_45fef": "The YARA rule \"suspicious_powershell_activity_45fef\" is designed to detect potentially malicious PowerShell activity associated with in-memory execution and evasion techniques, specifically targeting the use of obfuscated commands and attempts to bypass security mechanisms such as AMSI (Antimalware Scan Interface).   "malicious_powershell_tcp_reconnect": "The YARA rule \"malicious_powershell_tcp_reconnect\" is designed to detect scripts that establish a reverse shell connection using PowerShell, specifically by leveraging TCP sockets to facilitate remote command execution.   "ps_t1119_powershell_collection": "The YARA rule \"ps_t1119_powershell_collection\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell scripts for data collection activities, specifically targeting the technique identified by MITRE ATT&CK as T1059.001, which involves the execution of commands and scripts via PowerShell.   "pester_test_script_detection_661a7": "The YARA rule \"pester_test_script_detection_661a7\" is designed to identify PowerShell scripts associated with Pester testing, which may be leveraged by adversaries to execute unauthorized or malicious code under the guise of legitimate testing activities.   "malicious_powershell_script_c0b49": "The YARA rule \"malicious_powershell_script_c0b49\" is designed to detect obfuscated PowerShell scripts that exhibit characteristics of string manipulation and encoding techniques commonly used to evade detection, specifically targeting scripts that may execute commands or payloads associated with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001 (PowerShell).   "malicious_powershell_backdoor_access": "The YARA rule \"malicious_powershell_backdoor_access\" is designed to detect PowerShell scripts that facilitate unauthorized backdoor access to tenant environments, specifically by identifying patterns indicative of compromised application identities, unauthorized access token usage, and the establishment of temporary access credentials.   "reverse_interactive_powershell": "The YARA rule \"reverse_interactive_powershell\" is designed to detect a PowerShell script that facilitates reverse shell access, allowing an attacker to remotely execute commands on a compromised system.   "ps_asr_rules_registry_mod": "The YARA rule \"ps_asr_rules_registry_mod\" is designed to detect modifications to the Windows registry related to Attack Surface Reduction (ASR) rules in Windows Defender, specifically targeting the registry path associated with ASR configurations.   "invoke_windowsenum_detection": "The YARA rule \"invoke_windowsenum_detection\" is designed to identify suspicious enumeration activities conducted by the Invoke-WindowsEnum PowerShell script, which is often used by adversaries to gather detailed information about a Windows system's configuration, user group memberships, recent file access, security software presence, and network defenses.   "powershell_pester_testing": "The YARA rule \"powershell_pester_testing\" is designed to detect the use of Pester, a testing framework for PowerShell scripts, which may be leveraged by adversaries to execute or test malicious scripts under the guise of legitimate script testing practices.   "powershell_edrchecker_detection": "The YARA rule \"powershell_edrchecker_detection\" is designed to identify the execution of a PowerShell script that performs reconnaissance activities to enumerate and assess the presence of endpoint detection and response (EDR) products on a system, potentially indicating a preparatory step for evasion or further malicious actions.   "powersploit_outminidump_detection": "The YARA rule \"powersploit_outminidump_detection\" is designed to identify the use of PowerSploit, a post-exploitation framework, specifically targeting the creation of memory dumps from processes using PowerShell commands.   "ps_amsi_bypass_309": "The YARA rule \"ps_amsi_bypass_309\" is designed to detect attempts to bypass the Antimalware Scan Interface (AMSI) in PowerShell scripts, a technique commonly used by attackers to evade detection and execute malicious code.   "detect_tokenprivilege_tool": "The YARA rule \"detect_tokenprivilege_tool\" is designed to identify the use of a PowerShell tool that manipulates token privileges, specifically targeting the technique of modifying process privileges to escalate permissions or bypass security controls, as categorized under MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001 (PowerShell). This detection focuses on identifying strings associated with privilege manipulation functions, such as \"enableprivilege\" and \"adjusttokenprivileges,\" which are indicative of attempts to exploit or abuse token privileges for unauthorized access or privilege escalation.",
  "ps_injection_293": "The YARA rule \"ps_injection_293\" is designed to detect instances of PowerShell injection, specifically targeting the technique identified by MITRE ATT&CK as T1059.001, which involves adversaries injecting malicious scripts or commands into PowerShell processes to execute unauthorized actions. The detection string \"$s = 'oijaaaayinlm'\" likely represents a unique identifier or pattern associated with a specific variant of this injection technique.",
  "generic_powershell_e2fbf_470": "The YARA rule \"generic_powershell_e2fbf_470\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell scripts that invoke the \"invoke-handlekatz\" function, which is indicative of credential theft activities associated with the Mimikatz tool.   "information_gathering_script": "The YARA rule \"information_gathering_script\" is designed to detect scripts that perform reconnaissance activities by collecting information about user accounts, their associated groups, and system boot times on a local machine, indicative of the PowerShell scripting technique for system discovery as outlined in MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001. This behavior is typically associated with adversaries seeking to understand the system environment and user privileges to facilitate further exploitation or lateral movement.",
  "invoke_printdemon": "The YARA rule \"invoke_printdemon\" is designed to detect the execution of the PrintDemon privilege escalation tool, which exploits vulnerabilities in the Windows Print Spooler service to elevate privileges using PowerShell commands and registry modifications. This rule identifies specific strings associated with the tool's operation, including indicators of print job initiation, registry manipulation, and PowerShell execution, aligning with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001 for command and scripting interpreter abuse.",
  "ps_windows_steal_chrome_cookies_windows_": "The YARA rule \"ps_windows_steal_chrome_cookies_windows_\" is designed to detect malicious activity involving the theft of cookies from Google Chrome on Windows systems, specifically targeting the manipulation of cookie attributes such as \"is_httponly\" and \"is_secure\" to potentially bypass security measures.   "findwmilocaladminaccess_detection": "The YARA rule \"findwmilocaladminaccess_detection\" is designed to identify the execution of the \"find-wmilocaladminaccess\" script, which is used to enumerate and verify local administrative access on remote systems via Windows Management Instrumentation (WMI), potentially indicating lateral movement or privilege escalation attempts.   "pester_powershell_testdetection": "The YARA rule \"pester_powershell_testdetection\" is designed to identify potentially malicious PowerShell scripts that misuse the Pester testing framework functionalities, which may indicate adversaries leveraging scripting techniques for execution or automation of malicious activities. Specifically, it targets scripts that utilize unusual or suspicious Pester-related commands, aligning with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves PowerShell-based execution for unauthorized actions.",
  "simulate_dnstunnel_script": "The YARA rule \"simulate_dnstunnel_script\" is designed to detect scripts associated with DNS tunneling, a technique used to covertly exfiltrate data or establish command and control channels by encoding information within DNS queries, which is categorized under MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001 (Command and Scripting Interpreter: PowerShell).   "powershell_minidump_malicious_activity": "The YARA rule \"powershell_minidump_malicious_activity\" is designed to detect potentially malicious activity involving the use of PowerShell to create or manipulate memory dumps, specifically targeting the technique of minidump creation as outlined in MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001.   "wsl_reverse_shell_detection": "The YARA rule \"wsl_reverse_shell_detection\" is designed to identify attempts to establish a reverse shell within the Windows Subsystem for Linux (WSL) environment using netcat, a technique associated with command and scripting interpreter abuse (MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001).   "malicious_powershell_pastebin": "The YARA rule \"malicious_powershell_pastebin\" is designed to detect a PowerShell script that potentially engages in malicious activity by uploading files to Pastebin, a common tactic for data exfiltration or sharing of unauthorized data.   "powershell_script_serverclient": "The YARA rule \"powershell_script_serverclient\" is designed to detect potentially malicious PowerShell scripts that operate in server and client modes to facilitate encrypted command transmission, indicative of command and control (C2) activities.   "invoke_adidnscheck": "The YARA rule \"invoke_adidnscheck\" is designed to detect the use of the PowerShell command \"invoke-adidnscheck,\" which is associated with the technique of Command and Scripting Interpreter (T1059.001) used by threat actors to query Active Directory DNS records for reconnaissance purposes.   "generic_powershell_b356c_475": "The YARA rule \"generic_powershell_b356c_475\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell scripts that employ the \"invoke-blocketw\" command, which is indicative of a technique used for network traffic manipulation or blocking, aligning with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001 (PowerShell).   "ps_potential_uacbypasscomputerdefaults": "The YARA rule \"ps_potential_uacbypasscomputerdefaults\" is designed to detect attempts to bypass User Account Control (UAC) by modifying registry settings associated with the \"ms-settings\" protocol handler to execute arbitrary commands via \"computerdefaults.exe,\" leveraging PowerShell commands such as \"set-itemproperty\" and \"start-process.\" This technique is categorized under MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves command and scripting interpreter abuse for privilege escalation or execution of unauthorized processes.",
  "geolocation_script_detection": "The YARA rule \"geolocation_script_detection\" is designed to identify the execution of PowerShell scripts that attempt to gather geolocation information by resolving local host data and querying external services such as ifconfig.me and ipapi.co, which may indicate reconnaissance activities associated with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001 (PowerShell).   "bruteforce_ad_detection": "The YARA rule \"bruteforce_ad_detection\" is designed to identify brute force attacks targeting Active Directory user accounts by detecting specific strings indicative of unauthorized attempts to systematically guess user credentials.   "ps_obfuscation_326": "The YARA rule \"ps_obfuscation_326\" is designed to detect obfuscated PowerShell scripts that attempt to evade detection by using various permutations of the word \"download,\" a technique associated with the execution of malicious scripts via PowerShell, as referenced by MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001.   "suspicious_powershell_script_14200": "The YARA rule \"suspicious_powershell_script_14200\" is designed to detect PowerShell scripts that exhibit potentially malicious behavior by utilizing functions such as \"loadlibrary,\" \"getprocaddress,\" and \"virtualprotect,\" which are indicative of attempts to execute code injection or memory manipulation techniques.   "powershell_hooking_amsi": "The YARA rule \"powershell_hooking_amsi\" is designed to detect PowerShell scripts that attempt to bypass the Antimalware Scan Interface (AMSI) by employing hooking techniques, which is indicative of adversaries trying to evade security mechanisms and execute potentially malicious code.   "ps_obfuscation_332": "The YARA rule \"ps_obfuscation_332\" is designed to detect obfuscated PowerShell scripts that utilize string manipulation techniques, such as reversing and joining characters, to conceal malicious commands or payloads.   "ps_windows_get_domaingroup_with_powerview": "The YARA rule \"ps_windows_get_domaingroup_with_powerview\" is designed to detect the use of PowerView, a PowerShell tool, to enumerate domain groups within a Windows environment, which is indicative of adversaries performing Active Directory enumeration as part of their reconnaissance activities.   "patchamsi_winshell_malicious": "The YARA rule \"patchamsi_winshell_malicious\" is designed to detect attempts to disable or bypass the Antimalware Scan Interface (AMSI) within PowerShell, a technique often employed by adversaries to execute malicious code without being detected by security software.   "ping_sweep_detection": "The YARA rule \"ping_sweep_detection\" is designed to identify the execution of a PowerShell script that performs a network reconnaissance technique known as a ping sweep, which systematically sends ICMP echo requests to a range of IP addresses (192.168.0.1 to 192.168.0.254) to discover active hosts within a local network.   "test_error_script": "The YARA rule \"test_error_script\" is designed to detect the presence of specific PowerShell script behaviors associated with testing error handling and mock function calls, which may indicate the use of scripting for adversarial purposes or the execution of test scripts that simulate error conditions.   "azure_powershell_deployment": "The YARA rule \"azure_powershell_deployment\" is designed to detect potentially malicious activities involving Azure automation and deployment processes executed via PowerShell, specifically targeting the use of Azure CLI commands and resource management operations that align with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which pertains to the use of command and scripting interpreters.   "suspicious_import_bucket": "The YARA rule \"suspicious_import_bucket\" is designed to detect potentially malicious activity in PowerShell scripts that manipulate the Scoop package manager environment, specifically targeting scripts that reference the \"scoop_home\" environment variable and perform import operations using the \"import-bucket-tests\" pattern.   "invokerunas_detection": "The \"invokerunas_detection\" YARA rule is designed to identify the execution of a potentially malicious PowerShell script that leverages the \"Invoke-RunAs\" technique to escalate privileges and execute code with elevated permissions, as indicated by the presence of suspicious strings related to privilege enabling and process creation.   "ps_injection_291": "The YARA rule \"ps_injection_291\" is designed to detect the presence of PowerShell injection techniques, specifically targeting the execution of shellcode within PowerShell scripts, as indicated by the detection string \".shellcode.\" This rule aligns with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the abuse of PowerShell for executing malicious code, potentially leading to unauthorized system access or control.",
  "malicious_invoke_validator": "The YARA rule \"malicious_invoke_validator\" is designed to detect scripts or binaries that utilize specific invocation patterns associated with PowerShell, particularly targeting the use of \"invoke-something,\" \"myvalidator,\" and \"validatescript\" strings, which may indicate the execution of potentially malicious or unauthorized validation scripts.   "ps_malicious_framework_314": "The YARA rule \"ps_malicious_framework_314\" is designed to detect the presence of a malicious PowerShell framework, specifically targeting the use of the \"@404death\" string, which is indicative of a script or tool associated with the execution of malicious commands or scripts via PowerShell, aligning with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001 (PowerShell).   "powershell_incidentresponsetoolkit_detection": "The YARA rule \"powershell_incidentresponsetoolkit_detection\" is designed to identify potentially malicious activities associated with the misuse of PowerShell for executing incident response toolkits, which may involve unauthorized account creation, manipulation of remote desktop services, creation of scheduled tasks, and thread injection.   "sharploginprompt_maliciousscript": "The YARA rule \"sharploginprompt_maliciousscript\" is designed to detect the use of the \"invoke-sharploginprompt\" command, which is indicative of potential malicious activities involving PowerShell scripts that leverage .NET reflection and assembly loading techniques to execute code in memory, aligning with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001 (PowerShell).   "ps_disable_schtasks_defender": "The YARA rule \"ps_disable_schtasks_defender\" is designed to detect malicious behavior associated with the use of PowerShell scripts to disable or delete scheduled tasks related to Windows Defender, potentially as part of an evasion technique.   "malicious_keystroke_capture": "The YARA rule \"malicious_keystroke_capture\" is designed to detect the execution of a PowerShell script that captures keystrokes and exfiltrates data, potentially indicating the presence of a keylogger.   "setremotewmi_malicious": "The YARA rule \"setremotewmi_malicious\" is designed to detect unauthorized modifications to Windows Management Instrumentation (WMI) namespace permissions, which can facilitate remote code execution or unauthorized access by identifying specific strings associated with malicious scripts, altered WMI providers, and changes to DCOM access control lists.   "ps_dde_atomic": "The YARA rule \"ps_dde_atomic\" is designed to detect the use of Dynamic Data Exchange (DDE) within PowerShell scripts, which is a technique associated with code execution and lateral movement, as indicated by MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001.   "ps_amsi_bypass_257": "The YARA rule \"ps_amsi_bypass_257\" is designed to detect attempts to bypass the Antimalware Scan Interface (AMSI) in PowerShell scripts by identifying obfuscated patterns of the \"GetField\" method, which is commonly used in AMSI bypass techniques.   "winget_application_management": "The YARA rule \"winget_application_management\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell scripts that manage applications from the Microsoft Store, potentially indicating unauthorized or malicious manipulation of application installations and removals.   "blindghost_malware_detection": "The \"blindghost_malware_detection\" YARA rule is designed to identify a PowerShell script associated with the \"BlindGhost\" malware, which employs techniques such as LLMNR/NBT-NS spoofing and WPAD manipulation to facilitate network-based attacks and potentially redirect traffic to malicious servers. This rule targets specific patterns indicative of network exploitation and unauthorized resource access, aligning with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves command and scripting interpreter abuse.",
  "ps_suspicious_vbs_file_run_from_startup_folder": "The YARA rule \"ps_suspicious_vbs_file_run_from_startup_folder\" is designed to detect the execution of potentially malicious Visual Basic Script (VBS) files from the startup folder, which may indicate persistence mechanisms employed by adversaries to maintain access on a compromised system. This rule specifically targets behaviors associated with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001 (Command and Scripting Interpreter: Visual Basic), and T1547.001 (Boot or Logon Autostart Execution: Registry Run Keys/Startup Folder), highlighting the use of \"copy-item\" commands and the presence of \"vbsstartup.vbs\" in the startup directory path.",
  "invoke_egresscheck": "The YARA rule \"invoke_egresscheck\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell scripts that employ the \"invoke-egresscheck\" function, which is indicative of adversaries executing scripts to test network egress capabilities as part of command and scripting interpreter techniques, specifically aligning with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001. This behavior is commonly associated with lateral movement or data exfiltration attempts, where attackers assess outbound network connectivity to facilitate further malicious activities.",
  "privilege_adjustment_detection": "The YARA rule \"privilege_adjustment_detection\" is designed to identify malicious PowerShell scripts that attempt to manipulate token privileges, specifically targeting techniques for privilege escalation by adjusting or enabling privileges such as \"SeDebugPrivilege\" using functions like \"EnablePrivilege\", \"AdjustTokenPrivileges\", \"OpenProcessToken\", and \"LookupPrivilegeValue\". This detection aligns with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the use of scripting for execution and potential unauthorized access to sensitive system operations.",
  "ps_art_ttp_38": "The YARA rule \"ps_art_ttp_38\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell scripts that manipulate Windows network settings, specifically targeting the Winsock2 parameters in the registry to load alternative DLLs such as \"altwinsock2dll.dll,\" which is indicative of the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001 (PowerShell). This behavior suggests potential network configuration tampering or persistence mechanisms employed by adversaries to maintain control or intercept network communications.",
  "bloodhoundold_detection": "The YARA rule \"bloodhoundold_detection\" is designed to identify the use of BloodHound, a tool commonly employed by attackers to map and analyze Active Directory environments for privilege escalation paths, by detecting specific strings associated with LDAP queries and Active Directory enumeration techniques. The rule targets behaviors related to the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves PowerShell scripting for executing reconnaissance and data collection activities within a network.",
  "ps_art_ttp_10": "The YARA rule \"ps_art_ttp_10\" is designed to detect PowerShell-based application path bypass techniques, specifically targeting scripts that utilize \"invoke-apppathbypass\" and \"downloadstring\" commands, which are indicative of adversaries attempting to execute unauthorized code or download malicious content via PowerShell. This rule aligns with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, highlighting the use of PowerShell for command and script execution in a potentially harmful manner.",
  "ps_kerberos_ticket_access_364": "The YARA rule \"ps_kerberos_ticket_access_364\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell scripts that access Kerberos tickets, specifically identifying the manipulation or request of Kerberos security tokens, which is indicative of credential theft or lateral movement attempts within a network. This rule aligns with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, highlighting the use of PowerShell for malicious scripting activities.",
  "domainpasswordspray_maliciousactivity": "The YARA rule \"domainpasswordspray_maliciousactivity\" is designed to detect malicious PowerShell script activity associated with password spray attacks, a technique where an attacker attempts to gain unauthorized access by systematically trying a single password against multiple user accounts. The rule identifies specific strings indicative of such activity, including script commands and output messages related to the execution and success of password spraying attempts, aligning with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001 (PowerShell).",
  "detect_malicious_parent_pid_spoofing": "The YARA rule \"detect_malicious_parent_pid_spoofing\" identifies PowerShell scripts that attempt to spoof the parent process ID, a technique used to disguise the origin of a process and evade detection, aligning with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001. It detects specific strings indicative of this behavior, such as \"parent process id\" and \"processnativemethods,\" which are commonly associated with malicious process manipulation and execution.",
  "eventsvccreation_detection": "The YARA rule \"eventsvccreation_detection\" is designed to identify potentially malicious PowerShell activity associated with the creation of services, specifically targeting the use of PowerShell commands and scripts that query or manipulate event logs related to service creation events, as indicated by the presence of strings such as \"get-eventsvccreation\" and \"wevtutil query-events.\" This detection aligns with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the use of PowerShell for execution, often leveraged by threat actors to establish persistence or escalate privileges through unauthorized service creation.",
  "ps_potential_credential_dump_1082": "The YARA rule \"ps_potential_credential_dump_1082\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell scripts that attempt to add a security package, indicative of credential dumping activities. This behavior aligns with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the use of PowerShell for executing malicious commands to potentially extract sensitive authentication data from a system.",
  "ps_obfuscation_285": "The YARA rule \"ps_obfuscation_285\" is designed to detect obfuscated PowerShell scripts that utilize the \"documents.open('http\" pattern, indicative of attempts to execute remote scripts via HTTP, aligning with the MITRE technique T1059.001 for PowerShell. This rule targets the identification of potentially malicious behavior involving script-based execution and delivery of payloads from external sources.",
  "malicious_powershell_activity_afc2d": "The YARA rule \"malicious_powershell_activity_afc2d\" is designed to detect potentially malicious PowerShell scripts that exhibit behaviors indicative of code execution, such as using the \"sapi.spvoice\" component for text-to-speech functionality and suppressing output, aligning with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the abuse of PowerShell for execution. This rule targets scripts that may be used for stealthy operations or obfuscation in cyber attacks.",
  "out_compresseddll": "The YARA rule \"out_compresseddll\" is designed to detect the presence of a compressed DLL file associated with the execution of malicious scripts or code, specifically targeting the technique of PowerShell scripting as outlined in MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001. This rule identifies potential threats where adversaries use compressed DLLs to obfuscate and execute malicious payloads via PowerShell, indicating a risk of script-based attacks.",
  "generic_powershell_e9287_136": "The YARA rule \"generic_powershell_e9287_136\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell scripts that employ the \"invoke-shield\" command, which is indicative of potentially malicious activity related to command and scripting interpreter abuse, specifically targeting PowerShell as outlined in MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001. This rule aims to identify scripts that may be used for executing unauthorized or harmful operations within a system, leveraging PowerShell's capabilities for script-based attacks.",
  "privilege_elevation_notepad_loop": "The YARA rule \"privilege_elevation_notepad_loop\" is designed to detect a PowerShell script that attempts to elevate privileges by modifying execution policies for the current user and repeatedly launching instances of Notepad in a maximized window, potentially as a distraction or to maintain persistence. This behavior aligns with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the use of scripting for execution, indicating a potential threat of privilege escalation and unauthorized process creation.",
  "filemace_timestamp_manipulation": "The YARA rule \"filemace_timestamp_manipulation\" is designed to detect the execution of a PowerShell script that alters file timestamps, potentially to obfuscate unauthorized file modifications or access, aligning with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves scripting for execution. This rule identifies specific strings indicative of timestamp manipulation activities, suggesting an attempt to evade forensic analysis or audit trails.",
  "powershell_amsipatch_detection": "The YARA rule \"powershell_amsipatch_detection\" is designed to identify attempts to bypass or patch the Antimalware Scan Interface (AMSI) in PowerShell processes, which is a technique associated with evading security mechanisms and executing potentially malicious code without detection. This rule specifically targets indicators of AMSI manipulation, such as identifying the process architecture, AMSI DLL handle, and targeted memory addresses, aligning with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001 for PowerShell.",
  "malicious_trusteddocuments_script": "The YARA rule \"malicious_trusteddocuments_script\" is designed to detect potentially malicious PowerShell scripts that manipulate or enumerate trusted documents, which may indicate an attempt to bypass security controls by exploiting trusted document settings. This behavior aligns with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the use of scripting for execution, potentially allowing an attacker to execute unauthorized actions or gain persistence on a compromised system.",
  "ps_webserver_logging_art": "The YARA rule \"ps_webserver_logging_art\" is designed to detect attempts to modify web server configurations to disable HTTP logging, specifically by identifying the use of the \"set-webconfigurationproperty\" command with parameters related to \"httplogging\" and \"dontlog\" set to \"true.\" This behavior is indicative of adversaries employing the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001 (PowerShell), aiming to evade detection by suppressing logging of web server activities.",
  "malicious_powershell_script_detection_2f66e": "The YARA rule \"malicious_powershell_script_detection_2f66e\" is designed to identify potentially malicious PowerShell scripts that exhibit behaviors associated with code execution, network communication, and data encoding, which are indicative of the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001 (PowerShell). This rule detects patterns such as the use of \"iex\" for executing expressions, \"new-object\" for creating COM objects, and \"base64 encoded string\" for obfuscation, which are commonly employed in scripts to facilitate unauthorized access, data exfiltration, or lateral movement within a network.",
  "ps_obfuscation_284": "The YARA rule \"ps_obfuscation_284\" is designed to detect the use of \"certutil.exe\" with the \"/ping\" parameter, which is indicative of command and scripting interpreter obfuscation techniques (MITRE ATT&CK T1059.001) often employed by threat actors to evade detection while executing malicious commands. This behavior suggests an attempt to misuse legitimate Windows utilities for unauthorized network communication or data exfiltration.",
  "ps_windows_stage_data_from_discovery_bat": "The YARA rule \"ps_windows_stage_data_from_discovery_bat\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell to download a file named \"discovery.bat\" from the internet and save it locally, which aligns with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1074.001, indicating potential data staging for exfiltration or further malicious activity. This rule identifies a specific pattern of behavior that may be indicative of a threat actor preparing to execute or distribute malicious scripts within a Windows environment.",
  "ps_invoke_expression_1110": "The YARA rule \"ps_invoke_expression_1110\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell commands that utilize the \"Invoke-Expression\" cmdlet or its alias \"iex,\" which are commonly employed by attackers to execute arbitrary code or scripts, often as part of command and script execution techniques. This rule specifically targets behaviors associated with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, highlighting potential script-based threats that may facilitate unauthorized access or execution within a compromised environment.",
  "azure_role_assignment_abuse": "The YARA rule \"azure_role_assignment_abuse\" is designed to detect potential misuse of Azure role assignments through PowerShell scripts, specifically identifying attempts to obtain access tokens and manipulate authorization headers using REST API calls, which aligns with the MITRE technique T1059.001 (PowerShell). This rule targets suspicious activities that may indicate privilege escalation or unauthorized access within Azure environments by monitoring for specific command patterns associated with token handling and API invocation.",
  "invoke_createcertificate": "The YARA rule \"invoke_createcertificate\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell scripts that invoke the creation of digital certificates, which may be indicative of adversaries leveraging PowerShell for malicious activities such as credential theft or establishing persistence.   "ps_art_ttp_11": "The YARA rule \"ps_art_ttp_11\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell commands, specifically targeting the execution of scripts or commands via \"iex\" (Invoke-Expression) and \"invoke-wmiexec,\" which are indicative of adversaries leveraging PowerShell for remote code execution or lateral movement, aligning with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001.   "ps_art_ttp_39": "The YARA rule \"ps_art_ttp_39\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell for executing commands via COM objects, specifically targeting the technique of using \"Activator::CreateInstance\" and \"GetTypeFromProgID\" to instantiate and manipulate COM objects like \"MMC20.Application\" for executing shell commands, which aligns with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001 (PowerShell).   "ps_obfuscation_247": "The YARA rule \"ps_obfuscation_247\" is designed to detect obfuscated PowerShell scripts that utilize the \"verbosepreference.tostring()[\" pattern, indicative of attempts to evade detection by manipulating PowerShell's verbosity settings.   "malicious_powershell_enum": "The YARA rule \"malicious_powershell_enum\" is designed to detect PowerShell scripts that facilitate the enumeration of Microsoft online accounts, potentially for credential stuffing or password spraying attacks, as indicated by strings related to user enumeration and password manipulation. This rule targets the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the use of PowerShell for executing malicious scripts to compromise user accounts and bypass multi-factor authentication mechanisms.",
  "amsi_bypass_tool_detection": "The YARA rule \"amsi_bypass_tool_detection\" is designed to identify PowerShell scripts that employ techniques to bypass the Antimalware Scan Interface (AMSI), a security feature in Windows that helps detect and block malicious scripts. By detecting specific strings and patterns associated with AMSI bypass methods, this rule targets scripts that attempt to evade security measures, aligning with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves command and scripting interpreter abuse.",
  "amsi_bypass_detection_702a9": "The YARA rule \"amsi_bypass_detection_702a9\" is designed to detect attempts to bypass the Antimalware Scan Interface (AMSI) in PowerShell scripts, which is a technique associated with the MITRE ATT&CK tactic T1059.001 (PowerShell).   "powersploit_gpppassword_extraction": "The YARA rule \"powersploit_gpppassword_extraction\" is designed to detect the use of the PowerSploit framework to extract plaintext passwords from Group Policy Preferences, a technique associated with the execution of malicious scripts or commands in PowerShell (MITRE ATT&CK Technique T1059.001).   "generic_powershell_9fee8_718": "The YARA rule \"generic_powershell_9fee8_718\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell scripts that attempt to bypass User Account Control (UAC) through token manipulation, a technique associated with privilege escalation. This detection aligns with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the use of PowerShell for executing potentially malicious commands or scripts.",
  "malicious_powershell_tcpclient_f6fe1": "The YARA rule \"malicious_powershell_tcpclient_f6fe1\" is designed to detect potentially malicious PowerShell scripts that utilize the TcpClient class to establish network connections, likely for unauthorized data transmission or remote command execution, as indicated by the presence of specific strings related to socket communication and data handling. This rule targets the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the use of scripting for execution, highlighting the risk of PowerShell being leveraged for lateral movement or exfiltration activities.",
  "powershell_logging_bypass": "The YARA rule \"powershell_logging_bypass\" is designed to detect attempts by PowerShell scripts to evade logging mechanisms, specifically targeting techniques that disable or manipulate script block logging, as indicated by the presence of strings such as \"cachedgrouppolicy\" and \"enablescriptblock.\" This rule aligns with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the use of PowerShell for execution, highlighting potential threats related to logging evasion and unauthorized script execution.",
  "ps_token_manipulation_1077": "The YARA rule \"ps_token_manipulation_1077\" is designed to detect the use of the \"CreateProcessWithToken\" function, which is indicative of a technique where adversaries manipulate process tokens to execute code with elevated privileges.   "invoke_reverttoself": "The YARA rule \"invoke_reverttoself\" is designed to detect the use of the \"Invoke-RevertToSelf\" method, which is associated with PowerShell scripting for impersonation and privilege escalation, aligning with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001.   "malicious_get_powershellhistory": "The YARA rule \"malicious_get_powershellhistory\" is designed to detect potentially malicious PowerShell scripts that attempt to access and retrieve PowerShell command history from remote computers, which may indicate unauthorized surveillance or data exfiltration activities.   "generic_powershell_9e6cd_311": "The YARA rule \"generic_powershell_9e6cd_311\" is designed to detect the use of specific PowerShell scripts that perform system reconnaissance, credential checking, and data pattern analysis, which are indicative of post-exploitation activities.   "spoolsampleng_detection": "The YARA rule \"spoolsampleng_detection\" is designed to identify malicious PowerShell scripts that exploit the \"spoolsample\" technique, which is associated with in-memory execution and potential privilege escalation through unconstrained delegation.   "generic_powershell_c84de_26": "The YARA rule \"generic_powershell_c84de_26\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell scripts that employ the \"invoke-inmemoryassembly\" technique, which is indicative of in-memory execution of malicious code without writing to disk.   "get_microphoneaudio_detection": "The YARA rule \"get_microphoneaudio_detection\" is designed to identify malicious PowerShell scripts that utilize the \"get-microphoneaudio\" function to record audio from a system's microphone, potentially for unauthorized surveillance or data exfiltration purposes.   "malicious_ad_user_creation": "The YARA rule \"malicious_ad_user_creation\" is designed to detect potentially unauthorized or suspicious activities related to the creation of Active Directory users and their addition to privileged groups, such as \"domain admins\" and \"backup operators,\" which may indicate attempts to escalate privileges or establish persistence within a network.   "generic_powershell_cb504_85": "The YARA rule \"generic_powershell_cb504_85\" is designed to detect the use of obfuscated or potentially malicious PowerShell scripts, specifically identifying the invocation of the \"invoke-schnff\" function, which may be associated with script-based execution techniques.   "ps_windows_icedid_botnet_http_put": "The YARA rule \"ps_windows_icedid_botnet_http_put\" is designed to detect malicious activity associated with the IcedID botnet, specifically identifying the creation of a text file that is subsequently uploaded to a server using the HTTP PUT method with a content-type header, followed by the deletion of the created file.   "malicious_lnk_creation_detection": "The YARA rule \"malicious_lnk_creation_detection\" is designed to identify suspicious PowerShell script activity that creates shortcut (.lnk) files in the startup directory, potentially indicating an attempt to establish persistence on a system by executing scripts or programs automatically upon user login.   "malicious_template_injection": "The YARA rule \"malicious_template_injection\" is designed to detect a malicious PowerShell script that facilitates the injection of payloads into Microsoft Word templates, indicative of a technique used to execute code via Office macros.   "generic_powershell_ff17b_722": "The YARA rule \"generic_powershell_ff17b_722\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell scripts associated with post-exploitation frameworks, specifically identifying strings related to \"invoke-empire\" and \"invoke-shellcommand,\" which are indicative of command and control activities.   "ps_web_post_linenumbers": "The YARA rule \"ps_web_post_linenumbers\" is designed to detect PowerShell scripts that exhibit behavior consistent with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, specifically identifying scripts that manipulate files and perform web requests, potentially for data exfiltration or command-and-control communication. The rule targets scripts that create or modify a file named \"linenumbers.txt\" in the temporary environment, utilize commands such as \"test-path\" and \"add-content,\" and make HTTP requests to \"example.com,\" indicating possible malicious activity involving data staging and transmission.",
  "generic_powershell_ef476_743": "The YARA rule \"generic_powershell_ef476_743\" is designed to detect the use of obfuscated or potentially malicious PowerShell scripts that employ dynamic invocation techniques, as indicated by the presence of strings such as \"invoke-dynamiccommand,\" \"invoke-check,\" and \"invoke-usercheck.\" This rule targets the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the execution of commands and scripts through PowerShell, often used by threat actors to execute code, bypass security controls, and maintain persistence on compromised systems.",
  "generic_powershell_99aec_421": "The YARA rule \"generic_powershell_99aec_421\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell scripts that employ the \"invoke-peinjection\" technique, which is indicative of process injection—a method used by attackers to execute malicious code within the address space of another process.   "powershell_reverseshell_detection": "The YARA rule \"powershell_reverseshell_detection\" is designed to identify malicious PowerShell scripts that facilitate reverse shell connections, a technique where an attacker gains remote access to a compromised system by initiating a connection from the victim's machine to the attacker's server.   "malicious_scheduled_task_detection": "The YARA rule \"malicious_scheduled_task_detection\" is designed to identify potentially malicious activity involving the retrieval and manipulation of scheduled tasks through PowerShell, which may indicate an adversary's attempt to execute or manage tasks for persistence or privilege escalation. It targets specific PowerShell commands and patterns associated with the enumeration and modification of scheduled tasks, aligning with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the use of scripting for execution.",
  "privilege_adjustment_powershell": "The YARA rule \"privilege_adjustment_powershell\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell scripts that attempt to adjust process privileges, specifically targeting techniques for privilege escalation or unauthorized access to system resources.   "malicious_start_processasuser": "The YARA rule \"malicious_start_processasuser\" is designed to detect the execution of scripts that utilize the \"Start-ProcessAsUser\" technique, potentially indicating malicious activity such as credential theft or unauthorized process creation under a different user context, as characterized by MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001 (PowerShell). The detection strings suggest monitoring for attempts to manipulate credentials, create processes, and handle secure strings, which are indicative of attempts to execute code with elevated privileges or impersonate users.",
  "detect_find_wmilocaladminaccess": "The YARA rule \"detect_find_wmilocaladminaccess\" is designed to identify the execution of the \"find-wmilocaladminaccess\" script, which is associated with the abuse of Windows Management Instrumentation (WMI) for local administrative access enumeration, a technique categorized under MITRE ATT&CK's T1059.001 (PowerShell). This detection targets specific script patterns indicative of unauthorized privilege escalation attempts or reconnaissance activities within a network environment.",
  "mouseclick_screenshot_capture": "The YARA rule \"mouseclick_screenshot_capture\" is designed to detect scripts that utilize the Windows Problem Steps Recorder (psr.exe) to capture screenshots in response to mouse click events, potentially indicating unauthorized monitoring or data exfiltration activities.   "prefetch_manager_tool": "The YARA rule \"prefetch_manager_tool\" is designed to detect a PowerShell script that manipulates Windows prefetch files, potentially indicating unauthorized system optimization or forensic artifact tampering.   "sqlserviceaccountpwhashdetection": "The YARA rule \"sqlserviceaccountpwhashdetection\" is designed to identify the execution of the \"get-sqlserviceaccountpwhash.ps1\" script, which is associated with credential theft and lateral movement techniques targeting SQL service accounts by capturing password hashes. This detection aligns with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, indicating the use of PowerShell for malicious activities such as sniffing network traffic, attacking SQL services, and exploiting SQL stored procedures like \"xp_dirtree\" and \"xp_fileexist\" to facilitate unauthorized access and data exfiltration.",
  "uac_bypass_script_detection": "The YARA rule \"uac_bypass_script_detection\" is designed to identify the use of PowerShell scripts that attempt to bypass User Account Control (UAC) by exploiting registry manipulation techniques, specifically targeting the \"HKCU:\\Software\\Classes\" path and the \"ms-settings\\shell\\open\" registry key to execute code with elevated privileges without user consent. This detection aligns with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves scripting for execution, highlighting a potential privilege escalation threat.",
  "powershell_urlcheck_script": "The YARA rule \"powershell_urlcheck_script\" is designed to detect potentially malicious PowerShell scripts that utilize web-based operations, specifically identifying scripts that invoke URL checks, use the System.Net.WebClient class to download content, and mimic legitimate user-agent strings such as \"Mozilla/5.0,\" which are indicative of command and control or data exfiltration activities. This rule targets the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the use of PowerShell for execution, highlighting potential threats related to script-based network communication and credential manipulation through \"convertto-securestring.\"",
  "suspiciouspowershellactivity": "The YARA rule \"suspiciouspowershellactivity\" is designed to detect potentially malicious PowerShell activity by identifying scripts that create new aliases, decode Base64 strings, and concatenate strings indicative of obfuscation techniques.   "sendtopastebin_detection": "The YARA rule \"sendtopastebin_detection\" is designed to identify malicious PowerShell scripts that facilitate the exfiltration of data to Pastebin, a common tactic used by threat actors to covertly transfer stolen information.   "ps_obfuscation_333": "The YARA rule \"ps_obfuscation_333\" is designed to detect obfuscated PowerShell scripts that utilize unconventional character sequences, specifically identifying patterns where non-standard characters are followed by a backtick (`) in succession.   "gptobfuscation_script": "The YARA rule \"gptobfuscation_script\" is designed to detect the use of the Invoke-GPTObfuscation PowerShell tool, which is associated with obfuscating scripts to evade detection, a technique categorized under MITRE ATT&CK's T1059.001 (PowerShell).   "maliciousprocesslibrarydetection": "The YARA rule \"maliciousprocesslibrarydetection\" is designed to identify potentially malicious activities related to process library manipulation, specifically targeting techniques that involve executing scripts or code via process injection or manipulation, as indicated by MITRE technique T1059.001.   "malicious_sharp_assembly_exec": "The YARA rule \"malicious_sharp_assembly_exec\" is designed to detect the execution of potentially malicious .NET assemblies via PowerShell scripts, specifically identifying the use of the \"invoke-sharp\" command to load and execute assemblies, which aligns with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001 (PowerShell).   "generic_powershell_27b8f_463": "The YARA rule \"generic_powershell_27b8f_463\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell scripts that employ the \"invoke-sessionbunny\" command, which is indicative of adversaries leveraging PowerShell for execution, specifically targeting the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001 (PowerShell).   "malicious_process_termination_script": "The YARA rule \"malicious_process_termination_script\" is designed to detect scripts that employ the \"stop-process -force\" command to terminate processes associated with common blue team applications and utilities, such as Wireshark, Microsoft Edge, Chrome, and PowerShell, potentially disrupting security monitoring and analysis activities.   "pesterfilecontentcheck": "The YARA rule \"pesterfilecontentcheck\" is designed to detect scripts or files that utilize specific strings associated with PowerShell commands for file content matching and validation, potentially indicating the use of PowerShell for malicious scripting or automation tasks.   "disable_amsi_techniques": "The YARA rule \"disable_amsi_techniques\" is designed to detect PowerShell scripts that attempt to disable the Antimalware Scan Interface (AMSI) using various methods, which is a common tactic employed by adversaries to bypass security measures and execute malicious code undetected.   "invoke_downgradeattack": "The YARA rule \"invoke_downgradeattack\" is designed to detect the presence of scripts or commands that attempt to execute a downgrade attack using PowerShell, specifically targeting the technique identified by MITRE ATT&CK as T1059.001, which involves the use of PowerShell for malicious purposes.   "invoke_ntlmauth_detection": "The YARA rule \"invoke_ntlmauth_detection\" is designed to identify the use of the \"invoke-ntlmauth\" PowerShell function, which is indicative of potential NTLM authentication abuse, often associated with lateral movement or credential theft techniques.   "ps_framework_rubeus": "The YARA rule \"ps_framework_rubeus\" is designed to detect the presence of \"Rubeus,\" a tool commonly used for Kerberos ticket manipulation and credential theft, which aligns with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, indicating the use of PowerShell for execution.   "ps_adfs_token": "The YARA rule \"ps_adfs_token\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell commands associated with the AADInternals module, specifically targeting the exportation of ADFS certificates, which may indicate attempts to compromise or manipulate Active Directory Federation Services (ADFS) tokens.   "ps_art_ttp_28": "The YARA rule \"ps_art_ttp_28\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell commands, specifically targeting the enumeration of hidden files and directories within the user's AppData paths, which are commonly associated with credential storage.   "ps_reverse_shell_298": "The YARA rule \"ps_reverse_shell_298\" is designed to detect the presence of PowerShell-based reverse shell activity, specifically targeting scripts that utilize the \"Invoke-Expression\" command to execute code and redirect output using \"2>&1\" in conjunction with network communication via \"tcpclient,\" indicative of the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001 (PowerShell). This rule identifies attempts to establish unauthorized remote access to a system by capturing patterns associated with reverse shell creation and execution.",
  "suspicious_powershell_idletime": "The YARA rule \"suspicious_powershell_idletime\" is designed to detect potentially malicious PowerShell scripts that utilize idle time logic to execute code stealthily, often by loading assemblies and decoding base64 strings, which may indicate attempts to evade detection and perform unauthorized actions. This rule targets behaviors associated with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the use of PowerShell for execution, particularly focusing on scripts that leverage user input and idle time to trigger malicious activities.",
  "ps_art_ttp_14": "The YARA rule \"ps_art_ttp_14\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell commands that modify registry settings related to Internet configurations, specifically targeting the \"HKCU:\\Software\\Microsoft\\Windows\\CurrentVersion\\Internet Settings\" path, which is indicative of the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001 (PowerShell).   "malicious_powershell_script_4666a": "The YARA rule \"malicious_powershell_script_4666a\" is designed to detect the presence of a reverse shell implemented in PowerShell scripts, which is a technique used by attackers to gain remote control over a compromised system. It identifies specific patterns associated with network socket manipulation, data encoding, and command execution indicative of MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the use of PowerShell for malicious purposes.",
  "ps_av_enumeration_1188": "The YARA rule \"ps_av_enumeration_1188\" is designed to detect scripts or binaries that enumerate installed antivirus products on a system, which is indicative of adversaries attempting to identify security software as part of system discovery or evasion techniques.   "detect_malicious_powershell_ntlm_extract": "The YARA rule \"detect_malicious_powershell_ntlm_extract\" is designed to identify PowerShell scripts that are involved in the extraction of NTLM credentials, a technique associated with credential access and lateral movement within compromised systems. Specifically, it targets scripts that utilize functions and variables indicative of NTLM credential harvesting, such as \"invoke-ntlmextract\" and manipulation of registry keys, aligning with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001 for PowerShell-based execution.",
  "generic_powershell_be471_658": "The YARA rule \"generic_powershell_be471_658\" is designed to detect the execution of potentially malicious PowerShell scripts, specifically those utilizing the \"invoke-puppies\" command, which may indicate the use of obfuscated or unauthorized script execution techniques.   "generic_powershell_2d40f_536": "The YARA rule \"generic_powershell_2d40f_536\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell scripts that invoke the \"mimidogz\" function, which is indicative of credential theft activities associated with the Mimikatz tool. This rule targets the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the use of PowerShell for malicious purposes, specifically for accessing and extracting sensitive information from compromised systems.",
  "malicious_powershell_commands": "The YARA rule \"malicious_powershell_commands\" is designed to detect potentially malicious PowerShell activity that involves executing web-based commands, specifically targeting scripts that utilize techniques such as bypassing execution policies, basic parsing of web requests, and encoding manipulations, which are indicative of adversaries attempting to download or execute payloads from the internet. This rule aligns with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, highlighting the use of PowerShell for command and scripting interpreter purposes, often employed in initial access or lateral movement phases of cyber attacks.",
  "psasyncshell_malware": "The YARA rule \"psasyncshell_malware\" is designed to detect the presence of a script associated with malicious remote shell capabilities, specifically targeting the use of PowerShell for command and script execution, as indicated by MITRE technique T1059.001. The detection strings suggest the rule identifies scripts that establish remote shell sessions, encode data, and manage asynchronous operations, which are indicative of potential unauthorized remote access and control activities.",
  "adcs_forestexploiter": "The YARA rule \"adcs_forestexploiter\" is designed to detect malicious activity associated with the exploitation of Active Directory Certificate Services (ADCS) within a forest environment, specifically targeting script-based execution techniques as indicated by the MITRE tag T1059.001. The detection strings suggest the rule identifies obfuscated patterns and enumeration activities typical of an attacker attempting to gain unauthorized access or escalate privileges within an ADCS infrastructure.",
  "ps_token_manipulation_1072": "The YARA rule \"ps_token_manipulation_1072\" is designed to detect the use of the \"GetTokenInformation\" function, which is indicative of a technique where adversaries manipulate process tokens to escalate privileges or bypass security controls, aligning with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001 (Command and Scripting Interpreter: PowerShell). This behavior is commonly associated with attempts to gain unauthorized access or execute commands with elevated permissions on a compromised system.",
  "suspicious_getnetstat_script": "The YARA rule \"suspicious_getnetstat_script\" is designed to detect the execution of a potentially malicious PowerShell script that mimics the behavior of the netstat command to gather network connection information, possibly for unauthorized monitoring or data exfiltration purposes. This rule targets the technique of command and scripting interpreter abuse, specifically PowerShell, as outlined in MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, indicating a threat actor's attempt to covertly execute network diagnostics and capture system data.",
  "generic_powershell_c236e_355": "The YARA rule \"generic_powershell_c236e_355\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell scripts that invoke the PsExec tool, a technique commonly associated with lateral movement and remote code execution within a network, as categorized under MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001. This rule targets the identification of potentially malicious activity where adversaries leverage PowerShell to execute commands on remote systems, often indicative of unauthorized access or propagation of malware.",
  "ps_chrome_history": "The YARA rule \"ps_chrome_history\" is designed to detect potential unauthorized access or exfiltration of Google Chrome browsing history, indicated by the presence of strings related to user identity (\"whoami\"), browser data (\"google\", \"chromebrowsinghistory.txt\"), and file paths (\"appdata\", \"history\"). This rule targets the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves command and scripting interpreter activity, suggesting a threat actor may be leveraging scripts to gather and possibly exfiltrate sensitive browsing data from a compromised system.",
  "malicious_powershell_enumservices": "The YARA rule \"malicious_powershell_enumservices\" is designed to detect potentially malicious PowerShell scripts that target service path audits by identifying unquoted service paths and checking for writeable paths, which can be exploited for privilege escalation. This rule aligns with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, indicating the use of PowerShell for executing commands that enumerate, audit, and potentially modify service paths to facilitate unauthorized access or persistence.",
  "invoke_gherkin_script_detection": "The YARA rule \"invoke_gherkin_script_detection\" is designed to identify the execution of Gherkin scripts, which are indicative of behavior associated with scripting and automation frameworks potentially used for malicious purposes, such as executing unauthorized or harmful scripts.   "invoke_servicestart": "The YARA rule \"invoke_servicestart\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell scripts that initiate service start commands, which is indicative of the technique T1059.001 under MITRE ATT&CK, representing the use of PowerShell for execution. This behavior is commonly associated with adversaries attempting to execute malicious payloads or scripts by leveraging PowerShell to start or manipulate services on a compromised system.",
  "ps_injection_242": "The YARA rule \"ps_injection_242\" is designed to detect the presence of PowerShell injection attacks, specifically targeting the execution of shellcode within PowerShell scripts, as indicated by the detection string \"$shellcode.\" This rule aligns with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the abuse of PowerShell for executing malicious code, potentially leading to unauthorized system access or control.",
  "ps_windows_dns_regular_beaconing": "The YARA rule \"ps_windows_dns_regular_beaconing\" is designed to detect malicious behavior indicative of an infected host communicating with a command and control (C2) server through DNS queries at regular intervals, a technique commonly associated with covert data exfiltration or remote command execution. This rule specifically targets the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1071.004, which involves the use of DNS as a communication channel for C2 traffic, often characterized by patterns such as specific query types, domain and subdomain usage, and configurable intervals and jitter in the beaconing process.",
  "uac_bypass_script": "The YARA rule \"uac_bypass_script\" is designed to detect PowerShell scripts that attempt to bypass User Account Control (UAC) for privilege escalation, specifically targeting techniques that manipulate execution policies and utilize specific DLLs and commands to elevate privileges without user consent.   "onedrive_file_monitor": "The YARA rule \"onedrive_file_monitor\" is designed to detect potentially malicious PowerShell scripts that monitor and manipulate OneDrive files by identifying specific patterns such as file names (\"license_info.txt\" and \"version_info.txt\") and PowerShell commands (\"invoke-expression\", \"copy-item -path\", \"write-progress -activity\", \"start-job -name\"). This rule targets the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the use of PowerShell for execution, potentially indicating unauthorized access or data exfiltration activities.",
  "malicious_powershell_email_activity": "The YARA rule \"malicious_powershell_email_activity\" is designed to detect PowerShell scripts that exhibit behaviors indicative of unauthorized access or manipulation of Microsoft Exchange email accounts, including actions such as reading, sending, or stopping Outlook processes, and creating log files or listing folders. This rule targets the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the use of PowerShell for malicious purposes, specifically focusing on email-related activities that may signify a threat actor's attempt to compromise or exploit email systems.",
  "ps_golden_ticket_attack": "The YARA rule \"ps_golden_ticket_attack\" is designed to detect the presence of a Golden Ticket attack, a technique used by adversaries to forge Kerberos tickets and gain unauthorized access to systems, often leveraging tools like Mimikatz. This rule identifies specific indicators such as script names, command patterns, and tool usage associated with the execution and completion of such attacks, aligning with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves command and scripting interpreter abuse.",
  "blind_ghost_powershell": "The YARA rule \"blind_ghost_powershell\" is designed to detect the presence and activity of the Blind Ghost malware, which utilizes PowerShell scripts to perform network reconnaissance, identify active hosts, execute malicious payloads, and initiate attacks.   "generic_powershell_f9e77_206": "The YARA rule \"generic_powershell_f9e77_206\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell scripts that employ the \"invoke-adsbackdoor\" function, which is indicative of adversaries leveraging PowerShell for command and control or persistence mechanisms, aligning with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001 (PowerShell).   "invoke_ninjacopy": "The YARA rule \"invoke_ninjacopy\" is designed to detect the use of the \"Invoke-NinjaCopy\" command, which is a PowerShell-based technique for copying files from a system, often used by adversaries to exfiltrate sensitive data without triggering standard file access alerts.   "invoke_ssidexfil": "The YARA rule \"invoke_ssidexfil\" is designed to detect the execution of a PowerShell script that exfiltrates SSID information from a compromised system, aligning with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the use of PowerShell for malicious activities.   "generic_powershell_1dcba_148": "The YARA rule \"generic_powershell_1dcba_148\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell scripts that employ the \"invoke-posh-packer\" command, which is indicative of obfuscation techniques used to evade detection and analysis, aligning with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001 (PowerShell).   "powershell_postexploitation_techniques": "The YARA rule \"powershell_postexploitation_techniques\" is designed to detect PowerShell scripts that are indicative of post-exploitation activities, specifically targeting the retrieval of sensitive information and privilege escalation.   "decrypt_encrypted_script": "The YARA rule \"decrypt_encrypted_script\" is designed to detect the malicious behavior of decrypting and executing encrypted PowerShell scripts, which is indicative of the technique where adversaries use encoded scripts to evade detection and execute arbitrary code on a target system.   "out_encodedcommand": "The YARA rule \"out_encodedcommand\" is designed to detect the use of the \"Out-EncodedCommand\" technique, which is associated with PowerShell command obfuscation, allowing attackers to execute encoded scripts to evade detection.   "generic_powershell_db315_293": "The YARA rule \"generic_powershell_db315_293\" is designed to detect the use of potentially malicious PowerShell scripts that employ specific command patterns, such as \"invoke-shield\" and \"invoke-tasks,\" which may indicate the execution of unauthorized or harmful activities.   "ps_obfuscation_336": "The YARA rule \"ps_obfuscation_336\" is designed to detect the use of obfuscated PowerShell commands, specifically identifying instances where the \"-split\" operator is used repeatedly to manipulate strings, a technique often employed to evade detection and analysis.   "potential_malicious_httpserver": "The YARA rule \"potential_malicious_httpserver\" is designed to detect scripts that utilize PowerShell to establish an HTTP file server, potentially indicating unauthorized data exfiltration or lateral movement within a network.   "ps_obfuscation_322": "The YARA rule \"ps_obfuscation_322\" is designed to detect obfuscated PowerShell commands, specifically targeting variations of the \"new\" keyword that are split with backticks, a common technique used to evade detection. This rule aligns with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the use of scripting for execution, highlighting the threat of script-based obfuscation in malicious activities.",
  "amsi_bypass_powershell_2251c": "The YARA rule \"amsi_bypass_powershell_2251c\" is designed to detect a malicious technique where a PowerShell script attempts to bypass the Antimalware Scan Interface (AMSI) using the egghunter method. This is achieved by loading the \"amsi.dll\" library and employing specific patterns such as \"[intptr]::loadlibrary\" and \"bypass-amsi,\" which are indicative of attempts to evade security mechanisms and execute potentially harmful code, aligning with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001 (Command and Scripting Interpreter: PowerShell).",
  "invoke_shellcode_detection_ac13f": "The YARA rule \"invoke_shellcode_detection_ac13f\" is designed to detect the execution of the \"invoke-shellcode\" function from the PowerSploit framework, which is commonly used to inject shellcode into a running process, facilitating unauthorized code execution and potential remote access via techniques such as Meterpreter payloads.   "suspicious_taskcreationevent": "The YARA rule \"suspicious_taskcreationevent\" is designed to detect potentially malicious behavior involving the creation of scheduled tasks via PowerShell scripts, specifically targeting events where the \"Get-WinEvent\" cmdlet is used to filter for security logs with Event ID 4698, which indicates a new task creation.   "amsi_patch_detection": "The YARA rule \"amsi_patch_detection\" is designed to identify attempts to bypass or patch the Antimalware Scan Interface (AMSI) in both 32-bit and 64-bit processes, a technique commonly associated with script-based malware execution and evasion, as indicated by MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001 (PowerShell).   "myvalidator_script_detection": "The YARA rule \"myvalidator_script_detection\" is designed to identify the execution of PowerShell scripts associated with the \"myvalidator\" framework, which may be used for automated testing or validation processes.   "powershell_type_verification": "The YARA rule \"powershell_type_verification\" is designed to detect PowerShell scripts that perform type conformity checks and throw exceptions when there is a mismatch, which is indicative of adversaries using PowerShell for scripting and automation to ensure data integrity or manipulate data types as part of their execution flow, aligning with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001 (PowerShell).   "vba_macro_generator_detection": "The YARA rule \"vba_macro_generator_detection\" is designed to identify potentially malicious VBA macro generators that utilize techniques such as downloading payloads from URLs, executing raw payloads via shell commands, and leveraging server-side HTTP requests, which are indicative of the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001 (Command and Scripting Interpreter: PowerShell).   "powerlurk_eventsubscription_detection": "The YARA rule \"powerlurk_eventsubscription_detection\" is designed to identify malicious PowerShell scripts that exploit Windows Event Subscription mechanisms to execute unauthorized actions, aligning with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which pertains to Command and Scripting Interpreter: PowerShell.   "ps_cmd_dotnet_startup_hooks": "The YARA rule \"ps_cmd_dotnet_startup_hooks\" is designed to detect the presence of potentially malicious .NET startup hooks, specifically identifying the use of \"80410d_preloader.dll\" and the execution of the \"dotnet -h\" command, which may indicate an attempt to execute unauthorized or malicious .NET assemblies.   "generic_powershell_cbc1d_417": "The YARA rule \"generic_powershell_cbc1d_417\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell scripts that invoke the \"invoke-jsratrundll\" command, which is indicative of the execution of malicious scripts or payloads via PowerShell, a technique associated with command and scripting interpreter abuse as categorized under MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001.   "noisy_cricket_persistence_cleaner": "The YARA rule \"noisy_cricket_persistence_cleaner\" is designed to detect scripts or activities associated with the removal of malware persistence mechanisms, specifically targeting PowerShell scripts that may attempt to clean or disable persistence methods related to boot or event triggers, as indicated by MITRE technique tags T1059.001 (PowerShell) and references to persistence techniques T1547.001 (Boot or Logon Autostart Execution) and T1546.007 (Event Triggered Execution).   "invoke_deaduserbackdoor": "The YARA rule \"invoke_deaduserbackdoor\" is designed to detect the presence of a specific backdoor malware that utilizes PowerShell scripting, as indicated by the MITRE technique tag T1059.001, which pertains to Command and Scripting Interpreter: PowerShell.   "malicious_powershell_tcpclient_9f6bc": "The YARA rule \"malicious_powershell_tcpclient_9f6bc\" is designed to detect scripts that utilize PowerShell to establish network connections using the TcpClient class, potentially indicating malicious activity such as unauthorized data transmission or remote command execution.   "ps_cobaltstrike_269": "The YARA rule \"ps_cobaltstrike_269\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell command execution associated with Cobalt Strike, specifically identifying the invocation of the \"iex $doit\" pattern, which is indicative of script-based execution techniques.   "ps_obfuscation_334": "The YARA rule \"ps_obfuscation_334\" is designed to detect obfuscated PowerShell scripts that utilize repeated patterns of the string \"rahc[+]\" followed by a two or three-digit number, indicating the use of string manipulation techniques to evade detection.   "malicious_diskcleanup_task": "The YARA rule \"malicious_diskcleanup_task\" is designed to detect the execution of a potentially malicious scheduled task that leverages the \"SilentCleanup\" feature in Windows, often associated with the abuse of system environment variables and task scheduling to execute unauthorized scripts or commands.   "powershell_malicious_command": "The YARA rule \"powershell_malicious_command\" is designed to detect PowerShell scripts exhibiting command-and-control (C2) behavior, specifically by identifying patterns indicative of network communication with a remote server, such as HTTP requests and TCP client connections, potentially used for data exfiltration or remote command execution.   "generic_powershell_4b7d9_473": "The YARA rule \"generic_powershell_4b7d9_473\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell scripts that employ the \"invoke-wcmdump\" command, which is indicative of the execution of potentially malicious scripts for command and control or data exfiltration purposes.   "suspicious_smtp_activity": "The YARA rule \"suspicious_smtp_activity\" is designed to detect potential malicious activity involving the use of PowerShell to send emails via SMTP, specifically targeting the use of Yahoo's SMTP server, with indicators of insecure credential handling and email transmission, aligning with the MITRE technique T1059.001 (PowerShell).   "potentially_malicious_powershell_script": "The YARA rule \"potentially_malicious_powershell_script\" is designed to detect PowerShell scripts that manipulate audit policies, potentially indicating attempts to evade security monitoring or alter system logging configurations.   "generic_powershell_8996e_376": "The YARA rule \"generic_powershell_8996e_376\" is designed to detect the use of the \"Invoke-Nightmare\" script, which is associated with exploiting vulnerabilities in Windows Print Spooler service to achieve privilege escalation, aligning with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001 for PowerShell.   "powershell_service_permission_checker": "The YARA rule \"powershell_service_permission_checker\" is designed to detect a potentially malicious PowerShell script that checks service permissions, characterized by specific strings indicating assembly loading, output writing, service completion, and base64 decoding.   "generic_powershell_9eae5_372": "The YARA rule \"generic_powershell_9eae5_372\" is designed to detect the use of obfuscated or potentially malicious PowerShell scripts, specifically targeting the execution of binary code through the invocation of suspicious functions, as indicated by the detection string \"invoke-s0m3b1n4ry.\" This rule aligns with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which pertains to the abuse of PowerShell for execution, often used by threat actors to facilitate unauthorized access or control over systems.",
  "malicious_powershell_gethttpstatus": "The YARA rule \"malicious_powershell_gethttpstatus\" is designed to detect potentially malicious PowerShell scripts that utilize network-related operations, such as establishing TCP connections and evaluating server certificates, which are indicative of suspicious activity related to command and control communication or data exfiltration.   "ps_microburst_azappservicescmd": "The YARA rule \"ps_microburst_azappservicescmd\" is designed to detect the use of the PowerShell command \"invoke-azappservicescmd,\" which is indicative of adversaries leveraging PowerShell scripting to interact with Azure App Services, potentially for unauthorized access or manipulation. This activity aligns with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the use of PowerShell for execution, highlighting a threat related to script-based execution in cloud environments.",
  "generic_powershell_0c200_479": "The YARA rule \"generic_powershell_0c200_479\" is designed to detect the use of potentially malicious PowerShell scripts, specifically those invoking suspicious functions or commands, as indicated by the presence of the string \"invoke-wsuspicious.\" This rule targets the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the abuse of PowerShell for execution of unauthorized or harmful activities within a system.",
  "ps_injection_268": "The YARA rule \"ps_injection_268\" is designed to detect process injection techniques, specifically targeting the use of the \"resumethread\" function, which is indicative of adversaries attempting to execute malicious code within the address space of another process.   "powershell_webclienthelpers_malicious": "The YARA rule \"powershell_webclienthelpers_malicious\" is designed to detect potentially malicious PowerShell scripts that utilize web client helper functions to execute unauthorized or harmful operations, as indicated by specific strings related to HTTP bypass, function loading, and reflection techniques. This rule targets the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the use of scripting for execution, highlighting the script's capability to manipulate web requests and execute reflective code loading, often used in fileless malware attacks.",
  "wdigest_credential_dump": "The YARA rule \"wdigest_credential_dump\" is designed to detect malicious activity associated with the dumping of credentials from memory using the WDigest authentication protocol, a technique often employed by attackers to extract plaintext passwords from a compromised system.
