  "reversetcp_shell_detection": "The YARA rule \"reversetcp_shell_detection\" is designed to identify the presence of reverse TCP shell scripts, which are indicative of remote code execution attempts where an attacker establishes a connection back to their system to execute commands on a compromised host.   "invoke_createremotethread": "The YARA rule \"invoke_createremotethread\" is designed to detect the use of the \"CreateRemoteThread\" technique, which is commonly employed by adversaries to execute code within the address space of another process, facilitating process injection and potentially leading to privilege escalation or evasion of security controls.   "malicious_nishang_payload": "The YARA rule \"malicious_nishang_payload\" is designed to detect PowerShell scripts associated with the Nishang framework that utilize techniques for downloading and executing payloads, specifically identifying patterns such as the creation of COM objects for Internet Explorer, writing bytes to disk, and executing potentially malicious files like \"svcmondr.exe.\" This rule targets the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the use of PowerShell for execution, highlighting its role in facilitating unauthorized remote code execution and potential system compromise.",
  "suspicious_find_strings_script": "The YARA rule \"suspicious_find_strings_script\" is designed to detect a PowerShell script that exhibits behavior indicative of searching for and potentially exfiltrating sensitive information, such as passwords and Wi-Fi credentials, from a system. This rule targets the technique of Command and Scripting Interpreter: PowerShell (MITRE ATT&CK T1059.001), which is often employed by threat actors to execute scripts for unauthorized data access and collection.",
  "ps_caldera_ttp_16": "The YARA rule \"ps_caldera_ttp_16\" is designed to detect attempts to disable or stop the Windows Defender service, which is indicative of adversaries employing the PowerShell technique to impair system defenses as part of the MITRE ATT&CK tactic T1059.001 (PowerShell). This behavior is commonly associated with efforts to weaken endpoint protection and facilitate further malicious activities on a compromised system.",
  "ps_art_ttp_89": "The YARA rule \"ps_art_ttp_89\" is designed to detect the presence of \"webbrowserpassview.exe,\" a tool commonly used for credential theft by extracting saved passwords from web browsers. This detection aligns with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the use of command and scripting interpreters for malicious purposes.",
  "sharpsecdump_detection": "The YARA rule \"sharpsecdump_detection\" is designed to identify potentially malicious PowerShell activity associated with the SharpSecDump tool, which is used for credential dumping by leveraging memory manipulation and data compression techniques. It targets specific strings indicative of SharpSecDump's operations, such as memory object creation, Base64 conversion, gzip compression, and registry querying, aligning with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001 for PowerShell-based execution.",
  "persistence_mechanisms_cleaning": "The YARA rule \"persistence_mechanisms_cleaning\" is designed to detect suspicious activity in PowerShell scripts that attempt to remove or clean persistence mechanisms, such as scheduled tasks, PowerShell profiles, event viewer logs, netsh configurations, and WMI entries, which may indicate efforts to evade detection or forensic analysis. This rule targets the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, highlighting potential post-compromise actions aimed at erasing traces of persistence to hinder incident response and recovery efforts.",
  "malicious_powershell_beacon": "The YARA rule \"malicious_powershell_beacon\" is designed to detect the execution of potentially malicious PowerShell scripts that employ techniques such as encryption, decryption, and data exfiltration, indicative of command and control (C2) activities. Specifically, it targets scripts that utilize functions like \"encrypt-compressedstring\" and \"send-response,\" aligning with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the use of PowerShell for executing malicious commands and scripts.",
  "malicious_powershell_export_script": "The YARA rule \"malicious_powershell_export_script\" is designed to detect potentially malicious PowerShell scripts that are involved in unauthorized or suspicious activities related to exporting Exchange mailboxes, which may indicate attempts to exfiltrate sensitive email data.   "ps_art_ttp_88": "The YARA rule \"ps_art_ttp_88\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell for remote management and execution, specifically identifying the creation and invocation of CIM (Common Information Model) sessions, which aligns with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001 (PowerShell).   "simple_reverse_shell_powershell_babd6": "The YARA rule \"simple_reverse_shell_powershell_babd6\" is designed to detect a PowerShell-based reverse shell script that establishes a TCP connection to a remote IP address, allowing an attacker to execute arbitrary commands on the compromised system.   "asamsi_maliciousscript": "The YARA rule \"asamsi_maliciousscript\" is designed to detect suspicious PowerShell activity indicative of malicious tasks, specifically targeting techniques associated with process injection and evasion, as evidenced by strings related to patching MSI and ETW functions.   "ps_caldera_ttp_17": "The YARA rule \"ps_caldera_ttp_17\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell scripts that emulate administrative actions, which aligns with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, indicating potential abuse of PowerShell for execution of malicious commands or scripts. This rule targets the identification of threat actors leveraging PowerShell to gain unauthorized administrative control or execute harmful operations within a system.",
  "powershell_amsi_patch_3d149": "The YARA rule \"powershell_amsi_patch_3d149\" is designed to detect attempts to bypass or patch the Antimalware Scan Interface (AMSI) in PowerShell, which is a technique often used by adversaries to execute malicious scripts without being detected by security software.   "nishang_addregbackdoor_1801e": "The YARA rule \"nishang_addregbackdoor_1801e\" is designed to detect the use of Nishang, a PowerShell-based penetration testing framework, specifically identifying attempts to create a registry-based backdoor by modifying execution options and debugger settings to hijack legitimate processes.   "mockfunction_detection": "The YARA rule \"mockfunction_detection\" is designed to identify the manipulation of global functions within PowerShell scripts, specifically targeting techniques that involve altering or removing functions such as \"test-path\" and \"remove-item,\" which can be indicative of adversaries attempting to evade detection or disrupt system operations.   "powershell_schedule_task_manipulation": "The YARA rule \"powershell_schedule_task_manipulation\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell scripts to manipulate scheduled tasks, which is indicative of adversaries attempting to establish persistence or execute malicious activities by creating, deleting, or modifying scheduled tasks.   "ps_potential_credential_dump_1032": "The YARA rule \"ps_potential_credential_dump_1032\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell scripts that attempt to enumerate security packages, a technique commonly associated with credential dumping activities.   "phishing_reports_processor": "The YARA rule \"phishing_reports_processor\" is designed to detect the execution of a PowerShell script that processes data from phishing reports, potentially indicating the use of PowerShell for malicious purposes such as data exfiltration or manipulation.   "malicious_powershell_calcexecution": "The YARA rule \"malicious_powershell_calcexecution\" is designed to detect the execution of a potentially malicious PowerShell script that launches the calculator application (calc.exe) under administrative privileges, which may indicate an attempt to exploit system vulnerabilities or execute unauthorized commands.   "ps_uninstall_sensor": "The YARA rule \"ps_uninstall_sensor\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell commands that attempt to forcibly uninstall Cybereason security software components, specifically the \"activeprobe\" and \"sensor,\" indicating potential adversary activity aimed at disabling endpoint protection mechanisms.   "ps_art_ttp_63": "The YARA rule \"ps_art_ttp_63\" is designed to detect malicious activity associated with the execution of PowerShell scripts, specifically targeting the technique identified by MITRE ATT&CK as T1059.001, which involves the use of PowerShell for execution.   "ps_remote_cert_trust_general": "The YARA rule \"ps_remote_cert_trust_general\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell scripts associated with the Atomic Red Team framework, specifically targeting the presence of certificate files like \"atomic.pfx,\" which may indicate attempts to bypass security controls or establish unauthorized trust relationships.   "ps_art_ttp_77": "The YARA rule \"ps_art_ttp_77\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell commands that modify Windows Defender preferences by adding file extensions, such as \".exe\", to the exclusion list, potentially allowing malicious executables to bypass security scans.   "powershell_pester_test_script": "The YARA rule \"powershell_pester_test_script\" is designed to detect the presence of potentially malicious PowerShell scripts that utilize Pester, a testing framework, to execute or simulate tests with suspicious elements such as non-existent modules and mock calls, indicative of script-based obfuscation or evasion techniques.   "malicious_rdp_enabler": "The YARA rule \"malicious_rdp_enabler\" is designed to detect potentially malicious activity involving the use of PowerShell scripts to enable Remote Desktop Protocol (RDP) connections on Windows systems, which may indicate unauthorized access attempts or lateral movement within a network.   "ps_art_ttp_1": "The YARA rule \"ps_art_ttp_1\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell commands that attempt to manipulate IIS web server modules, specifically targeting the creation or modification of the \"defaultdocumentmodule_atomic\" module, which aligns with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, indicating potential exploitation of PowerShell for malicious configuration changes.   "malicious_tcp_client_activity": "The YARA rule \"malicious_tcp_client_activity\" is designed to detect PowerShell-based network activity indicative of malicious behavior, specifically targeting the use of TCP client connections to execute commands and potentially exfiltrate data, as characterized by MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001 (PowerShell).   "reverse_bind_shell_detection": "The YARA rule \"reverse_bind_shell_detection\" is designed to identify scripts associated with Nishang, a post-exploitation framework, that facilitate reverse or bind shell connections using PowerShell, which is indicative of adversaries leveraging PowerShell for remote command execution and control.   "malicious_powershell_rat_script": "The YARA rule \"malicious_powershell_rat_script\" is designed to detect the execution of a potentially malicious PowerShell Remote Access Trojan (RAT) script, which utilizes techniques such as invoking expressions, creating web client objects, and manipulating SSL certificates to facilitate unauthorized remote control and data exfiltration.   "generic_powershell_fdb70_267": "The YARA rule \"generic_powershell_fdb70_267\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell scripts that employ the \"invoke-posh-csharppacker\" function, which is indicative of a technique used to execute or pack malicious payloads using PowerShell, aligning with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001 (PowerShell).   "ps_windows_extract_memory_via_vba": "The YARA rule \"ps_windows_extract_memory_via_vba\" is designed to detect malicious scripts or activities that utilize PowerShell commands to extract sensitive data from memory or binary files, specifically targeting Active Directory user information without requiring pre-authentication.   "generic_powershell_c8f49_22": "The YARA rule \"generic_powershell_c8f49_22\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell scripts associated with password spraying attacks, specifically targeting domain accounts, as indicated by the presence of strings such as \"invoke-domainpasswordspray\" and \"invoke-spraysinglepassword.\" This rule aligns with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the use of PowerShell for execution, highlighting its role in credential access and lateral movement within a network.",
  "hivenightmare_malicious_activity": "The YARA rule \"hivenightmare_malicious_activity\" is designed to detect the execution of proof-of-concept scripts associated with the HiveNightmare vulnerability, which exploits shadow copies to access sensitive registry hives on vulnerable Windows systems. This rule identifies malicious activity related to the abuse of the Windows Management Instrumentation (WMI) command-line interface, as indicated by MITRE technique T1059.001, to dump SAM and other registry hives, potentially leading to unauthorized access and privilege escalation.",
  "powershell_hook_scripts": "The YARA rule \"powershell_hook_scripts\" is designed to detect scripts that utilize PowerShell for malicious purposes, specifically targeting the use of script hooks and parameters indicative of code injection or execution techniques.   "malicious_powershell_enumeration": "The YARA rule \"malicious_powershell_enumeration\" is designed to detect PowerShell scripts that enumerate privileged groups within a domain, potentially indicating reconnaissance activity by an adversary seeking to identify high-value targets for privilege escalation.   "generic_powershell_cfd47_214": "The YARA rule \"generic_powershell_cfd47_214\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell scripts that invoke the \"invoke-allchecks\" function, which is commonly associated with post-exploitation frameworks used for privilege escalation and system reconnaissance.   "potential_malicious_powershell_script_34da8": "The YARA rule \"potential_malicious_powershell_script_34da8\" is designed to detect potentially malicious PowerShell scripts that exhibit behaviors associated with the execution of unauthorized or suspicious operations, such as querying system directories, suppressing error messages, and potentially interacting with system components in a stealthy manner.   "timedscreenshot_activity": "The YARA rule \"timedscreenshot_activity\" is designed to detect the execution of a PowerShell script that captures screenshots at timed intervals, potentially indicating unauthorized surveillance or data exfiltration activities.   "unconstrained_domain_persistence_helper": "The YARA rule \"unconstrained_domain_persistence_helper\" is designed to detect scripting activities associated with unconstrained delegation in Active Directory environments, specifically targeting the manipulation of domain user permissions and resource-based constrained delegation settings.   "ps_windows_encoded_vbs_code_execution": "The YARA rule \"ps_windows_encoded_vbs_code_execution\" is designed to detect the execution of encoded Visual Basic Script (VBS) from within a malicious document, leveraging PowerShell commands such as \"invoke-maldoc\" and \"exec\" to facilitate code execution.   "reverseshell_powershell_script": "The YARA rule \"reverseshell_powershell_script\" is designed to detect scripts that establish a reverse shell connection using PowerShell, characterized by the use of .NET networking classes such as \"system.net.sockets\" and \"tcpclient($ip,$port)\" to connect to a remote server, potentially allowing unauthorized remote access and control over the compromised system.   "malicious_getcimscheduledtask_script_detection": "The YARA rule \"malicious_getcimscheduledtask_script_detection\" is designed to identify the execution of PowerShell scripts that maliciously utilize the Get-CimScheduledTask function, potentially indicating attempts to enumerate or manipulate scheduled tasks for unauthorized access or persistence.   "ps_system_scope_cor_profiler": "The YARA rule \"ps_system_scope_cor_profiler\" is designed to detect modifications to the Windows Registry, specifically targeting the \"HKLM:\\System\\CurrentControlSet\\Control\\Session Manager\\Environment\" path, which may indicate the presence of a .NET profiler being set via environment variables, a technique associated with process injection or execution hijacking as described in MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001.   "powershell_amsi_bypass_825c7": "The YARA rule \"powershell_amsi_bypass_825c7\" is designed to detect attempts to bypass the Antimalware Scan Interface (AMSI) using a PowerShell script, which is a technique often employed by adversaries to execute malicious code without being detected by security software.   "basic_pe_parser": "The YARA rule \"basic_pe_parser\" is designed to detect the presence of a PowerShell script that utilizes PsReflect to parse Portable Executable (PE) files, potentially indicating the use of a script by Matthew Graeber that leverages reflective DLL injection techniques.   "malicious_invoke_shellcode": "The YARA rule \"malicious_invoke_shellcode\" is designed to detect the use of the Invoke-Shellcode function from the PowerSploit framework, which is commonly employed by attackers to execute shellcode within the memory space of a target process, facilitating unauthorized code execution and potential privilege escalation.   "ms16032_powershell_exploit_6d921": "The YARA rule \"ms16032_powershell_exploit_6d921\" is designed to detect the presence of a PowerShell script exploiting the MS16-032 vulnerability, which targets Windows systems to elevate privileges by manipulating thread tokens and duplicating handles.   "ps_malicious_execution_326": "The YARA rule \"ps_malicious_execution_326\" is designed to detect the execution of potentially malicious PowerShell scripts, specifically targeting scripts that contain the string \"edr-testing-script,\" which may indicate attempts to test or bypass endpoint detection and response (EDR) systems.   "invoke_dpslight": "The YARA rule \"invoke_dpslight\" is designed to detect the use of the \"invoke-dpslight\" command, which is associated with PowerShell scripting for execution, a technique categorized under MITRE ATT&CK's T1059.001.   "suspicious_ps_registry_manipulation": "The YARA rule \"suspicious_ps_registry_manipulation\" is designed to detect PowerShell scripts that exhibit potentially malicious behavior by manipulating the Windows registry, specifically targeting user-specific registry paths such as \"HKCU:\\Software\\Classes\" to alter command execution settings, while employing encoded commands to obfuscate their activities.   "malicious_powershell_activity_29923": "The YARA rule \"malicious_powershell_activity_29923\" is designed to detect potentially malicious PowerShell activity characterized by the retrieval and execution of commands from external sources, leveraging techniques such as web requests and encoding manipulations to bypass execution policies.   "ps_ladon_365": "The YARA rule \"ps_ladon_365\" is designed to detect the presence of the Ladon scanning tool, which is often used for network reconnaissance and lateral movement by executing PowerShell scripts.   "keepassconfig_maliciousactivity": "The YARA rule \"keepassconfig_maliciousactivity\" is designed to detect unauthorized manipulation of KeePass configuration settings, specifically targeting activities that involve adding or removing configuration triggers, exporting database information, and exfiltrating copied data.   "invoke_memoryloadlibrary": "The YARA rule \"invoke_memoryloadlibrary\" is designed to detect the use of the \"Invoke-MemoryLoadLibrary\" technique, which involves loading libraries directly into memory without writing them to disk, a method often employed by adversaries to execute code stealthily and evade traditional file-based detection mechanisms.   "invoke_whisker_detection": "The YARA rule \"invoke_whisker_detection\" is designed to identify PowerShell scripts that utilize the \"invoke-whisker\" function, which is indicative of potentially malicious activity involving script-based code execution and manipulation of .NET assemblies, often associated with reflective loading and in-memory execution techniques.   "powershell_script_sample": "The YARA rule \"powershell_script_sample\" is designed to detect potentially malicious PowerShell scripts that exhibit unusual coding patterns and behaviors, such as the use of Git commands, handling of pipeline operations, and specific code quirks like trailing whitespace and short version references.   "invoke_prasadhak": "The YARA rule \"invoke_prasadhak\" is designed to detect the presence of scripts or commands that utilize the \"invoke-prasadhak\" pattern, which is indicative of PowerShell-based obfuscation techniques commonly associated with adversaries leveraging PowerShell for execution, as referenced by MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001.   "ps_art_ttp_3": "The YARA rule \"ps_art_ttp_3\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell for script execution, specifically targeting the abuse of the InstallUtil utility, as indicated by the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001 and the presence of suspicious filenames such as \"installerassemblyfilename\" and \"t1218.004.dll.\" This rule identifies potential attempts to execute malicious code through trusted Windows utilities, a common technique used by adversaries to bypass security controls.",
  "powershell_getnetsession_suspicious": "The YARA rule \"powershell_getnetsession_suspicious\" is designed to detect potentially malicious PowerShell activity that involves the collection of network session information, which may indicate attempts at user impersonation or privilege escalation, as characterized by MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001.   "powerview_maliciousactivity": "The YARA rule \"powerview_maliciousactivity\" is designed to detect potentially malicious activities associated with PowerView, a PowerShell tool used for network reconnaissance and privilege escalation, by identifying specific command patterns such as \"invoke-userhunter\" and \"get-objectacl\" that align with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves PowerShell-based scripting for adversarial purposes.   "powershell_invocation_amsi_bypass": "The YARA rule \"powershell_invocation_amsi_bypass\" is designed to detect attempts to bypass the Antimalware Scan Interface (AMSI) in PowerShell scripts, a technique commonly used by adversaries to evade detection and execute malicious code without being intercepted by security software.   "malicious_powershell_webrequest_81848": "The YARA rule \"malicious_powershell_webrequest_81848\" is designed to detect PowerShell scripts that exhibit behavior indicative of malicious activity, specifically by making web requests using \"invoke-webrequest\" with \"usebasicparsing\" and potentially bypassing security warnings, as well as executing commands in a persistent loop.   "ps_art_ttp_49": "The YARA rule \"ps_art_ttp_49\" is designed to detect the presence of specific strings associated with the execution of PowerShell scripts, as indicated by the MITRE ATT&CK technique tag T1059.001, which pertains to Command and Scripting Interpreter: PowerShell.   "ps_microburst_azurepasswords": "The YARA rule \"ps_microburst_azurepasswords\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell scripts that attempt to retrieve Azure passwords, indicating potential credential access or unauthorized attempts to harvest sensitive information from Azure environments.   "ps_t1134_002_gettoken": "The YARA rule \"ps_t1134_002_gettoken\" is designed to detect the presence of a PowerShell script named \"gettoken.ps1\" that is associated with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1134.002, which involves the abuse of Token Manipulation for privilege escalation.   "ps_caldera_ttp_15": "The YARA rule \"ps_caldera_ttp_15\" is designed to detect PowerShell commands that modify Windows Defender settings to weaken system defenses, specifically by disabling intrusion prevention, real-time monitoring, IOAV protection, script scanning, and controlled folder access.   "amsi_unchained_detection": "The YARA rule \"amsi_unchained_detection\" is designed to identify scripts that attempt to disable or bypass the Antimalware Scan Interface (AMSI) by patching its functions, a technique commonly used by malware to evade detection during script execution.   "generic_powershell_efeed_490": "The YARA rule \"generic_powershell_efeed_490\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell scripts that employ specific functions such as \"invoke-sharploader\" and \"invoke-blocketw,\" which are indicative of malicious activity related to script-based loading and execution of potentially harmful payloads.   "unauthorized_task_creation": "The YARA rule \"unauthorized_task_creation\" is designed to detect the malicious behavior of creating or manipulating scheduled tasks as a means of privilege escalation, which is a common technique used by adversaries to gain elevated access on a system.   "ps_caldera_ttp_29": "The YARA rule \"ps_caldera_ttp_29\" is designed to detect malicious PowerShell activity associated with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the use of PowerShell for execution.   "invoke_backdoorlnk": "The YARA rule \"invoke_backdoorlnk\" is designed to detect the presence of scripts or commands that utilize the \"invoke-backdoorlnk\" pattern, indicating potential execution of a backdoor via Windows Command Shell, as classified under MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001.   "malicious_powershell_activity_f7173": "The YARA rule \"malicious_powershell_activity_f7173\" is designed to detect potentially malicious PowerShell activity by identifying common obfuscation and execution techniques, such as encoding commands in Base64, using the `Invoke-Expression` cmdlet, and executing PowerShell scripts with the `-noprofile` and `-enc` flags.   "ps_payloadloader": "The YARA rule \"ps_payloadloader\" is designed to detect malicious PowerShell scripts that utilize reflective loading techniques to execute .NET assemblies in memory, often associated with the execution of payloads without writing them to disk.   "fodhelper_uac_bypass_c7cd7": "The YARA rule \"fodhelper_uac_bypass_c7cd7\" is designed to detect scripts that attempt to bypass User Account Control (UAC) using the Fodhelper executable, a technique categorized under MITRE ATT&CK's \"Command and Scripting Interpreter: PowerShell\" (T1059.001).   "malicious_hiddenuser_account_manipulation": "The YARA rule \"malicious_hiddenuser_account_manipulation\" is designed to detect activities related to the creation, modification, or deletion of hidden user accounts, which may involve manipulating account visibility or exploiting administrator privileges to facilitate unauthorized remote desktop protocol (RDP) connections.   "extracts_wifi_credentials": "The YARA rule \"extracts_wifi_credentials\" is designed to detect malicious PowerShell script activity that attempts to extract and potentially exfiltrate Wi-Fi credentials from a system by utilizing the \"netsh wlan show profiles\" command to enumerate wireless profiles and retrieve stored passwords, which aligns with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001 (Command and Scripting Interpreter: PowerShell).   "privilege_escalation_checks": "The YARA rule \"privilege_escalation_checks\" is designed to detect attempts at privilege escalation by identifying scripts or commands that check for misconfigured file permissions and unquoted service paths, which can be exploited to gain unauthorized elevated access.   "laps_password_extraction": "The YARA rule \"laps_password_extraction\" is designed to detect malicious PowerShell scripts that attempt to extract Local Administrator Password Solution (LAPS) passwords from Active Directory, leveraging the technique of PowerShell scripting as described in MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001.   "powerhub_malicious_detection": "The YARA rule \"powerhub_malicious_detection\" is designed to identify potentially malicious activity associated with PowerHub scripts, which may involve the execution of encoded or encrypted payloads, module activation, and shellcode invocation, indicative of command and scripting interpreter abuse as per MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001.   "zerologon_exploit_detection": "The YARA rule \"zerologon_exploit_detection\" is designed to identify PowerShell scripts that attempt to exploit the Zerologon vulnerability (CVE-2020-1472), which allows an attacker to gain unauthorized access to a domain controller by manipulating the Netlogon authentication process.   "ps_caldera_ttp_14": "The YARA rule \"ps_caldera_ttp_14\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell scripts that leverage CIM (Common Information Model) classes to create and manage processes, specifically identifying attempts to execute commands via `cmd.exe` with a delay and subsequent file deletion, indicative of process manipulation and stealthy execution techniques.   "windows_sensitiveinfo_searcher": "The YARA rule \"windows_sensitiveinfo_searcher\" is designed to detect scripts used in post-exploitation scenarios that search for sensitive information within shared folders on Windows systems, leveraging techniques such as credential input and network drive manipulation.   "malicious_powershell_network_activity_05573": "The YARA rule \"malicious_powershell_network_activity_05573\" is designed to detect potentially malicious PowerShell scripts that exhibit network activity indicative of command execution, specifically targeting techniques associated with MITRE ATT&CK's T1059.001, which involves PowerShell.   "amsi_patcher": "The YARA rule \"amsi_patcher\" is designed to detect PowerShell scripts that attempt to disable or bypass the Antimalware Scan Interface (AMSI) by patching its functionality, a technique commonly used by attackers to evade detection of malicious scripts.   "ps_winrm_1127": "The YARA rule \"ps_winrm_1127\" is designed to detect the use of the \"Enter-PSSession\" command, which is indicative of remote command execution via Windows Remote Management (WinRM), a technique associated with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001.   "ps_atomictest_fixed_clsids_general": "The YARA rule \"ps_atomictest_fixed_clsids_general\" is designed to detect the presence of the \"atomictest.dll\" file, which is indicative of a potential execution of malicious scripts or payloads via PowerShell, aligning with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001 (PowerShell).   "ps_edge_history": "The YARA rule \"ps_edge_history\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell scripts that exfiltrate or manipulate Microsoft Edge browsing history, potentially indicating unauthorized data access or collection.   "ps_art_ttp_74": "The YARA rule \"ps_art_ttp_74\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell for persistence and proxy configuration, specifically identifying attempts to modify the Windows Registry to establish persistence via the \"HKCU\\Software\\Microsoft\\Windows\\CurrentVersion\\Run\" key and configure a SOCKS5 proxy using PowerShell, which aligns with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001 (PowerShell).   "poormansarmory_import": "The YARA rule \"poormansarmory_import\" is designed to detect the import activity of the \"poormansarmory\" script, which is associated with PowerShell-based reverse engineering and exploitation techniques, specifically targeting the execution of scripts that may facilitate unauthorized access or control over systems.   "ps_art_ttp_60": "The YARA rule \"ps_art_ttp_60\" is designed to detect PowerShell-based command execution indicative of potential Kerberoasting attacks, as identified by the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001.   "invoke_inj": "The YARA rule \"invoke_inj\" is designed to detect the use of the \"invoke-inj\" string, which is indicative of PowerShell command-line activity associated with the technique T1059.001, Command and Scripting Interpreter: PowerShell.   "templateinjection_detect": "The YARA rule \"templateinjection_detect\" is designed to identify malicious activity involving template injection in Word documents, where an attacker modifies or embeds a template URL within a document to execute unauthorized code or deliver payloads.   "ps_art_ttp_2": "The YARA rule \"ps_art_ttp_2\" is designed to detect the presence of a malicious DLL, \"atomicredteampwfilter.dll,\" which is associated with the modification of the Windows Registry path \"hklm:\\\\system\\\\currentcontrolset\\\\control\\\\lsa,\" indicating potential credential theft or manipulation through the use of a password filter DLL.   "powershell_process_hollowing": "The YARA rule \"powershell_process_hollowing\" is designed to detect PowerShell scripts that exhibit characteristics indicative of process hollowing, a technique where a legitimate process is manipulated to execute malicious code by replacing its memory space.   "potential_puttykeys_theft": "The YARA rule \"potential_puttykeys_theft\" is designed to detect scripts that attempt to exfiltrate sensitive information from PuTTY, specifically targeting session keys, passwords, and configuration files.   "wificredentials_extractor": "The YARA rule \"wificredentials_extractor\" is designed to detect the presence of a tool or script that extracts Wi-Fi credentials by identifying patterns associated with the use of the \"netsh wlan export\" command to export wireless profiles, and subsequent parsing of XML files to retrieve sensitive information such as SSID and password.   "kerberoast_userspn_detection": "The YARA rule \"kerberoast_userspn_detection\" is designed to identify scripts or activities related to Kerberoasting, a technique used by attackers to extract and crack service account credentials from Active Directory by enumerating Service Principal Names (SPNs).   "sherlock_powersploit_detection": "The YARA rule \"sherlock_powersploit_detection\" is designed to identify the use of the Sherlock PowerShell script, which is employed to detect and potentially exploit vulnerabilities in Windows systems, specifically targeting local privilege escalation vulnerabilities.   "powershell_screenshot_capture": "The YARA rule \"powershell_screenshot_capture\" is designed to detect a PowerShell script that captures screen activity by identifying specific strings related to image processing and system information retrieval, such as \"system.drawing.bitmap\" and \"tobase64string.\" This rule targets the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the use of PowerShell for malicious purposes, specifically for unauthorized screen capture and potential data exfiltration.",
  "malicious_browser_credential_dump": "The YARA rule \"malicious_browser_credential_dump\" is designed to detect scripts that exhibit behavior indicative of credential theft from web browsers, specifically targeting the technique of credential dumping as outlined in MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001.   "dcshadow_permissions_modification": "The YARA rule \"dcshadow_permissions_modification\" is designed to detect the use of the DCShadow technique, which involves modifying Active Directory objects to grant permissions that facilitate unauthorized replication and control over domain controllers.   "malicious_powershell_detector": "The \"malicious_powershell_detector\" YARA rule is designed to identify potentially malicious PowerShell scripts that exhibit behaviors associated with system manipulation, privilege escalation, and network reconnaissance, as indicated by strings related to process initiation, encryption, system configuration changes, and network status checks.   "pesterscript_detection": "The YARA rule \"pesterscript_detection\" is designed to identify scripts that potentially misuse Pester functions, a testing framework for PowerShell, to perform unauthorized assertions and manipulations, which may indicate attempts to execute malicious code or conduct privilege escalation.   "hellokitty_registry_injection": "The \"hellokitty_registry_injection\" YARA rule is designed to detect malicious script execution associated with the \"HelloKitty\" threat actor, specifically targeting registry manipulation to achieve User Account Control (UAC) bypass by exploiting the \"fodhelper.exe\" process and modifying registry keys under \"HKCU:\\software\\classes\" and \"ms-settings\\shell\" with \"delegateexecute\" commands.   "ps_art_ttp_101": "The YARA rule \"ps_art_ttp_101\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell scripts that employ XOR encryption with a static key for obfuscating data, specifically targeting scripts that make web requests, which aligns with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001 (PowerShell).   "invoke_loopbackdoor": "The YARA rule \"invoke_loopbackdoor\" is designed to detect the presence of a script or command that utilizes the \"invoke-loopbackdoor\" string, indicating the execution of a PowerShell-based backdoor. This detection aligns with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the use of PowerShell for malicious command and script execution, potentially allowing unauthorized remote access or control over a compromised system.",
  "ssh_service_setup": "The YARA rule \"ssh_service_setup\" is designed to detect the execution of a PowerShell script that installs and configures the OpenSSH service on a Windows system, potentially indicating unauthorized or malicious activity related to remote access setup. This rule targets the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the use of PowerShell for scripting and automation, often leveraged by threat actors to establish persistence or facilitate lateral movement within a network.",
  "ps_credential_dump_361": "The YARA rule \"ps_credential_dump_361\" is designed to detect the use of the `MiniDumpWriteDump` method, which is commonly employed in credential dumping attacks to extract sensitive information from process memory. This rule targets the technique associated with MITRE ATT&CK ID T1059.001, indicating the use of scripting for malicious purposes, specifically in the context of credential theft.",
  "ps_remote_cert_trust": "The YARA rule \"ps_remote_cert_trust\" is designed to detect the execution of a PowerShell script that retrieves and runs a remote script from a GitHub repository, which is associated with the technique of exploiting certificate trust settings to import a potentially malicious PFX certificate using certutil.exe, indicative of credential manipulation or trust exploitation as per MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001. This rule specifically targets the behavior of downloading and executing scripts that alter certificate trust settings, potentially facilitating unauthorized access or persistence within a system.",
  "lsa_secrets_extraction": "The YARA rule \"lsa_secrets_extraction\" is designed to detect PowerShell scripts that attempt to extract Local Security Authority (LSA) secrets from the Windows registry, a technique associated with credential theft and privilege escalation, as indicated by MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001. The rule identifies specific strings related to registry access and elevated permissions, which are indicative of potentially malicious activities aimed at accessing sensitive security policy data.",
  "ps_art_ttp_79": "The YARA rule \"ps_art_ttp_79\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell commands that modify Windows Defender settings by adding process exclusions, specifically targeting the exclusion of \"outlook.exe\" from antivirus scanning. This behavior aligns with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the use of PowerShell for execution, potentially indicating an attempt to evade security measures and facilitate malicious activities.",
  "apphostconfig_passwordrecovery": "The YARA rule \"apphostconfig_passwordrecovery\" is designed to detect the use of a PowerShell script that leverages the \"get-applicationhost\" command to recover passwords associated with application pools and virtual directories in IIS configurations, potentially indicating unauthorized access or credential theft. This activity aligns with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the use of PowerShell for malicious purposes, such as credential access and system exploitation.",
  "patch_amsi_detection": "The \"patch_amsi_detection\" YARA rule is designed to identify attempts to bypass the Antimalware Scan Interface (AMSI) by detecting known patching techniques that modify or disable AMSI's functionality, potentially allowing malicious scripts to execute without being scanned. This rule targets behaviors associated with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the use of scripting to evade security controls and execute unauthorized code.",
  "potential_remote_smb_share_access": "The YARA rule \"potential_remote_smb_share_access\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell scripts that attempt to access SMB shares on remote computers, potentially indicating lateral movement or unauthorized access within a network. This rule targets the technique of using CIM (Common Information Model) cmdlets, such as \"get-cimsmbshare\" and \"invoke-cimmethod,\" which are associated with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, highlighting the use of scripting for remote system interaction and resource enumeration.",
  "remove_volumeshadowcopy": "The YARA rule \"remove_volumeshadowcopy\" is designed to detect the execution of commands or scripts that attempt to delete or disable Volume Shadow Copies, a technique often used by ransomware to prevent data recovery. This behavior is associated with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the use of command and scripting interpreters for malicious purposes.",
  "ps_art_ttp_45": "The YARA rule \"ps_art_ttp_45\" is designed to detect the presence of PowerShell scripts associated with the technique \"PowerShell\" under MITRE ATT&CK ID T1059.001, specifically targeting scripts that exhibit characteristics of process hollowing, a technique used by adversaries to execute malicious code within the address space of a legitimate process. The detection string \"start-hollow.ps1\" suggests the rule identifies scripts that initiate or facilitate this form of code injection, indicating potential attempts to evade security controls and execute unauthorized actions.",
  "malicious_powershell_script_1dd38": "The YARA rule \"malicious_powershell_script_1dd38\" is designed to detect PowerShell scripts exhibiting characteristics of reflective DLL injection, indicated by the presence of strings related to assembly manipulation and field access, which aligns with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001 (PowerShell). This rule targets scripts that potentially execute malicious code by dynamically loading and interacting with assemblies in a non-public, static manner, suggesting advanced evasion and execution tactics.",
  "gopher_powershell_script": "The YARA rule \"gopher_powershell_script\" is designed to detect a suspicious PowerShell script that executes embedded assembly code, potentially indicating malicious activity such as code injection or execution of unauthorized binaries. The rule targets specific strings associated with the Gopher protocol and PowerShell commands, aligning with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the use of scripting for adversarial purposes.",
  "ps_art_ttp_51": "The YARA rule \"ps_art_ttp_51\" is designed to detect the execution of the regsvr32.exe utility, commonly used for proxy execution of malicious scripts or DLLs, specifically targeting the \"allthethingsx86.dll\" on AMD64 architecture, aligning with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves command and scripting interpreter abuse. This rule identifies potential exploitation of regsvr32 for executing unauthorized or harmful code, indicative of script-based threats or lateral movement within a compromised system.",
  "malicious_powershell_script_detection_027d7": "The YARA rule \"malicious_powershell_script_detection_027d7\" is designed to identify the execution of potentially malicious PowerShell scripts that utilize techniques such as encoded command execution, bypassing execution policies, and network communication via HTTP, indicative of PowerShell-based remote access trojans (RATs) or downloaders. Specifically, it targets scripts that employ the \"new-object net.webclient\" method for network operations, aligning with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves PowerShell for command and script execution.",
  "chrome_credential_extraction_detection": "The YARA rule \"chrome_credential_extraction_detection\" is designed to identify PowerShell scripts that attempt to extract credentials from Google Chrome's \"Login Data\" database, potentially indicating credential theft or unauthorized access. This detection aligns with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the use of PowerShell for malicious purposes, specifically targeting the extraction and misuse of stored browser credentials.",
  "ps_caldera_ttp_19": "The YARA rule \"ps_caldera_ttp_19\" is designed to detect scripts or binaries that utilize FTP operations for data exfiltration or remote file manipulation, specifically targeting behaviors associated with the use of FTP methods, credentials, and passive/binary modes, as well as the handling of data streams. This rule aligns with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the use of PowerShell for command and script execution, indicating potential malicious activity related to unauthorized data transfer or remote access.",
  "generic_powershell_c27f6_128": "The YARA rule \"generic_powershell_c27f6_128\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell scripts that perform network port scanning activities, specifically identifying the invocation of the \"invoke-portscan\" command. This behavior is associated with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the use of PowerShell for malicious purposes, such as reconnaissance and lateral movement within a network.",
  "ps_art_ttp_86": "The YARA rule \"ps_art_ttp_86\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell scripts that attempt to gather user environment information, specifically targeting the retrieval of the current username and storing it in a file named \"currentactiveuser.txt,\" which aligns with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, indicating potential script-based execution for reconnaissance or credential access. This rule identifies suspicious activity that may be indicative of an attacker leveraging PowerShell to enumerate user details within a compromised environment.",
  "invoke_reflectivepeinjection": "The YARA rule \"invoke_reflectivepeinjection\" is designed to detect the use of reflective PE injection, a technique where a Portable Executable (PE) is loaded into a process's memory without being written to disk, often used by attackers to execute malicious code stealthily. This technique is associated with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves command and scripting interpreter abuse for executing payloads in memory.",
  "ps_caldera_ttp_31": "The YARA rule \"ps_caldera_ttp_31\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell scripts that interact with the meme-api.herokuapp.com service and manipulate user environment variables to set a wallpaper path, indicative of potential script-based command and control activity or system manipulation. This rule targets the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves PowerShell-based execution, commonly used for malicious scripting and automation in cyber threats.",
  "invoke_bruteforce": "The YARA rule \"invoke_bruteforce\" is designed to detect scripts or binaries that utilize the \"invoke-bruteforce\" command, indicative of a brute force attack technique targeting authentication mechanisms, as categorized under MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001 (Command and Scripting Interpreter: PowerShell). This rule specifically identifies attempts to gain unauthorized access by systematically trying multiple passwords or passphrases.",
  "reverse_http_shell_detection": "The YARA rule \"reverse_http_shell_detection\" is designed to identify malicious PowerShell scripts that establish a reverse HTTP shell, a technique where an attacker gains remote control over a compromised system by executing commands via HTTP requests. This rule specifically targets scripts that utilize encoded PowerShell commands and REST methods to receive and execute commands, aligning with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which pertains to the use of PowerShell for command and scripting interpreter purposes.",
  "ps_caldera_ttp_25": "The YARA rule \"ps_caldera_ttp_25\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell scripts that enumerate files with specific extensions related to cryptographic keys and certificates (e.g., .key, .pgp, .pem) across the file system, which is indicative of the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, PowerShell, often used by threat actors for credential access or data exfiltration. This rule targets the behavior of recursively searching for sensitive files, potentially for unauthorized access or exploitation.",
  "ps_art_ttp_92": "The YARA rule \"ps_art_ttp_92\" is designed to detect the use of \"invoke-rubeus,\" a command associated with the execution of PowerShell scripts for credential theft and Kerberos ticket manipulation, aligning with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001 (PowerShell). This rule targets the identification of potential lateral movement or privilege escalation activities within a network environment.",
  "ps_art_ttp_93": "The YARA rule \"ps_art_ttp_93\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell command-line interface for execution, specifically targeting techniques that bypass User Account Control (UAC) as indicated by the detection string \"dccuacbypass.\" This rule aligns with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves adversaries leveraging PowerShell for malicious activities such as privilege escalation or unauthorized system access.",
  "ps_caldera_ttp_24": "The YARA rule \"ps_caldera_ttp_24\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell scripts that iterate over processes to identify and potentially interact with the \"lsass\" process, which is indicative of credential dumping attempts. This rule targets the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, highlighting the use of PowerShell for malicious activities related to process manipulation and credential access.",
  "bypassamsi_detection": "The YARA rule \"bypassamsi_detection\" is designed to identify PowerShell scripts that attempt to circumvent the Anti-Malware Scan Interface (AMSI), a security feature intended to prevent malicious code execution. By detecting specific strings such as \"bypass-amsi9000\" and \"enter-pshostprocess,\" the rule targets techniques associated with MITRE ATT&CK tactic T1059.001, which involves the execution of scripts to evade security mechanisms and gain unauthorized access or control over systems.",
  "ps_art_ttp_87": "The YARA rule \"ps_art_ttp_87\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell for malicious purposes, specifically targeting the abuse of environment variables and registry manipulation to execute scripts or load malicious DLLs, as indicated by the MITRE technique tag T1059.001 (PowerShell). The rule identifies suspicious patterns such as specific registry paths, PowerShell command parameters, and DLL names that are commonly associated with persistence and privilege escalation tactics.",
  "ps_caldera_ttp_30": "The YARA rule \"ps_caldera_ttp_30\" is designed to detect the execution of PowerShell scripts that utilize the PsTools suite for remote administration, potentially indicating the use of the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001 (PowerShell). This rule identifies patterns associated with downloading, extracting, and executing PsTools, which may be indicative of lateral movement or unauthorized system control activities.",
  "leak_installed_browsers_info": "The YARA rule \"leak_installed_browsers_info\" is designed to detect scripts that attempt to enumerate installed web browsers and potentially exfiltrate stored credentials, leveraging PowerShell-based techniques, as indicated by the presence of strings related to browser enumeration and credential dumping. This rule targets the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the use of PowerShell for malicious scripting activities, highlighting a threat of information disclosure and credential theft.",
  "check_vm_script_detection": "The YARA rule \"check_vm_script_detection\" is designed to identify scripts from the Nishang framework that are used to detect virtual machine environments, which is a technique often employed by adversaries to evade detection and analysis by security researchers. This rule targets the specific strings associated with various virtual machine platforms, aligning with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, indicating the use of PowerShell for system discovery and environment awareness.",
  "ps_caldera_ttp_18": "The YARA rule \"ps_caldera_ttp_18\" is designed to detect malicious activities associated with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the use of command-line interfaces to manipulate Wi-Fi network settings, such as retrieving all available Wi-Fi networks, enabling Wi-Fi, and accessing preferred Wi-Fi networks. This behavior is indicative of an adversary attempting to gather network information or manipulate network configurations for unauthorized access or data exfiltration.",
  "ps_art_ttp_50": "The YARA rule \"ps_art_ttp_50\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell scripts that employ encoded commands, specifically targeting the technique identified by MITRE ATT&CK as T1059.001, which involves the execution of scripts through PowerShell. Additionally, the rule identifies the presence of a DLL associated with the technique T1218.009, indicating potential abuse of Windows Management Instrumentation (WMI) for execution, suggesting a threat related to script-based execution and lateral movement.",
  "malicious_powershell_signature": "The YARA rule \"malicious_powershell_signature\" is designed to detect potentially malicious PowerShell scripts that aim to disable Windows Defender and bypass AMSI (Antimalware Scan Interface) protections, while also utilizing techniques such as downloading external content via `System.Net.WebClient`. This rule targets scripts that exhibit behaviors associated with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the use of PowerShell for executing malicious commands and loading assemblies to facilitate further exploitation or compromise.",
  "invoke_adsbackdoor_detection": "The YARA rule \"invoke_adsbackdoor_detection\" is designed to identify the malicious use of PowerShell scripts that leverage alternate data streams for persistence, specifically targeting the \"invoke-adsbackdoor\" technique. This detection focuses on identifying script-based threats that execute commands via PowerShell and manipulate registry settings to maintain persistence, aligning with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001 (Command and Scripting Interpreter: PowerShell).",
  "suspicious_powershell_assembly": "The YARA rule \"suspicious_powershell_assembly\" is designed to detect the use of obfuscated PowerShell commands that manipulate .NET assemblies, specifically targeting techniques associated with dynamic code execution and reflection, as indicated by the presence of \"[ref].assembly\" and \"gettype('{0}m{1}{2}'\". This behavior is commonly linked to adversaries leveraging PowerShell for executing malicious payloads or evading detection, corresponding to MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves command and scripting interpreter abuse.",
  "ps_art_ttp_78": "The YARA rule \"ps_art_ttp_78\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell commands that modify Windows Defender preferences by adding exclusion paths, specifically targeting the technique of \"Command and Scripting Interpreter: PowerShell\" as identified by MITRE ATT&CK ID T1059.001. This behavior may indicate an attempt to evade antivirus detection by excluding specific directories from scanning, which is a common tactic used by adversaries to maintain persistence or conceal malicious activities.",
  "dll_hijacking_poc": "The YARA rule \"dll_hijacking_poc\" is designed to detect attempts at DLL hijacking facilitated by a PowerShell script, which involves manipulating the loading process of dynamic link libraries to execute malicious code. The rule identifies specific strings indicative of AMSI bypass techniques and file manipulation commands, aligning with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which pertains to PowerShell-based execution.",
  "wmicdriveby_powershell_malicious": "The YARA rule \"wmicdriveby_powershell_malicious\" is designed to detect potentially malicious PowerShell scripts that utilize Office Web Client and WMIC drive-by techniques to execute code with elevated privileges, bypassing execution policies. It specifically targets scripts that download and execute remote content using PowerShell commands such as \"iex\" and \"invoke-wmimethod,\" which are indicative of the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, associated with command and scripting interpreter abuse.",
  "ps_windows_winpwn_dotnet": "The YARA rule \"ps_windows_winpwn_dotnet\" is designed to detect the presence of the WinPwn tool, specifically its .NET service-binaries enumeration functionality, which is indicative of adversaries performing system discovery and reconnaissance using PowerShell scripts. This rule targets the technique associated with MITRE ATT&CK T1518, focusing on identifying attempts to download and execute scripts that facilitate the enumeration of .NET services on Windows systems.",
  "malicious_powershell_activity_469a6": "The YARA rule \"malicious_powershell_activity_469a6\" is designed to detect potentially harmful PowerShell activities that involve file manipulation and command execution, specifically targeting behaviors associated with credential theft, firewall configuration changes, remote method invocation, process initiation, job scheduling, and network communication using PowerShell. The rule aligns with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which pertains to the use of PowerShell for executing malicious scripts and commands, indicating a threat of unauthorized access and system compromise.",
  "suspicious_powershell_activity_d7d75": "The YARA rule \"suspicious_powershell_activity_d7d75\" is designed to detect potentially malicious PowerShell scripts that engage in file manipulation and system data collection activities, which may indicate attempts to exfiltrate data or perform unauthorized system reconnaissance. Specifically, it targets the use of PowerShell commands associated with file copying, directory listing, string searching, data exporting, and file compression, aligning with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the abuse of PowerShell for execution.",
  "powershell_newfunctiondelegate": "The YARA rule \"powershell_newfunctiondelegate\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell scripts that employ suspicious techniques to execute assembly language functions, specifically by identifying patterns associated with dynamic memory allocation and function pointer manipulation, such as \"kernel32::virtualalloc\" and \"getfunctionpointer.\" This rule targets the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the abuse of PowerShell for executing potentially malicious code, indicative of in-memory execution or fileless malware tactics.",
  "invoke_sniffer_detection": "The YARA rule \"invoke_sniffer_detection\" is designed to identify the execution of a PowerShell-based packet sniffer, which is indicative of network reconnaissance or data interception activities. It detects specific strings and patterns associated with the creation and operation of a packet sniffer, leveraging PowerShell commands and techniques aligned with MITRE ATT&CK's \"Command and Scripting Interpreter: PowerShell\" (T1059.001), potentially used by adversaries to capture and analyze network traffic for unauthorized data access or exfiltration.",
  "malicious_pastebin_exfiltration": "The YARA rule \"malicious_pastebin_exfiltration\" is designed to detect scripts that utilize Pastebin as a platform for data exfiltration, specifically identifying patterns associated with API authentication and HTTP requests to Pastebin's login URI, indicative of PowerShell-based command and scripting interpreter activity as per MITRE technique T1059.001. This rule targets the malicious behavior of unauthorized data transfer through web utilities, highlighting potential threats related to credential theft and data leakage.",
  "malicious_powershell_script_2b862": "The YARA rule \"malicious_powershell_script_2b862\" is designed to detect PowerShell scripts that perform HTTP authentication testing and scanning, potentially indicating credential brute-forcing or unauthorized access attempts. It targets scripts that include specific strings related to login attempts, HTTP basic authentication testing, and web requests, aligning with the MITRE technique T1059.001, which involves the use of PowerShell for malicious purposes.",
  "generic_powershell_c15ee_156": "The YARA rule \"generic_powershell_c15ee_156\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell scripts that employ advanced techniques for in-memory execution and evasion, specifically targeting behaviors associated with \"invoke-memoryshell\" and \"invoke-polymorphicevasion.\" This rule aligns with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, indicating its focus on identifying malicious scripting activities that facilitate stealthy execution and persistence within compromised systems.",
  "detect_sql_powershell": "The YARA rule \"detect_sql_powershell\" is designed to identify potentially malicious PowerShell scripts that interact with SQL databases, specifically targeting scripts that may perform unauthorized SQL operations or reconnaissance activities, as indicated by the presence of functions and commands such as \"get-sqlconnectiontest\" and \"instance_regread.\" This rule aligns with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the use of PowerShell for execution, highlighting the risk of exploitation through SQL-related PowerShell commands that could facilitate lateral movement or privilege escalation within a network.",
  "cimlocaladmin_detection": "The YARA rule \"cimlocaladmin_detection\" is designed to identify potentially suspicious activity related to the use of the \"get-cimlocaladmin\" script, which may be employed by adversaries to enumerate local administrator accounts on Windows systems using CIM cmdlets. This detection aligns with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, indicating the use of PowerShell for system discovery and lateral movement, potentially facilitating unauthorized access or privilege escalation.",
  "hex_encoding_detection": "The YARA rule \"hex_encoding_detection\" is designed to identify scripts or code snippets that utilize hex encoding techniques, specifically targeting behaviors associated with PowerShell commands that convert text to byte arrays using ASCII encoding and format output in hexadecimal representation, which is indicative of obfuscation or data exfiltration techniques. This detection aligns with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves command and scripting interpreter abuse, often used by threat actors to evade detection and execute malicious payloads.",
  "ps_t1027_006_remote_art": "The YARA rule \"ps_t1027_006_remote_art\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell scripts that leverage the \"Invoke-WebRequest\" command to download potentially malicious content from GitHub, specifically targeting scripts associated with the MITRE technique T1059.001, which involves command and scripting interpreter usage for remote execution. This rule identifies artifacts indicative of adversaries employing remote scripts to execute commands or download resources, potentially as part of a broader attack strategy involving script-based threats.",
  "malicious_powershell_script_example_daa17": "The YARA rule \"malicious_powershell_script_example_daa17\" is designed to detect potentially malicious PowerShell scripts that attempt unauthorized access to databases by leveraging techniques such as container manipulation, credential testing, and service initiation, as indicated by the presence of specific strings related to database connectivity and management. This rule aligns with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the use of PowerShell for execution, highlighting a threat category focused on unauthorized database access and manipulation through scripting.",
  "ps_art_ttp_100": "The YARA rule \"ps_art_ttp_100\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell scripts that invoke malicious documents, aligning with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which pertains to the use of PowerShell for execution. This rule specifically targets the string \"invoke-maldoc,\" indicating the presence of scripts that may be used to execute or deliver malware through document files.",
  "generic_powershell_9aafd_493": "The YARA rule \"generic_powershell_9aafd_493\" is designed to detect the use of potentially malicious PowerShell scripts that employ various \"invoke\" functions, which are indicative of obfuscation and execution techniques commonly associated with script-based threats and post-exploitation activities. Tagged with MITRE technique T1059.001, this rule specifically targets behaviors related to the execution of obfuscated or automated PowerShell commands that may be used for lateral movement, persistence, or system manipulation.",
  "malicious_powershell_activity_d4e10": "The YARA rule \"malicious_powershell_activity_d4e10\" is designed to detect potentially harmful PowerShell scripts that exhibit behaviors associated with disabling security features, downloading malicious payloads, and executing credential theft tools like Mimikatz, aligning with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001 for PowerShell. The presence of strings such as \"interopservices,\" \"amsiutils,\" \"disablerealtime,\" \"downloadstring,\" and \"invoke-mimikatz\" indicates attempts to bypass security mechanisms, download and execute malicious code, and perform credential harvesting.",
  "ps_suspicious_jse_file_run_from_startup_folder": "The YARA rule \"ps_suspicious_jse_file_run_from_startup_folder\" is designed to detect the execution of potentially malicious JScript Encoded (JSE) files from the Windows Startup folder, which may indicate persistence mechanisms employed by adversaries to maintain access on a compromised system. This rule specifically targets the use of PowerShell commands, such as \"copy-item,\" in conjunction with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001 (Command and Scripting Interpreter: PowerShell) and T1547.001 (Boot or Logon Autostart Execution: Registry Run Keys/Startup Folder), highlighting a threat that leverages script-based persistence through startup folder manipulation.",
  "ps_microburst_azhybridworkerextraction": "The YARA rule \"ps_microburst_azhybridworkerextraction\" is designed to detect the use of the PowerShell command \"invoke-azhybridworkerextraction,\" which is associated with the execution of scripts or commands on Azure Hybrid Worker environments. This activity aligns with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, indicating potential abuse of PowerShell for unauthorized or malicious script execution in cloud-based or hybrid environments.",
  "set_criticalprocess": "The YARA rule \"set_criticalprocess\" is designed to detect the use of the \"set-criticalprocess\" command, which is associated with the abuse of Windows command-line interfaces to elevate a process to critical status, potentially preventing its termination and enabling persistence or evasion techniques. This behavior aligns with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, indicating a threat related to command and scripting interpreter misuse.",
  "ps_scheduled_task_1151": "The YARA rule \"ps_scheduled_task_1151\" is designed to detect the use of the \"schtasks\" command, which is indicative of adversaries leveraging scheduled tasks for execution, a technique categorized under MITRE ATT&CK ID T1059.001 (Command and Scripting Interpreter: PowerShell). This behavior is commonly associated with persistence mechanisms or task automation in malicious activities.",
  "get_gpppassword_detection": "The YARA rule \"get_gpppassword_detection\" is designed to identify scripts or activities that attempt to retrieve and decrypt passwords stored in Group Policy Preferences (GPP), a technique associated with PowerShell scripting and indicative of credential access or privilege escalation efforts. This detection aligns with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the use of PowerShell for executing commands that may compromise security by accessing sensitive configuration data.",
  "encode_base64_bypass": "The YARA rule \"encode_base64_bypass\" is designed to detect a PowerShell script employing an AMSI (Antimalware Scan Interface) bypass technique, which is a method used by attackers to evade security software and execute malicious payloads without detection. The rule identifies specific strings indicative of encoding payloads and bypassing AMSI, aligning with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which pertains to PowerShell-based execution.",
  "malicious_powershell_detection_d0b1e": "The YARA rule \"malicious_powershell_detection_d0b1e\" is designed to identify obfuscated PowerShell scripts that exhibit behaviors indicative of malicious activity, such as downloading and executing remote content, bypassing security mechanisms like AMSI (Antimalware Scan Interface), and manipulating PowerShell processes. This detection aligns with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the use of PowerShell for execution of potentially harmful scripts.",
  "invoke_smbscanner": "The YARA rule \"invoke_smbscanner\" is designed to detect the presence of scripts or tools that utilize the \"invoke-smbscanner\" command, which is indicative of attempts to scan and enumerate SMB (Server Message Block) services on a network. This activity aligns with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the use of PowerShell for executing commands that may facilitate lateral movement or reconnaissance within a network.",
  "invoke_adidnsspoofer": "The YARA rule \"invoke_adidnsspoofer\" is designed to detect the presence of scripts or commands that utilize the \"invoke-adidnsspoofer\" function, which is associated with DNS spoofing attacks targeting Active Directory environments. This detection aligns with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, indicating the use of PowerShell for executing malicious code to manipulate DNS responses and potentially redirect network traffic for further exploitation.",
  "powerdump_malicious_detection": "The YARA rule \"powerdump_malicious_detection\" is designed to identify the execution of potentially malicious PowerShell scripts that are used to dump credentials from a system, specifically targeting techniques associated with credential dumping. This rule detects patterns indicative of credential theft activities, such as invoking PowerShell functions to extract password hashes and security keys, which align with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001 (Command and Scripting Interpreter: PowerShell).",
  "powershell_get_ostoken_information": "The YARA rule \"powershell_get_ostoken_information\" is designed to detect the usage of a PowerShell script function that attempts to manipulate or access operating system tokens, specifically targeting processes like LSASS to elevate privileges or impersonate system accounts, which aligns with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001 (PowerShell). This rule identifies potential privilege escalation or credential theft activities by monitoring for specific strings indicative of attempts to bypass security controls and gain unauthorized access to sensitive system processes.",
  "suspicious_powershell_activity_786c0": "The YARA rule \"suspicious_powershell_activity_786c0\" is designed to detect potentially malicious PowerShell activity that involves the use of Windows Remote Management (WinRM) sessions and manipulation of credentials, indicative of lateral movement or credential theft techniques. Specifically, it targets the execution of PowerShell commands related to session invocation, secure string conversion, object creation for management tasks, and random name generation, aligning with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001 for PowerShell exploitation.",
  "ps_windows_auth_dll_lsass": "The YARA rule \"ps_windows_auth_dll_lsass\" is designed to detect the presence of a PowerShell script that attempts to manipulate the Local Security Authority Subsystem Service (LSASS) by copying a potentially malicious DLL named \"atomicredteampwfilter.dll\" into the system, targeting authentication packages and LSA keys. This activity is indicative of the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves PowerShell-based execution for credential theft or privilege escalation.",
  "malicious_powershell_branchhandler": "The YARA rule \"malicious_powershell_branchhandler\" is designed to detect potentially harmful activity in PowerShell scripts that involve the deletion of Git branches, which may indicate unauthorized or malicious manipulation of version control repositories.   "powershell_amsi_bypass_b2637": "The YARA rule \"powershell_amsi_bypass_b2637\" is designed to detect attempts to bypass the Antimalware Scan Interface (AMSI) in PowerShell scripts, which is a technique used by adversaries to evade detection and execute malicious code without interference from security software.   "ps_art_ttp_85": "The YARA rule \"ps_art_ttp_85\" is designed to detect the presence and execution of UltraViewer-related processes, specifically targeting the use of \"t1219_ultraviewer.exe\" and \"ultraviewer_desktop.exe\" with the \"-passthru\" parameter, which may indicate unauthorized remote access or control activities.   "malicious_windows_share": "The YARA rule \"malicious_windows_share\" is designed to detect a potentially malicious PowerShell script that creates and shares a Windows folder, specifically targeting the use of the \"Win32_Share\" class and related methods such as \"Get-Member\" and \"Shares.Create\" to establish unauthorized network shares.   "generic_powershell_acaab_558": "The YARA rule \"generic_powershell_acaab_558\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell scripts that potentially execute malicious tasks related to cryptocurrency wallets, as indicated by the presence of specific strings such as \"invoke-tasks\" and \"invoke-crypto_wallets.\" This rule targets the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the abuse of PowerShell for execution, commonly used by threat actors to automate malicious activities and facilitate unauthorized access to sensitive financial data.",
