  "ps_reg_add_idontexist": "The YARA rule \"ps_reg_add_idontexist\" is designed to detect the use of a command that modifies the Windows Registry to add a non-existent DLL file under the CLSID key, potentially indicating an attempt to execute or load malicious code via registry manipulation. This behavior aligns with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves command and scripting interpreter abuse for persistence or privilege escalation.",
  "malicious_powershell_detection_0ce2f": "The YARA rule \"malicious_powershell_detection_0ce2f\" is designed to identify PowerShell scripts that exhibit behavior indicative of DLL injection, specifically through downloading a DLL file from a remote server and injecting it into a running process, such as \"explorer.exe.\" This detection aligns with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the use of PowerShell for executing malicious code, potentially leading to unauthorized access or control over system processes.",
  "generic_powershell_7157b_402": "The YARA rule \"generic_powershell_7157b_402\" is designed to detect the use of the PowerShell command \"invoke-soteriaps,\" which is indicative of a script or tool potentially used for malicious purposes, such as executing unauthorized code or scripts on a system. This detection aligns with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the abuse of PowerShell for command and script execution, often leveraged by attackers for system exploitation or lateral movement.",
  "powershell_loadlibrary_malware": "The YARA rule \"powershell_loadlibrary_malware\" is designed to detect potentially malicious PowerShell scripts that utilize techniques for dynamically loading DLLs and invoking functions, which may indicate attempts to execute arbitrary code or perform unauthorized actions on a system. Specifically, it targets scripts employing methods such as \"invoke-loadlibrary\" and \"getdelegateforfunction,\" which align with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, highlighting the use of PowerShell for code execution.",
  "generic_powershell_c2ac5_41": "The YARA rule \"generic_powershell_c2ac5_41\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell scripts that attempt to bypass AMSI (Antimalware Scan Interface) protections, a technique commonly associated with adversaries seeking to execute malicious code undetected. This behavior aligns with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the abuse of PowerShell for execution of unauthorized or harmful activities.",
  "windows_console_malicious_activity": "The YARA rule \"windows_console_malicious_activity\" is designed to detect suspicious activities in Windows console tools that may indicate the execution of malicious scripts or commands, specifically targeting the collection of system information, user credentials, and exploitation of unquoted service paths. This rule aligns with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the use of command-line interfaces for executing potentially harmful operations.",
  "hostsfile_modification_script": "The YARA rule \"hostsfile_modification_script\" is designed to detect PowerShell scripts that modify the system's hosts file to potentially redirect network traffic, which is indicative of adversaries attempting to manipulate DNS resolutions for malicious purposes, such as redirecting users to a command and control (C2) server, exemplified by the presence of \"c2.evil.com.\" This activity aligns with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the use of scripting for execution, highlighting a potential threat of unauthorized network traffic redirection or interception.",
  "malicious_powershell_script_0a3f9": "The YARA rule \"malicious_powershell_script_0a3f9\" is designed to detect the execution of potentially malicious PowerShell scripts that utilize the `net.webclient` class to download files from a specified IP address (10.10.0.0), including executables such as \"eoploaddriver.exe\" and \"nc.exe\", which may indicate attempts to exploit vulnerabilities or establish unauthorized network connections. This rule targets the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the use of PowerShell for scripting and automation to facilitate malicious activities such as downloading and executing payloads.",
  "invoke_exfildatatogithub": "The YARA rule \"invoke_exfildatatogithub\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell scripts that exfiltrate data to GitHub repositories, leveraging the technique of command and scripting interpreter as outlined in MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001. This rule identifies potential data exfiltration activities where adversaries use PowerShell to transfer sensitive information to external GitHub locations, posing a threat to data confidentiality.",
  "generic_powershell_a58da_264": "The YARA rule \"generic_powershell_a58da_264\" is designed to detect the use of specific PowerShell scripts that manipulate Event Tracing for Windows (ETW) by invoking functions such as \"invoke-suspendetw\" and \"invoke-restoreetw,\" which are indicative of attempts to evade detection and analysis by disabling or restoring ETW logging. This behavior aligns with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the use of PowerShell for execution, often employed by adversaries to conceal malicious activities and hinder forensic investigations.",
  "ps_windows_dns_c2": "The YARA rule \"ps_windows_dns_c2\" is designed to detect the initiation of a command and control (C2) session over the DNS protocol, specifically targeting the use of dnscat2, a tool commonly employed for covert communication between compromised systems and threat actors. This rule identifies potential DNS tunneling activities associated with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1071.004, which involves using DNS as a communication channel for malicious purposes.",
  "malicious_pesterhavecount_detection": "The YARA rule \"malicious_pesterhavecount_detection\" is designed to identify potentially malicious PowerShell scripts that misuse the \"pesterhavecount\" function, which may indicate attempts to execute unauthorized or harmful code by exploiting script testing frameworks. This detection aligns with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, highlighting the use of scripting for execution, particularly in scenarios where adversaries leverage PowerShell for malicious purposes.",
  "potential_malicious_powershell_8c426": "The YARA rule \"potential_malicious_powershell_8c426\" is designed to detect potentially malicious PowerShell scripts that exhibit behaviors associated with code obfuscation, manipulation of text encoding, and error handling, which are indicative of the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001 (PowerShell). This rule targets scripts that may attempt to evade detection or perform unauthorized actions by utilizing specific functions and patterns such as \"undecoratesymbolname\" and \"dispose-objects,\" which are commonly used in sophisticated malware or exploitation frameworks.",
  "powershell_function_hash": "The YARA rule \"powershell_function_hash\" is designed to detect suspicious PowerShell activity that involves the use of function hashing techniques, which are often employed by adversaries to obfuscate and execute malicious code without direct reference to function names. This rule specifically targets patterns associated with the use of hashed function names, such as \"getprocaddresswithhash,\" and other indicators like \"meatsploit\" and \"rorvalue = 13,\" which align with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, indicating potential script-based execution for evading detection.",
  "ps_kerberos_1": "The YARA rule \"ps_kerberos_1\" is designed to detect malicious activity related to Kerberos authentication manipulation, specifically targeting the use of PowerShell scripts that may attempt to exploit or interfere with Kerberos ticket handling and authentication processes. This rule aligns with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the use of PowerShell for executing commands that could compromise security by interacting with authentication mechanisms.",
  "ps_createremotethread_1064": "The YARA rule \"ps_createremotethread_1064\" is designed to detect the use of the CreateRemoteThread function, which is commonly employed by adversaries to execute code within the address space of another process, facilitating process injection or remote code execution. This technique is associated with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, indicating its use in scripting for malicious purposes such as privilege escalation or persistence.",
  "mocking_global_functions_part_one": "The YARA rule \"mocking_global_functions_part_one\" is designed to detect scripts that temporarily replace or mock global functions in a system, potentially for malicious purposes such as evading security controls or altering system behavior, and subsequently verify their restoration. This activity is associated with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the use of PowerShell for execution, indicating a threat related to script-based manipulation of system functions.",
  "ps_mocbin_art": "The YARA rule \"ps_mocbin_art\" is designed to detect the execution of a headless Chrome browser potentially used for automated web interactions or data exfiltration, specifically targeting the mockbin.org domain, which may be associated with adversarial command and control activities. This rule aligns with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, indicating the use of command-line interfaces for malicious scripting or automation.",
  "malicious_powershell_downloads": "The YARA rule \"malicious_powershell_downloads\" is designed to detect PowerShell-based activities indicative of malicious tool downloads and reverse shell creation, specifically targeting behaviors associated with downloading and executing tools like Mimikatz, PowerCat, and BloodHound collectors. It identifies potential exploitation of the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the use of PowerShell for executing commands that facilitate unauthorized access and lateral movement within a network.",
  "invoke_globalo365mailsearch": "The YARA rule \"invoke_globalo365mailsearch\" is designed to detect the use of a PowerShell command that targets Microsoft Office 365 mailboxes, potentially indicating an attempt to perform unauthorized searches or data exfiltration within a cloud email environment. This activity aligns with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the use of PowerShell for execution, often leveraged by threat actors to manipulate or access sensitive information in cloud services.",
  "azure_cleanup_script": "The YARA rule \"azure_cleanup_script\" is designed to detect scripts or command sequences that perform unauthorized deletions of Azure Active Directory users, applications, and groups, potentially indicating malicious activity related to account and resource cleanup. This rule targets the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the use of command-line interfaces for execution, suggesting a threat actor's attempt to remove traces of their presence or disrupt cloud infrastructure operations.",
  "ps_art_ttp_9": "The YARA rule \"ps_art_ttp_9\" is designed to detect the execution of PowerShell scripts that utilize the \"invoke-athremotefxvgpudisablementcommand\" and \"atomictestharnesses\" strings, indicating potential abuse of PowerShell for remote command execution or testing harnesses, which aligns with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001 for PowerShell. This rule targets the identification of scripts that may be used for malicious purposes such as system manipulation or lateral movement within a network.",
  "ps_excel_4_macro": "The YARA rule \"ps_excel_4_macro\" is designed to detect the presence of Excel 4.0 macros, specifically targeting scripts that execute Visual Basic Script (VBS) code, which is indicative of the technique T1059.001 under the MITRE ATT&CK framework. This rule identifies potential malicious behavior where environment variables are manipulated to execute unauthorized scripts, posing a threat of code execution and system compromise.",
  "ps_obfuscation_229": "The YARA rule \"ps_obfuscation_229\" is designed to detect obfuscated PowerShell scripts that manipulate variables in a manner consistent with the technique of string concatenation and variable expansion, often used to evade detection and analysis. This rule targets the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the use of obfuscated scripting languages to execute malicious commands or payloads.",
  "keylogger_scheduler_detection": "The YARA rule \"keylogger_scheduler_detection\" is designed to identify a malicious PowerShell script that employs techniques to execute a keylogger in a stealthy manner by using hidden windows and scheduling tasks, as indicated by the use of \"start-process\" with \"-windowstyle hidden\" and the manipulation of date and environment variables. This behavior aligns with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the use of PowerShell for execution, often leveraged by threat actors to maintain persistence and evade detection.",
  "malicious_powershell_speechsynthesizer": "The YARA rule \"malicious_powershell_speechsynthesizer\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell scripts that leverage the System.Speech.SpeechSynthesizer class to execute potentially malicious activities, aligning with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the abuse of scripting for execution. This rule specifically identifies scripts that instantiate a SpeechSynthesizer object and invoke its Speak method, potentially indicating attempts to use text-to-speech functionalities for obfuscation or unauthorized actions.",
  "ps_art_ttp_57": "The YARA rule \"ps_art_ttp_57\" is designed to detect a privilege escalation technique where an attacker replaces the \"sethc.exe\" (Sticky Keys) executable with a malicious payload, such as \"cmd.exe,\" to gain unauthorized administrative access, as indicated by the presence of strings related to file replacement and permission modification. This technique is associated with the MITRE ATT&CK tactic T1059.001, which involves command and scripting interpreter abuse for execution.",
  "malicious_powershell_detection_680cb": "The YARA rule \"malicious_powershell_detection_680cb\" is designed to identify the misuse of PowerShell scripts that leverage the \"Invoke-RestMethod\" command to query the external service \"http://ipinfo.io/\" for IP address information, potentially indicating reconnaissance activity associated with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001 (PowerShell). This behavior may be indicative of an adversary attempting to ascertain the external IP address of a compromised system, which can be a precursor to further network exploitation or data exfiltration activities.",
  "ps_art_ttp_43": "The YARA rule \"ps_art_ttp_43\" is designed to detect potential abuse of the Windows Print Spooler service, specifically targeting the execution of PowerShell commands and registry modifications associated with the Print Spooler service, which aligns with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001 (PowerShell). This rule identifies suspicious activity that may indicate attempts to manipulate or exploit the Print Spooler service for privilege escalation or lateral movement within a network.",
  "ps_windows_password_spray_invoke_domainpasswordspray_light": "The YARA rule \"ps_windows_password_spray_invoke_domainpasswordspray_light\" is designed to detect the execution of PowerShell scripts that perform password spraying attacks against Windows domain accounts, specifically identifying scripts that utilize the DirectoryServices API to iterate over user accounts and report successful password attempts. This rule targets the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the use of PowerShell for malicious purposes, highlighting the threat of unauthorized access through systematic password guessing.",
  "ps_getkeystrokes_analysis": "The YARA rule \"ps_getkeystrokes_analysis\" is designed to detect PowerShell scripts that exhibit keylogging behavior, specifically targeting scripts that capture keystrokes and potentially log user input by identifying patterns such as \"getasynckeystate\" and \"mainwindowtitle.\" This rule aligns with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the use of PowerShell for malicious purposes, including unauthorized data collection and surveillance activities.",
  "powershell_pe_manipulation": "The YARA rule \"powershell_pe_manipulation\" is designed to detect PowerShell scripts that manipulate Portable Executable (PE) files, specifically targeting modifications to PE headers and section headers, which may indicate the injection or alteration of shellcode within the '.text' section. This behavior aligns with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, highlighting the use of PowerShell for malicious purposes such as code injection or execution of unauthorized payloads.",
  "powershell_stager_detection": "The YARA rule \"powershell_stager_detection\" is designed to identify malicious PowerShell scripts that utilize stager techniques to download and execute additional payloads, often bypassing execution policies, as indicated by the presence of strings such as \"downloadstring\" and \"-nop -ex bypass.\" This rule targets the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the use of PowerShell for command and script execution, commonly employed in initial access or lateral movement phases of cyber attacks.",
  "ps_caldera_ttp_23": "The YARA rule \"ps_caldera_ttp_23\" is designed to detect the execution of a PowerShell command that recursively searches for hidden directories named \".git\" within the \"C:\\Users\" directory, potentially indicating an attempt to locate and exfiltrate sensitive version control repositories. This behavior aligns with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the use of PowerShell for malicious purposes, such as unauthorized data access or lateral movement within a compromised system.",
  "ps_art_ttp_94": "The YARA rule \"ps_art_ttp_94\" is designed to detect the presence of scripts or activities associated with the Atomic Red Team framework, specifically targeting the execution of PowerShell scripts as indicated by MITRE technique T1059.001. The detection strings suggest monitoring for the download and execution of files related to Atomic Red Team's testing capabilities, which may be used for simulating adversary behavior in a controlled environment.",
  "invoke_psremoting": "The YARA rule \"invoke_psremoting\" is designed to detect the use of the \"Invoke-PSRemoting\" command, which is associated with the execution of remote PowerShell commands, a technique categorized under MITRE ATT&CK's T1059.001. This behavior may indicate lateral movement or remote code execution attempts by threat actors seeking to control or execute commands on remote systems within a network.",
  "invoke_reflectivedllinjection": "The YARA rule \"invoke_reflectivedllinjection\" is designed to detect the use of reflective DLL injection, a technique where a DLL is loaded into a process's memory without being written to disk, often used by adversaries to execute malicious code stealthily. This rule specifically targets the invocation of this technique, aligning with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves command and scripting interpreter abuse for execution.",
  "ps_caldera_ttp_37": "The YARA rule \"ps_caldera_ttp_37\" is designed to detect the execution of PowerShell scripts that utilize the \"Start-Process\" cmdlet with the \"-WindowStyle Hidden\" parameter, indicating potential stealthy execution of commands, alongside references to \"manx.go\" and \"downloaddata,\" which suggest the downloading and execution of malicious payloads. This rule targets the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the use of PowerShell for command and script execution, often employed by adversaries to conduct fileless attacks and evade detection.",
  "powershell_deobfuscation": "The YARA rule \"powershell_deobfuscation\" is designed to detect obfuscated PowerShell scripts that utilize techniques such as string manipulation and reflection to evade detection, specifically targeting the execution of potentially malicious code through deobfuscation methods. The rule identifies patterns associated with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the use of PowerShell for scripting and command execution, often employed by threat actors to execute payloads or perform reconnaissance on compromised systems.",
  "ps_stager_318": "The YARA rule \"ps_stager_318\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell scripts that employ the \"serverxmlhttp\" object to send HTTP requests and execute code via the \"iex\" (Invoke-Expression) command, indicative of a technique used for remote code execution and script-based stagers. This behavior aligns with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves PowerShell for command and script execution, often utilized in initial access or lateral movement phases of cyber attacks.",
  "get_objdump_script": "The YARA rule \"get_objdump_script\" is designed to detect the execution of a PowerShell script that analyzes COFF (Common Object File Format) headers, which may indicate the use of a script for reverse engineering or inspecting binary files, potentially as part of a reconnaissance or exploitation activity. This detection aligns with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the use of PowerShell for malicious purposes, highlighting a threat related to script-based execution and analysis of object files.",
  "invoke_bloodhound": "The YARA rule \"invoke_bloodhound\" is designed to detect the use of the \"Invoke-BloodHound\" command, which is associated with the execution of PowerShell scripts for Active Directory enumeration and privilege escalation analysis, aligning with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001 (PowerShell). This detection targets the reconnaissance phase of adversaries seeking to map and exploit Active Directory environments for lateral movement and privilege escalation.",
  "potential_malicious_task_script": "The YARA rule \"potential_malicious_task_script\" is designed to detect PowerShell scripts that potentially modify scheduled tasks, a technique associated with MITRE ATT&CK tactic T1059.001, which involves the use of command and scripting interpreters for execution. The detection focuses on identifying scripts that contain specific Spanish-language strings indicative of task manipulation, suggesting unauthorized or malicious alterations to scheduled tasks.",
  "ps_cert_atomic": "The YARA rule \"ps_cert_atomic\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell scripts associated with the Atomic Red Team framework, specifically targeting the generation or manipulation of self-signed certificates, which aligns with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001 (PowerShell). This rule identifies potential misuse of PowerShell for certificate-related activities that could be leveraged for unauthorized access or persistence in a compromised environment.",
  "potential_malicious_powershell_detection": "The YARA rule \"potential_malicious_powershell_detection\" is designed to identify potentially malicious PowerShell scripts that exhibit behaviors associated with downloading and executing remote content, cryptographic operations, and data encoding/decoding, which are indicative of the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001 (PowerShell). This rule targets scripts that may leverage PowerShell for unauthorized data retrieval, encryption, and obfuscation, commonly used in cyber attacks for payload delivery and evasion.",
  "leak_putty_credentials": "The YARA rule \"leak_putty_credentials\" is designed to detect scripts that exfiltrate stored credentials from PuTTY sessions, potentially leveraging techniques associated with MITRE ATT&CK's Command and Scripting Interpreter (T1059.001). It identifies specific strings indicative of credential theft, including references to PuTTY session data and proxy configurations, suggesting a threat actor's attempt to access sensitive information stored in the Windows registry under the path \"hkcu:\\\\software\\\\simon\".",
  "powershell_keylogger_exfiltration": "The YARA rule \"powershell_keylogger_exfiltration\" is designed to detect a malicious PowerShell script that functions as a keylogger, capturing keystrokes and potentially exfiltrating the collected data over the network. It identifies specific strings indicative of keylogging activity, such as keystroke capture and data transmission using web client methods, aligning with the MITRE technique T1059.001, which pertains to PowerShell-based scripting for execution.",
  "generic_powershell_b3f78_371": "The YARA rule \"generic_powershell_b3f78_371\" is designed to detect the use of obfuscated or potentially malicious PowerShell scripts, specifically identifying the presence of the string \"invoke-peepeepoopoo,\" which may indicate the execution of unauthorized or harmful code. This rule targets the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the abuse of PowerShell for scripting and command execution in a manner that could compromise system security.",
  "ps_microburst_azdomaininfo": "The YARA rule \"ps_microburst_azdomaininfo\" is designed to detect the use of the PowerShell command \"get-azdomaininfo,\" which is indicative of adversaries leveraging PowerShell scripting to gather domain information in Azure environments. This activity aligns with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the use of PowerShell for execution, potentially facilitating reconnaissance or lateral movement within cloud infrastructures.",
  "powershell_amsi_bypass_5ee4e": "The YARA rule \"powershell_amsi_bypass_5ee4e\" is designed to detect PowerShell scripts that employ techniques to bypass the Antimalware Scan Interface (AMSI), a security feature intended to prevent malicious scripts from executing. By identifying specific strings and patterns associated with AMSI bypass methods, this rule targets the evasion technique categorized under MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the use of scripting for execution.",
  "ps_stager_331": "The YARA rule \"ps_stager_331\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell scripts that employ obfuscation techniques, such as `iex` (Invoke-Expression), and potentially malicious operations like binary XOR (`-bxor`) and dynamic assembly loading (`y]::load`), which are indicative of script-based stagers used in initial access or persistence mechanisms. The rule also identifies attempts to create shortcuts in the startup folder, suggesting a technique for establishing persistence, aligning with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001 (PowerShell).",
  "ps_caldera_ttp_22": "The YARA rule \"ps_caldera_ttp_22\" is designed to detect scripts or code snippets that utilize PowerShell to interact with system variables and file paths, potentially indicating the execution of system discovery or data collection activities consistent with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001 (PowerShell). This rule identifies patterns that suggest the enumeration of system information and manipulation of file-related attributes, which may be indicative of reconnaissance or preparation for further malicious actions on Windows 10 systems.",
  "ps_caldera_ttp_36": "The YARA rule \"ps_caldera_ttp_36\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell scripts that leverage the BitsTransfer module for downloading files, staging them in specific folders, and utilizing the ProcDump tool to extract memory dumps from the lsass.exe process, indicative of credential theft techniques. This rule targets the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the execution of commands and scripts via PowerShell, commonly used in post-exploitation activities to facilitate lateral movement and privilege escalation.",
  "ps_art_ttp_95": "The YARA rule \"ps_art_ttp_95\" is designed to detect the modification of the Windows Registry key \"HKLM\\Software\\Microsoft\\Windows NT\\CurrentVersion\\Winlogon\\Shell\" to include \"explorer.exe, c:\\windows\\system32\\cmd.exe,\" which indicates a potential persistence mechanism where the command prompt is launched alongside the Windows Explorer shell upon user login. This behavior is associated with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, PowerShell, and suggests an attempt to maintain unauthorized access or execute commands automatically during system startup.",
  "nishang_lsa_secret": "The YARA rule \"nishang_lsa_secret\" is designed to detect the use of Nishang PowerShell scripts that extract Local Security Authority (LSA) secrets from the Windows registry, a technique associated with credential access and privilege escalation. This rule targets specific strings indicative of the script's operation, aligning with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves PowerShell-based execution for malicious purposes.",
  "untrusted_nuget_download": "The YARA rule \"untrusted_nuget_download\" is designed to detect potentially malicious activity involving the unauthorized or suspicious downloading and execution of NuGet packages, which may indicate script-based attacks or exploitation attempts using PowerShell commands. Specifically, it targets behaviors associated with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the use of PowerShell for execution, highlighting attempts to download and run NuGet packages from untrusted sources.",
  "invoke_tokenduplication": "The YARA rule \"invoke_tokenduplication\" is designed to detect the use of the \"Invoke-TokenDuplication\" technique, which is associated with PowerShell scripting to duplicate access tokens for privilege escalation or lateral movement within a network. This technique is categorized under MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, indicating the use of command and scripting interpreters for potentially unauthorized access or execution.",
  "invoke_defendertools": "The YARA rule \"invoke_defendertools\" is designed to detect the potentially malicious use of PowerShell scripts that interact with Windows Defender to disable real-time monitoring and AMSI (Antimalware Scan Interface), as well as add exclusions to bypass security measures, which aligns with the MITRE technique T1059.001 for PowerShell-based command and script execution. This behavior is indicative of threat actors attempting to weaken system defenses to facilitate further malicious activities.",
  "ps_cobaltstrike_349": "The YARA rule \"ps_cobaltstrike_349\" is designed to detect the presence of Cobalt Strike, a post-exploitation tool often used by threat actors for lateral movement and command execution, by identifying specific strings associated with its technique of using \"getdelegateforfunctionpointer\" to dynamically resolve and execute functions like \"VirtualAlloc\" via \"GetProcAddress\" in \"kernel32.dll.\" This detection aligns with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the use of PowerShell for malicious script execution.",
  "ps_disable_excel_protection": "The YARA rule \"ps_disable_excel_protection\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell scripts that modify registry settings to disable security warnings and protections in Microsoft Excel, potentially facilitating the execution of malicious macros or the opening of harmful attachments without user intervention. This behavior aligns with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the use of PowerShell for execution, indicating a threat actor's attempt to bypass security controls and enhance the delivery of malicious payloads.",
  "invoke_checklocaladminaccess": "The YARA rule \"invoke_checklocaladminaccess\" is designed to detect the use of a PowerShell script that checks for local administrative access on a system, which is indicative of the adversary technique T1059.001 (PowerShell) under the MITRE ATT&CK framework. This behavior is often associated with privilege escalation attempts, where attackers seek to identify and exploit administrative privileges to gain greater control over the compromised system.",
  "powershell_field_setvalue": "The YARA rule \"powershell_field_setvalue\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell scripts that invoke the \"setvalue\" method, potentially in combination with parameters such as \"$null,$true,\" which may indicate attempts to manipulate object fields or properties in a manner consistent with code injection or unauthorized configuration changes. This detection aligns with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the use of scripting for execution, highlighting potential threats related to script-based attacks or system exploitation.",
  "ps_get_aduser": "The YARA rule \"ps_get_aduser\" is designed to detect the use of the PowerShell cmdlet \"Get-ADUser,\" which is commonly employed in Active Directory enumeration activities. This detection is associated with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, indicating potential reconnaissance or lateral movement by adversaries seeking to gather information about user accounts within a network.",
  "ps_obfuscation_228": "The YARA rule \"ps_obfuscation_228\" is designed to detect obfuscated PowerShell scripts that manipulate the 'ofs' variable, a technique often used to obscure command execution and evade detection, aligning with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001 (PowerShell). This rule identifies patterns indicative of script obfuscation, which is commonly employed by threat actors to conceal malicious activities and bypass security controls.",
  "get_laps_passwords_detection": "The YARA rule \"get_laps_passwords_detection\" is designed to identify the potential misuse of scripts that attempt to retrieve Local Administrator Password Solution (LAPS) passwords from Active Directory, which is indicative of adversaries leveraging the Command and Scripting Interpreter technique (MITRE ATT&CK T1059.001) to gain unauthorized access to privileged credentials. The detection focuses on specific strings associated with LDAP queries and script operations that are commonly used to extract and manipulate sensitive password information from directory services.",
  "getkerbtix_potential_malicious_activity": "The YARA rule \"getkerbtix_potential_malicious_activity\" is designed to detect the execution of a PowerShell script that enumerates Kerberos tickets and gathers session information, indicative of potential credential theft or lateral movement activities. This rule targets the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the use of PowerShell for malicious purposes, specifically focusing on the unauthorized access and manipulation of authentication data within a network.",
  "ps_windows_get_domaincontroller_with_powerview": "The YARA rule \"ps_windows_get_domaincontroller_with_powerview\" is designed to detect the use of PowerView, a PowerShell tool, to execute the \"get-domaincontroller\" command, which is indicative of adversaries performing network reconnaissance to identify domain controllers within a Windows environment. This activity aligns with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1018, \"Remote System Discovery,\" and may be part of an initial information-gathering phase in a broader attack strategy.",
  "generic_powershell_8ab82_11": "The YARA rule \"generic_powershell_8ab82_11\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell scripts that invoke the \"invoke-psuacme\" command, which is indicative of potential abuse of PowerShell for unauthorized or malicious activities, aligning with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001 (PowerShell). This rule targets the detection of scripts that may be used for executing commands or scripts in a manner that could facilitate system compromise or unauthorized access.",
  "potential_malicious_powershell_script_f6cb5": "The YARA rule \"potential_malicious_powershell_script_f6cb5\" is designed to detect PowerShell scripts that exhibit behavior indicative of process dumping, a technique used by adversaries to extract the memory contents of a running process for analysis or exploitation.   "powershell_sitelist_exfiltration": "The YARA rule \"powershell_sitelist_exfiltration\" is designed to detect malicious PowerShell scripts that exfiltrate data by decrypting and transforming XML sitelist data, potentially using 3DES encryption in ECB mode, and subsequently transmitting it to a specified server IP.   "ps_caldera_ttp_34": "The YARA rule \"ps_caldera_ttp_34\" is designed to detect the execution of the \"esentutl.exe\" utility in conjunction with the use of Windows Management Instrumentation Command-line (WMIC) to create a process, indicative of potential abuse of system utilities for malicious purposes, specifically related to the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves command and scripting interpreter usage on Windows systems. This behavior may suggest an attempt to execute unauthorized scripts or commands, potentially for lateral movement or persistence within a compromised environment.",
  "ps_caldera_ttp_20": "The YARA rule \"ps_caldera_ttp_20\" is designed to detect scripts or programs that interact with GitHub's API, potentially for unauthorized data exfiltration or manipulation, by identifying specific strings related to GitHub user authentication, token handling, and data encoding. This rule is associated with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which pertains to PowerShell scripting, indicating the use of PowerShell to execute commands that may facilitate credential theft or unauthorized access to GitHub repositories.",
  "ps_art_ttp_97": "The YARA rule \"ps_art_ttp_97\" is designed to detect the presence of a specific persistence mechanism involving the modification of the Windows Registry to load a malicious DLL during the logon process, as indicated by the strings referencing \"atomicredteam\" and \"atomicnotificationpackage.dll.\" This rule targets the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves PowerShell-based scripting for execution, highlighting a threat related to unauthorized code execution and persistence on Windows systems.",
  "ps_art_ttp_83": "The YARA rule \"ps_art_ttp_83\" is designed to detect the abuse of Windows Registry modifications to establish persistence or execute malicious payloads by leveraging the \"HKCU:\\Software\\Classes\\AppX82a6gwre4fdg3bt635tn5ctqjf8msdd2\\shell\\open\\command\" registry path, which is indicative of a technique associated with MITRE ATT&CK ID T1059.001 (PowerShell). This rule specifically identifies the use of PowerShell commands to create or modify registry entries, potentially involving the \"wsreset.exe\" utility and the \"delegateexecute\" parameter, which can be exploited to execute arbitrary commands or scripts without user consent.",
  "invoke_reversednslookup_malicious_acc0b": "The YARA rule \"invoke_reversednslookup_malicious_acc0b\" is designed to detect the use of a potentially malicious PowerShell script that performs reverse DNS lookups, which may be indicative of adversaries attempting to gather network information or perform reconnaissance activities. The rule targets specific strings associated with improper input handling and invalid IP address resolution, aligning with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the use of PowerShell for execution.",
  "malicious_powershell_tcpclient_02b69": "The YARA rule \"malicious_powershell_tcpclient_02b69\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell scripts that establish network connections using the TcpClient class, potentially indicating command and control communication or data exfiltration, as evidenced by the presence of specific strings related to socket operations, ASCII encoding, and execution of received data.   "malicious_powershell_carbuncle": "The YARA rule \"malicious_powershell_carbuncle\" is designed to detect the execution of a potentially malicious PowerShell script known as \"invoke-carbuncle,\" which utilizes techniques such as Base64 encoding, GZIP compression, and dynamic loading of byte arrays to obfuscate and execute payloads. This rule targets the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, indicating a focus on PowerShell-based scripting for execution, often associated with post-exploitation activities and lateral movement.",
  "ps_art_ttp_96": "The YARA rule \"ps_art_ttp_96\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell commands that modify the Windows Registry to alter the Userinit value under the Winlogon key, potentially enabling the execution of cmd.exe upon user login, which is indicative of the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001 (PowerShell). This behavior is commonly associated with persistence mechanisms employed by threat actors to maintain access to compromised systems.",
  "ps_caldera_ttp_21": "The YARA rule \"ps_caldera_ttp_21\" is designed to detect scripts or binaries that interact with GitHub's API, potentially for unauthorized data exfiltration or manipulation, by identifying patterns related to GitHub user credentials, repository details, and encoded file operations. This rule targets the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves command and scripting interpreter activity, specifically PowerShell, indicating potential misuse of GitHub for malicious purposes such as credential theft or data exfiltration.",
  "ps_art_ttp_82": "The YARA rule \"ps_art_ttp_82\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell scripts that employ the technique of encoding data into Base64 format, potentially for the purpose of exfiltrating sensitive information via DNS tunneling, as indicated by the presence of strings related to data encoding and DNS server interactions. This rule targets the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the use of PowerShell for command and scripting interpreter activities, often leveraged by threat actors for obfuscation and data exfiltration.",
  "ps_caldera_ttp_35": "The YARA rule \"ps_caldera_ttp_35\" is designed to detect the presence of a specific string, \"proof that this machine was hacked,\" which indicates the execution of PowerShell scripts associated with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, commonly used for command and script execution in post-compromise activities.   "detect_sharpprintnightmare_usage": "The YARA rule \"detect_sharpprintnightmare_usage\" is designed to identify the execution of the SharpPrintNightmare tool within PowerShell scripts, which is associated with exploiting the PrintNightmare vulnerability to achieve remote code execution or privilege escalation. This detection targets specific strings indicative of the tool's usage, aligning with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which pertains to the use of PowerShell for malicious purposes.",
  "powershell_hostrecon_detection": "The \"powershell_hostrecon_detection\" YARA rule identifies the execution of the Invoke-HostRecon PowerShell script, which is used for reconnaissance activities by gathering system and network information such as hostname, IP address, domain details, user accounts, running processes, and performing network scans. This behavior aligns with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, indicating potential malicious use of PowerShell for system discovery and lateral movement preparation by adversaries.",
  "powershell_bypass_tool": "The YARA rule \"powershell_bypass_tool\" is designed to detect attempts to bypass the Antimalware Scan Interface (AMSI) using PowerShell, a technique often employed by adversaries to execute malicious scripts without being detected by security software. This rule specifically identifies patterns associated with AMSI bypass methods, which are categorized under MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, indicating the use of PowerShell for command and script execution.",
  "eventlogfailed_script": "The YARA rule \"eventlogfailed_script\" is designed to detect the suspicious use of PowerShell scripts that attempt to query Windows event logs for failed login attempts (event ID 4625) and associated network information, potentially indicating unauthorized access attempts or reconnaissance activities. This rule targets the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the use of PowerShell for execution, highlighting potential misuse of PowerShell cmdlets to gather information on failed authentication events and network connections.",
  "ps_art_ttp_55": "The YARA rule \"ps_art_ttp_55\" is designed to detect the execution of \"rtlcreateuserthread.exe,\" which is indicative of the use of PowerShell for script execution, aligning with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001. This technique involves the abuse of PowerShell to execute scripts and commands, potentially leading to unauthorized actions or exploitation within a system.",
  "ps_art_ttp_41": "The YARA rule \"ps_art_ttp_41\" is designed to detect the execution of malicious scripts via MSHTA, a legitimate Windows utility, which is indicative of the use of HTA files for script execution, aligning with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001 (PowerShell). This rule specifically targets patterns associated with the abuse of MSHTA to execute scripts from remote locations, a common tactic used by threat actors to deliver and execute payloads in a compromised environment.",
  "generic_powershell_5e70d_585": "The YARA rule \"generic_powershell_5e70d_585\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell scripts that employ various \"invoke-\" prefixed functions indicative of credential theft or data exfiltration from popular applications and services, such as messaging platforms and gaming accounts.   "generic_powershell_0cfb1_278": "The YARA rule \"generic_powershell_0cfb1_278\" is designed to detect the use of obfuscated PowerShell scripts, specifically targeting the \"invoke-obfuscation\" technique, which is commonly employed by threat actors to evade detection and execute malicious code. This rule aligns with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, indicating its focus on identifying potentially harmful command and scripting interpreter activities within a system.",
  "invoke_twitterbot": "The YARA rule \"invoke_twitterbot\" is designed to detect the execution of scripts or commands that utilize the \"invoke-twitterbot\" string, indicating potential use of PowerShell for automated interaction with Twitter, which aligns with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001 (PowerShell).   "malicious_killprocess_script": "The YARA rule \"malicious_killprocess_script\" is designed to detect the execution of a potentially malicious PowerShell script that attempts to terminate remote processes, which may indicate unauthorized process manipulation or privilege escalation attempts. This rule targets the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, focusing on the misuse of scripting to disrupt or control system operations by identifying specific strings related to process termination errors and administrative privilege requirements.",
  "generic_powershell_ddbae_366": "The YARA rule \"generic_powershell_ddbae_366\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell scripts that invoke the \"invoke-watson\" function, which is indicative of the execution of potentially malicious or unauthorized code via PowerShell, aligning with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001 (PowerShell). This rule targets the identification of scripts that may be used for post-exploitation activities, such as system reconnaissance or lateral movement within a compromised environment.",
  "powershell_functions_trawler": "The YARA rule \"powershell_functions_trawler\" is designed to detect potentially malicious PowerShell scripts that exhibit characteristics of command-line interface abuse, specifically targeting scripts that may be involved in reconnaissance or execution of unauthorized commands, as indicated by the presence of suspicious function names and object creation patterns. This rule aligns with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which pertains to the use of PowerShell for executing malicious activities.",
  "invoke_mimi": "The YARA rule \"invoke_mimi\" is designed to detect the use of the \"Invoke-Mimikatz\" command, which is associated with the execution of Mimikatz, a tool used for credential theft and privilege escalation. This detection aligns with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, indicating the use of PowerShell for malicious scripting activities.",
  "malicious_powershell_script_sample": "The YARA rule \"malicious_powershell_script_sample\" is designed to detect potentially malicious PowerShell scripts that utilize specific strings and patterns, such as \"missing-checkver,\" \"@args,\" \"convert-path,\" and \"scoop_home,\" which may indicate the use of PowerShell for executing unauthorized or harmful commands, aligning with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001 (Command and Scripting Interpreter: PowerShell). This rule targets scripts that may be leveraging PowerShell to perform actions like file path conversions or argument manipulations, potentially as part of a broader attack strategy involving script-based execution.",
  "generic_powershell_fe6e8_520": "The YARA rule \"generic_powershell_fe6e8_520\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell scripts that invoke shellcode execution, specifically identifying the string \"invoke-kematianshellcode,\" which is indicative of a technique used for executing arbitrary code in memory. This detection aligns with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, highlighting the use of PowerShell for command and script execution, often leveraged by threat actors for post-exploitation activities.",
  "ps_windows_google_chrome_load_unpacked_extension_with_command_line": "The YARA rule \"ps_windows_google_chrome_load_unpacked_extension_with_command_line\" is designed to detect the potentially malicious behavior of loading unpacked extensions in Google Chrome using the `--load-extension` parameter, which may be indicative of an attempt to execute unauthorized or harmful code via browser extensions. This rule specifically targets the technique of leveraging PowerShell commands, such as `invoke-webrequest`, and references to \"github\" to download or execute scripts, aligning with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1176, which involves browser extension abuse for persistence or data exfiltration.",
  "maliciousfileandftp": "The YARA rule \"maliciousfileandftp\" is designed to detect a script that performs file encryption, organizes the encrypted files into a random subfolder, compresses them, and subsequently exfiltrates the compressed archive via FTP, indicating a potential ransomware or data exfiltration operation. This rule specifically targets the use of PowerShell commands for file manipulation and FTP credentials for unauthorized data transfer, aligning with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves command and scripting interpreter abuse.",
  "ps_adgroupmember_query_admins": "The YARA rule \"ps_adgroupmember_query_admins\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell commands that query Active Directory group memberships, specifically targeting groups such as \"administrators\" and \"admins,\" which may indicate an attempt to enumerate privileged accounts for potential lateral movement or privilege escalation. This activity aligns with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the use of PowerShell for malicious purposes.",
  "invoke_passwordsprayews": "The YARA rule \"invoke_passwordsprayews\" is designed to detect the use of a PowerShell script that performs password spraying attacks against Exchange Web Services (EWS), a technique categorized under MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001 (PowerShell). This rule identifies attempts to exploit weak credentials by systematically trying common passwords across multiple accounts, potentially leading to unauthorized access to email services.",
  "powershell_test_script_detection": "The YARA rule \"powershell_test_script_detection\" is designed to identify PowerShell scripts that exhibit characteristics typical of testing frameworks, such as Pester, which may be leveraged by adversaries to execute scripts in a controlled environment, potentially bypassing security measures. This detection aligns with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, indicating the use of PowerShell for script execution, which can be exploited for malicious purposes such as privilege escalation or lateral movement.",
  "powershell_testframework_detection": "The YARA rule \"powershell_testframework_detection\" is designed to identify the execution of scripts associated with PowerShell testing frameworks, specifically targeting the use of Pester, a popular testing tool, which may be leveraged by adversaries to execute unauthorized or malicious code under the guise of legitimate testing activities. This detection aligns with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, indicating the use of PowerShell for script-based execution, potentially facilitating lateral movement or privilege escalation within a compromised environment.",
  "ps_art_ttp_105": "The YARA rule \"ps_art_ttp_105\" is designed to detect the use of Ngrok for tunneling and PowerShell's \"start-job -scriptblock\" command, indicating potential unauthorized remote access and execution of scripts, aligning with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves PowerShell-based command and scripting interpreter activities. This rule targets behaviors associated with establishing covert communication channels and executing remote tasks, often used in lateral movement or persistence by threat actors.",
  "get_gpppassworddetection": "The YARA rule \"get_gpppassworddetection\" is designed to identify PowerShell scripts that attempt to exploit Group Policy Preferences (GPP) to retrieve and decrypt stored passwords, a technique associated with credential access under MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001. This detection focuses on scripts that utilize specific functions and strings, such as \"get-gpppassword\" and \"get-decryptedcpassword,\" to access and decode passwords stored in SYSVOL directories, potentially exposing sensitive credentials to unauthorized users.",
  "malicious_user_removal_script": "The YARA rule \"malicious_user_removal_script\" is designed to detect the execution of PowerShell scripts that are likely involved in unauthorized user removal activities within Azure Active Directory environments, specifically targeting the use of the \"remove-azaduser\" command with the \"-confirm:$false\" parameter to bypass confirmation prompts, indicative of potential misuse or malicious intent. This rule aligns with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which pertains to the use of PowerShell for execution, highlighting a threat category involving unauthorized account manipulation and potential insider threats.",
  "powershell_newfixture_analysis": "The YARA rule \"powershell_newfixture_analysis\" is designed to detect potentially malicious PowerShell scripts that exhibit behavior associated with the creation and manipulation of fixtures, specifically through the use of commands such as \"new-fixture,\" \"join-path,\" and location management with \"push-location\" and \"pop-location.\" This rule targets scripts that may leverage the PowerShell scripting environment to execute unauthorized or suspicious activities, aligning with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which pertains to the use of PowerShell for execution.",
  "ps_credential_access_363": "The YARA rule \"ps_credential_access_363\" is designed to detect attempts to access or manipulate credentials in memory by identifying the use of the \".getbytes(\\\"ntpassword`0\\\")\" pattern, which is indicative of PowerShell scripts targeting credential theft. This rule aligns with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, highlighting the use of PowerShell for malicious activities related to credential access.",
  "ps_export_certificate_1192": "The YARA rule \"ps_export_certificate_1192\" is designed to detect the use of the PowerShell command \"export-pfxcertificate,\" which is associated with the technique of exporting digital certificates from a system, potentially indicating credential theft or unauthorized access to sensitive certificate data.   "getuserspns_malicious_script_detection": "The YARA rule \"getuserspns_malicious_script_detection\" is designed to identify PowerShell scripts associated with the kerberoasting technique, which targets service principal names (SPNs) in Active Directory to extract and crack Kerberos ticket hashes for privilege escalation. This rule detects specific strings indicative of querying domain information and manipulating SPNs, aligning with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves command and scripting interpreter abuse.",
  "timedscreenshot_maliciousdetection": "The YARA rule \"timedscreenshot_maliciousdetection\" is designed to identify the execution of a PowerShell script that captures screenshots at timed intervals, potentially indicating unauthorized surveillance or data exfiltration activities. It detects specific script patterns associated with downloading and executing a script from a remote source, leveraging MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which pertains to PowerShell-based command and scripting interpreter abuse.",
  "nishang_information_collection": "The YARA rule \"nishang_information_collection\" is designed to detect the use of the Nishang tool, which is a PowerShell-based framework used for information gathering and reconnaissance activities.   "get_netbios_interface_monitoring": "The YARA rule \"get_netbios_interface_monitoring\" is designed to detect potential unauthorized enumeration of network interfaces, which may indicate adversarial reconnaissance activities aimed at gathering information about network configurations and connected devices.   "malicious_powershell_script_0931f": "The YARA rule \"malicious_powershell_script_0931f\" is designed to detect the execution of a malicious PowerShell script that leverages Mimikatz for credential theft and Impacket for lateral movement, specifically targeting techniques related to Kerberos ticket manipulation and remote code execution.   "decrypt_rdcman_script": "The YARA rule \"decrypt_rdcman_script\" is designed to detect the execution of a PowerShell script that attempts to decrypt passwords stored in the Remote Desktop Connection Manager (RDCMan) by leveraging DPAPI decryption techniques.   "gherkin_pester_detection": "The YARA rule \"gherkin_pester_detection\" is designed to identify scripts that utilize Gherkin syntax within Pester testing frameworks, potentially indicating the execution of unauthorized or malicious testing activities that leverage scripting capabilities for system management, aligning with the MITRE technique T1059.001, which pertains to command and scripting interpreter abuse.   "malicious_powershell_disassembly": "The YARA rule \"malicious_powershell_disassembly\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell scripts that perform disassembly operations, potentially indicating the presence of malicious activity where attackers are attempting to analyze or reverse-engineer code using the Capstone disassembly framework. This rule targets specific strings associated with the manipulation of disassembly syntax and detail settings, which align with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, highlighting the use of scripting for adversarial purposes.",
  "powershell_artifact_cleaner": "The YARA rule \"powershell_artifact_cleaner\" is designed to detect a suspicious PowerShell script that attempts to clean or obfuscate traces of malicious activity, potentially indicating the use of a technique for defense evasion or artifact removal, as referenced by MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001.   "clm_bypass_technique": "The YARA rule \"clm_bypass_technique\" is designed to detect a PowerShell script that employs a technique to bypass AppLocker, a Windows application whitelisting feature, by identifying specific strings indicative of the script's execution stages.   "keylogger_detection": "The YARA rule \"keylogger_detection\" is designed to identify a malicious PowerShell script that functions as a keylogger, capturing keystrokes and potentially exfiltrating them via email using SMTP.   "malicious_powershell_script_3a5c5": "The YARA rule \"malicious_powershell_script_3a5c5\" is designed to detect the execution of potentially malicious PowerShell scripts that may be leveraging the technique of command and script interpreter abuse, specifically targeting PowerShell (MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001).   "sharpstay_detection": "The YARA rule \"sharpstay_detection\" is designed to identify suspicious PowerShell activity indicative of the execution of SharpStay, a tool used for in-memory execution of .NET assemblies, which aligns with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001 (PowerShell).   "ps_caldera_ttp_5": "The YARA rule \"ps_caldera_ttp_5\" is designed to detect the presence of the \"sandcat.go\" string, which is associated with the execution of scripts using the Go programming language, a technique categorized under MITRE ATT&CK ID T1059.001.   "sendtopastebin_powershell_malware": "The YARA rule \"sendtopastebin_powershell_malware\" is designed to detect a malicious PowerShell script that uploads file contents to Pastebin, potentially exfiltrating sensitive data.   "malicious_hiddenfolder_script": "The YARA rule \"malicious_hiddenfolder_script\" is designed to detect PowerShell scripts that manipulate super hidden system folders, potentially indicating unauthorized access or modification attempts.   "malicious_powershell_coveragescript": "The YARA rule \"malicious_powershell_coveragescript\" is designed to detect potentially malicious PowerShell scripts that utilize coverage analysis mechanisms, which may be indicative of adversaries attempting to analyze or manipulate code execution paths for evasion or exploitation purposes.   "invoke_poshrathttp_detection": "The YARA rule \"invoke_poshrathttp_detection\" is designed to identify the execution of a malicious PowerShell script, \"invoke-poshrathttp,\" which leverages obfuscated commands and network communication to download and execute payloads from a remote server, potentially indicating a Remote Access Trojan (RAT) deployment.   "ps_snmp_scan": "The YARA rule \"ps_snmp_scan\" is designed to detect the presence of SNMP scanning activity, which is indicative of network reconnaissance efforts aimed at identifying SNMP-enabled devices for potential exploitation. This behavior aligns with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves command-line interface usage for executing scripts or commands that facilitate unauthorized network exploration.",
  "invoke_kerberoas": "The YARA rule \"invoke_kerberoas\" is designed to detect the presence of scripts or commands related to \"Invoke-Kerberoast,\" a technique used for Kerberoasting, which involves extracting service account tickets from Active Directory to crack their passwords offline. This detection aligns with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, indicating the use of PowerShell for executing this credential theft method.",
  "generic_powershell_72692_509": "The YARA rule \"generic_powershell_72692_509\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell scripts that invoke potentially malicious functions, specifically identifying the presence of the string \"invoke-nancy,\" which may indicate the execution of unauthorized or harmful code.   "invoke_executemsbuildhelper": "The YARA rule \"invoke_executemsbuildhelper\" is designed to detect the use of a PowerShell script or command that invokes the \"Execute-MSBuildHelper\" function, which is indicative of a technique used to execute code via MSBuild, a legitimate Windows tool, as part of a script-based attack. This behavior aligns with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the use of PowerShell for execution, potentially allowing attackers to run arbitrary code and evade detection by leveraging trusted system utilities.",
  "ps_obfuscation_338": "The YARA rule \"ps_obfuscation_338\" is designed to detect obfuscated PowerShell scripts that utilize string manipulation techniques, specifically the use of methods like `Trim`, `TrimStart`, and `Split` with irregular spacing, indicative of attempts to evade detection and analysis. This rule targets the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the execution of obfuscated scripts to conceal malicious activities.",
  "newjscriptshell_detection": "The YARA rule \"newjscriptshell_detection\" is designed to identify the execution of a PowerShell script that manipulates Windows Management Instrumentation (WMI) objects, potentially for malicious purposes such as persistence or lateral movement, by detecting specific strings related to the creation and management of WMI filters, consumers, and bindings. This activity is associated with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the use of scripting for execution, indicating a potential threat of script-based attacks leveraging WMI for system manipulation or compromise.",
  "malicious_ad_login_check": "The YARA rule \"malicious_ad_login_check\" is designed to detect a PowerShell script that performs Active Directory login checks and logs the results, potentially indicating unauthorized attempts to access or compromise AD credentials. This rule targets the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the use of PowerShell for execution, and identifies specific strings that suggest the script is checking for compromised accounts and logging login attempts, thereby signaling possible credential theft or lateral movement activities.",
  "bruteforce_powershell_script": "The YARA rule \"bruteforce_powershell_script\" is designed to detect malicious PowerShell scripts that perform brute force attacks by attempting to discover hosts, fetch accounts, and match passwords, indicative of unauthorized attempts to gain access to systems.   "getcimdnscache_script": "The YARA rule \"getcimdnscache_script\" is designed to detect the use of a PowerShell function that queries the DNS cache, which may indicate an attempt to gather network reconnaissance information or perform lateral movement within a network. This activity is associated with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the use of PowerShell for executing malicious scripts or commands.",
  "ps_injection_264": "The YARA rule \"ps_injection_264\" is designed to detect instances of PowerShell injection, specifically targeting the technique identified by MITRE ATT&CK as T1059.001, which involves the execution of malicious scripts or commands via PowerShell. The detection string \"$s = '/eid5pdozaaaaefrqvbsuvzi'\" suggests the rule is tailored to identify specific encoded or obfuscated payloads indicative of unauthorized or harmful PowerShell activity.",
  "pester_powershell_testartifact": "The YARA rule \"pester_powershell_testartifact\" is designed to detect the execution of PowerShell scripts that utilize Pester, a testing framework, by identifying specific patterns related to test execution and logging, which may indicate unauthorized or malicious use of scripting for system reconnaissance or manipulation. This rule targets the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the abuse of PowerShell for executing scripts that could potentially be leveraged for adversarial purposes such as automated testing or data exfiltration.",
  "audit_event_log_script": "The YARA rule \"audit_event_log_script\" is designed to detect PowerShell scripts that perform unauthorized auditing of Windows event logs, specifically targeting the Application log, by leveraging the Get-WinEvent cmdlet and outputting event details such as event ID and level. This activity aligns with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the use of scripting for system discovery and potential data exfiltration, indicating a possible reconnaissance or information-gathering phase by a threat actor.",
  "ps_atomictest_fixed_clsids": "The YARA rule \"ps_atomictest_fixed_clsids\" is designed to detect the execution of malicious scripts or commands via the Windows Command and Scripting Interpreter technique (MITRE ATT&CK T1059.001), specifically targeting the use of a fixed CLSID associated with the \"atomictest.dll\" and the \"rundll32.exe\" process, which may indicate an attempt to execute or load potentially harmful code through COM objects. This rule identifies suspicious registry entries and command-line patterns that are indicative of script-based attacks leveraging COM components for persistence or execution.",
  "reverse_screenstream_detection": "The YARA rule \"reverse_screenstream_detection\" is designed to identify malicious PowerShell scripts that facilitate unauthorized screen data streaming by detecting specific patterns associated with HTTP communication, image data transmission, and error handling.   "invoke_poshrat": "The YARA rule \"invoke_poshrat\" is designed to detect the presence of the \"invoke-poshrat\" string, which is indicative of a PowerShell-based remote access trojan (RAT) used for executing malicious scripts and commands on compromised systems.   "ps_mimikatz_234": "The YARA rule \"ps_mimikatz_234\" is designed to detect the presence of the string \"mimikatz,\" which is indicative of the use of the Mimikatz tool, a post-exploitation utility commonly used for credential dumping from Windows systems.   "malicious_powershell_manipulator": "The YARA rule \"malicious_powershell_manipulator\" is designed to detect potentially malicious PowerShell scripts that manipulate registry keys, particularly within the HKCU hive, and perform role-based access checks, which are indicative of the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001 (PowerShell).   "potential_malicious_powershell_activity": "The YARA rule \"potential_malicious_powershell_activity\" is designed to detect suspicious PowerShell script activity indicative of potential malicious behavior, specifically targeting techniques related to patching MSI and ETW (Event Tracing for Windows) processes, which may be used to evade detection or manipulate system logging.   "malicious_progressbar_detection": "The YARA rule \"malicious_progressbar_detection\" is designed to identify potentially malicious activities involving PowerShell scripts that utilize progress bars, specifically targeting behaviors associated with system enumeration and network adapter querying, which align with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001 (PowerShell).   "ps_token_manipulation_1068": "The YARA rule \"ps_token_manipulation_1068\" is designed to detect the use of the \"openthreadtoken\" function, which is indicative of token manipulation techniques used by adversaries to escalate privileges or bypass security controls within a system.   "malicious_service_access_check": "The YARA rule \"malicious_service_access_check\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell scripts that enumerate and potentially manipulate service access permissions, indicating possible privilege escalation or unauthorized access attempts.   "screencapture_powershell": "The YARA rule \"screencapture_powershell\" is designed to detect a PowerShell script that captures screenshots of a user's screen, potentially indicating unauthorized surveillance or data exfiltration activities.   "domainpasswordspray_detection": "The YARA rule \"domainpasswordspray_detection\" is designed to identify PowerShell scripts that implement password spray attacks, a technique where an attacker attempts to gain unauthorized access by systematically trying a few common passwords across many accounts to avoid detection and account lockout.   "generic_powershell_98deb_510": "The YARA rule \"generic_powershell_98deb_510\" is designed to detect the use of the \"invoke-shadowspray\" command, which is indicative of a PowerShell-based code execution technique commonly associated with adversaries leveraging scripting for malicious purposes.   "ps_mimikatz_354": "The YARA rule \"ps_mimikatz_354\" is designed to detect the presence of Mimikatz, a post-exploitation tool used for credential extraction, specifically targeting the \"crypto::certificates\" command indicative of attempts to access or manipulate certificate data.   "generic_powershell_dfdce_690": "The YARA rule \"generic_powershell_dfdce_690\" is designed to detect the use of specific PowerShell scripts or functions, such as \"invoke-threadedfunction\" and \"invoke-userhunter,\" which are indicative of adversaries leveraging PowerShell for execution and lateral movement within a network, aligning with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001 (PowerShell).   "powershell_encode_command": "The YARA rule \"powershell_encode_command\" is designed to detect potentially malicious activity involving the encoding and compression of commands within PowerShell scripts, which may indicate attempts to obfuscate or execute hidden payloads.   "suspected_malicious_file_copy": "The YARA rule \"suspected_malicious_file_copy\" is designed to detect potentially malicious activities involving file copying operations executed through PowerShell scripts, specifically targeting behaviors associated with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves command and scripting interpreter usage.   "generic_powershell_eeaad_695": "The YARA rule \"generic_powershell_eeaad_695\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell scripts that invoke reverse shells, a technique associated with remote code execution and unauthorized access, as indicated by the detection string \"invoke-revsh.\" This rule targets the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the abuse of PowerShell for executing malicious commands and scripts.",
  "ps_obfuscation_270": "The YARA rule \"ps_obfuscation_270\" is designed to detect obfuscated PowerShell scripts that utilize the \"-bxor\" operator, a technique often employed to evade detection by security systems.   "invoke_empire": "The YARA rule \"invoke_empire\" is designed to detect the presence of the \"Invoke-Empire\" command, which is associated with the use of PowerShell for executing Empire, a post-exploitation framework commonly used for command and control activities.   "ps_art_ttp_26": "The YARA rule \"ps_art_ttp_26\" is designed to detect the presence of a malicious Excel macro file, specifically named \"phishingattachment.xlsm,\" which is associated with phishing attacks leveraging the MITRE technique T1059.001, indicating the use of PowerShell for execution.   "ps_windows_task_scheduler_via_vba": "The YARA rule \"ps_windows_task_scheduler_via_vba\" is designed to detect the use of VBA macros that leverage the Windows API to schedule tasks for executing code, specifically targeting the execution of \"notepad.exe,\" which may indicate an attempt to persist malicious activity on a system.   "ps_atomic_test": "The YARA rule \"ps_atomic_test\" is designed to detect the presence of the \"7e8ee7_atomictest.dll\" file, which is associated with the execution of PowerShell scripts for malicious purposes, specifically targeting the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001 (PowerShell).   "ps_createthread_1058": "The YARA rule \"ps_createthread_1058\" is designed to detect the use of the \"createthread\" function, which is indicative of process injection techniques commonly associated with adversaries executing malicious code within the address space of another process.   "ps_art_ttp_32": "The YARA rule \"ps_art_ttp_32\" is designed to detect malicious PowerShell scripts that establish network connections using TCP clients and SSL streams, potentially for executing remote commands or scripts, indicative of the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001 (PowerShell).   "ps_gotoassist_files": "The YARA rule \"ps_gotoassist_files\" is designed to detect the execution of potentially malicious scripts, specifically targeting the use of \"cscript\" to run a Visual Basic Script named \"recon.vbs,\" which may indicate script-based reconnaissance activities.   "malicious_powershell_activity_89476": "The YARA rule \"malicious_powershell_activity_89476\" is designed to detect potentially malicious PowerShell scripts that execute commands from a remote server, leveraging techniques such as web requests and encoding manipulations to bypass security measures.   "ps_caldera_ttp_46": "The YARA rule \"ps_caldera_ttp_46\" is designed to detect potential credential harvesting or unauthorized access attempts by identifying strings commonly associated with credential storage or manipulation, such as \"username =\", \"password =\", and \"session =\", along with the suspicious use of \"users\\\\public\\\\svchost.exe\" which may indicate the presence of a malicious executable masquerading as a legitimate system process.   "ps_caldera_ttp_47": "The YARA rule \"ps_caldera_ttp_47\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell scripts associated with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the execution of commands and scripts via PowerShell.   "potential_firewallrule_misuse": "The YARA rule \"potential_firewallrule_misuse\" is designed to detect the misuse of PowerShell scripts that manipulate firewall rules, potentially indicating unauthorized or malicious attempts to alter firewall configurations.   "generic_powershell_e2ddc_254": "The YARA rule \"generic_powershell_e2ddc_254\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell scripts that perform DLL injection, a technique often employed by adversaries to execute malicious code within the address space of another process, as indicated by the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001.   "nexys_powershell_detection": "The YARA rule \"nexys_powershell_detection\" is designed to identify potentially malicious activity associated with a PowerShell script named \"nexys,\" which appears to execute a multi-stage process involving initialization, bypass attempts, remote operations, and failure handling.   "ps_winapi_memoryallocation": "The YARA rule \"ps_winapi_memoryallocation\" is designed to detect the use of specific Windows API functions, such as VirtualAlloc, VirtualAllocEx, and HeapAlloc, which are commonly employed by malware to allocate memory for code execution or data storage, indicative of process injection or code obfuscation techniques.   "get_screenshot_allwindows": "The YARA rule \"get_screenshot_allwindows\" is designed to detect potentially malicious PowerShell scripts that perform screenshot-taking activities across all windows, utilizing techniques such as Base64 encoding, dynamic assembly loading, and web client manipulation, which are indicative of adversary tactics for data exfiltration and reconnaissance.   "ps_art_ttp_33": "The YARA rule \"ps_art_ttp_33\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell scripts that enumerate processes, specifically targeting the \"explorer\" process, and potentially manipulate or inject DLLs into executable files, indicative of process injection or manipulation techniques.   "ps_reverse_shell_297": "The YARA rule \"ps_reverse_shell_297\" is designed to detect the presence of a reverse shell implemented in PowerShell scripts, specifically identifying the use of \"useshellexecute\" and \"tcpclient\" strings, which are indicative of network-based command execution and communication techniques.   "outjava_nishang": "The YARA rule \"outjava_nishang\" is designed to detect the presence of Nishang, a post-exploitation framework that leverages PowerShell for executing malicious payloads, specifically targeting the use of Java-based execution techniques.
