  "ps_art_ttp_27": "The YARA rule \"ps_art_ttp_27\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell scripts associated with credential manipulation, specifically targeting the execution of scripts that attempt to change or set account passwords, as indicated by the presence of strings such as \"credfile,\" \"newcred.password,\" and \"set-adaccountpassword.\" This rule aligns with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the use of PowerShell for malicious purposes, often seen in adversary tactics for credential access and privilege escalation.",
  "generic_powershell_1ea7a_419": "The YARA rule \"generic_powershell_1ea7a_419\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell scripts that invoke the \"invoke-psgcat\" command, which is indicative of potentially malicious activity related to script-based code execution or obfuscation techniques.   "generic_powershell_d4a3a_396": "The YARA rule \"generic_powershell_d4a3a_396\" is designed to detect the use of the \"invoke-kerberoast\" command within PowerShell scripts, which is indicative of the Kerberoasting attack technique.   "suspicious_sqlquery_execution": "The YARA rule \"suspicious_sqlquery_execution\" is designed to detect potentially unauthorized execution of PowerShell scripts targeting SQL Server databases, specifically identifying attempts to access database credentials and configurations, as well as querying the SQL Server version, which aligns with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001 (PowerShell).   "bypass_sdclt_bypass": "The YARA rule \"bypass_sdclt_bypass\" is designed to detect scripts that exploit the sdclt.exe utility to bypass User Account Control (UAC) on Windows systems, a technique categorized under MITRE ATT&CK ID T1059.001 (Command and Scripting Interpreter: PowerShell).   "detect_nishang_encode_script": "The YARA rule \"detect_nishang_encode_script\" is designed to identify scripts associated with the Nishang framework that utilize PowerShell for encoding and compressing data, specifically through techniques such as base64 encoding and the use of encoded commands.   "disable_amsi_detection_4d2a3": "The YARA rule \"disable_amsi_detection_4d2a3\" is designed to detect malicious PowerShell scripts that attempt to disable the Antimalware Scan Interface (AMSI), a technique commonly used by adversaries to bypass security mechanisms and execute malicious code undetected.   "ps_uac_bypass_sdclt": "The YARA rule \"ps_uac_bypass_sdclt\" is designed to detect attempts to bypass User Account Control (UAC) using the sdclt.exe executable, a known technique for privilege escalation.   "malicious_powershell_xmlbypass": "The YARA rule \"malicious_powershell_xmlbypass\" is designed to detect PowerShell scripts that exploit CVE-2018-8492 to bypass application whitelisting mechanisms by leveraging the creation of MSXML2 COM objects and executing scripts in a constrained language mode.   "mangoprivilegeescalation": "The YARA rule \"mangoprivilegeescalation\" is designed to detect scripts associated with privilege escalation attempts in the Mango v(1.0) application, specifically targeting techniques that exploit scheduled tasks, system startup configurations, unquoted service paths, and potential password exposures.   "sherlock_powershell_exploit_detection_d1178": "The YARA rule \"sherlock_powershell_exploit_detection_d1178\" is designed to identify potentially malicious activity associated with the exploitation of system vulnerabilities using the Sherlock PowerShell script, which is known for enumerating and exploiting privilege escalation vulnerabilities on Windows systems.   "pesterbelike_powershell": "The YARA rule \"pesterbelike_powershell\" is designed to detect the potential misuse of the Pester testing framework's assertion operator in PowerShell scripts, which may be leveraged by adversaries to execute unauthorized code or manipulate script behavior, aligning with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001 (PowerShell).   "powershell_eternalblue": "The YARA rule \"powershell_eternalblue\" is designed to detect the presence of a PowerShell script that exploits the MS17-010 vulnerability, known as EternalBlue, which targets the Server Message Block (SMB) protocol to execute arbitrary code on vulnerable Windows systems.   "powershell_credstealer_detection": "The YARA rule \"powershell_credstealer_detection\" is designed to identify malicious PowerShell scripts that attempt to steal passwords and credentials by leveraging techniques such as SAM database access, privilege escalation, and decryption of stored credentials, as indicated by detection strings related to system environment manipulation, error handling, and specific script names.   "potential_malicious_powershell_script_f88da": "The YARA rule \"potential_malicious_powershell_script_f88da\" is designed to detect PowerShell scripts that potentially exhibit malicious behavior by utilizing AES encryption and decryption techniques, often indicative of obfuscation or data exfiltration activities.   "ps1_to_exe_detection": "The \"ps1_to_exe_detection\" YARA rule is designed to identify the use of PowerShell scripts that convert or execute code as an executable, leveraging techniques such as dynamic invocation of Windows API functions (e.g., GetProcAddress, LoadLibrary) and manipulation of memory protections (e.g., VirtualProtect).   "amsi_scan_interception": "The YARA rule \"amsi_scan_interception\" is designed to detect attempts to intercept or manipulate the AMSI (Antimalware Scan Interface) scan function, a technique often employed by adversaries to bypass or disable security scanning mechanisms in scripting environments, as categorized under MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001.   "ps_readprocessmemory_1063": "The YARA rule \"ps_readprocessmemory_1063\" is designed to detect the use of the ReadProcessMemory function, which is commonly employed by adversaries to read the memory of other processes for data exfiltration or to facilitate code injection.   "ps_invoke_dcsyncth_2": "The YARA rule \"ps_invoke_dcsyncth_2\" is designed to detect the execution of PowerShell scripts that load Mimikatz into memory and perform the DCSync attack technique, which is used to extract credentials from a domain controller by impersonating domain controller replication requests.   "ps_injection_271": "The YARA rule \"ps_injection_271\" is designed to detect the use of the VirtualProtect function, which is commonly associated with process injection techniques, specifically targeting the PowerShell scripting environment as indicated by MITRE technique T1059.001.   "pesterbelikedetection": "The YARA rule \"pesterbelikedetection\" is designed to identify suspicious activity related to the misuse of the \"belikeexactly\" function, which may indicate the execution of malicious scripts or commands in a PowerShell environment, aligning with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001 for Command and Scripting Interpreter: PowerShell.   "ps_injection_265": "The YARA rule \"ps_injection_265\" is designed to detect instances of PowerShell injection, specifically targeting the technique identified by MITRE ATT&CK as T1059.001, which involves the execution of malicious scripts or commands via PowerShell.   "hoaxshell": "The YARA rule \"hoaxshell\" is designed to detect the presence of a malicious web shell, specifically identifying scripts that utilize the \"invoke-webrequest\" command in conjunction with the identifier \"hoaxid,\" which may indicate unauthorized remote command execution or data exfiltration activities.   "powershell_amsi_bypass_e496e": "The YARA rule \"powershell_amsi_bypass_e496e\" is designed to detect PowerShell scripts that implement techniques to bypass the Antimalware Scan Interface (AMSI), which is a security feature intended to prevent malicious scripts from executing.   "ps_invoke_domaindictionary": "The YARA rule \"ps_invoke_domaindictionary\" is designed to detect the use of the \"Invoke-DomainDictionary\" command, which is associated with PowerShell scripting for domain enumeration and reconnaissance activities.   "generic_powershell_aa195_119": "The YARA rule \"generic_powershell_aa195_119\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell scripts that invoke cryptographic functions, specifically targeting the \"invoke-cipher\" command, which may indicate attempts to encrypt or decrypt data as part of a malicious activity.   "pester_script_testing_detection": "The YARA rule \"pester_script_testing_detection\" is designed to identify potentially malicious or suspicious activity within PowerShell scripts that utilize the Pester testing framework, specifically targeting unusual or error-prone command patterns that may indicate misuse or exploitation of script testing capabilities.   "non_malicious_powershell_script": "The YARA rule \"non_malicious_powershell_script\" is designed to detect PowerShell scripts that perform benign system administration tasks, such as gathering WMI data, resetting error action preferences, and interacting with network adapters and Windows Update settings, which are associated with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001 (PowerShell).   "malicious_powershell_netactivity": "The YARA rule \"malicious_powershell_netactivity\" is designed to detect potentially malicious PowerShell scripts that perform networking activities, indicative of system reconnaissance and data exfiltration.   "ps_obfuscation_339": "The YARA rule \"ps_obfuscation_339\" is designed to detect obfuscated PowerShell scripts that utilize the \"foreach\" or \"foreach-object\" constructs combined with bitwise XOR operations on character data, a technique often employed to evade detection and analysis.   "invoke_winsatbypass_detection": "The YARA rule \"invoke_winsatbypass_detection\" is designed to identify the execution of scripts or tools that utilize the \"invoke-winsatbypass\" technique to bypass User Account Control (UAC) on Windows systems, potentially allowing unauthorized elevation of privileges.   "malicious_powershell_script_37631": "The YARA rule \"malicious_powershell_script_37631\" is designed to detect PowerShell scripts that exhibit characteristics of malicious activity, specifically targeting the abuse of .NET assemblies and Windows Forms for potentially unauthorized actions such as clipboard manipulation and automated keystroke sending.   "pester_powershell_detection": "The YARA rule \"pester_powershell_detection\" is designed to identify potentially malicious PowerShell scripts that utilize suspicious functions related to path existence checks, which may indicate adversarial use of scripting for system discovery or configuration validation.   "reverse_tcp_powershell": "The YARA rule \"reverse_tcp_powershell\" is designed to detect the execution of a PowerShell script that establishes a reverse TCP connection, commonly used for remote access and control by attackers, and is associated with a specific backdoor script authored by Ivan Sincek.   "malicious_powershell_exitusage": "The YARA rule \"malicious_powershell_exitusage\" is designed to detect potentially malicious PowerShell scripts that misuse or manipulate control flow statements, such as \"break\" and \"continue,\" which may indicate attempts to evade detection or disrupt normal script execution.   "wmi_persistence_detection": "The YARA rule \"wmi_persistence_detection\" is designed to identify suspicious PowerShell scripts that leverage Windows Management Instrumentation (WMI) for persistence, specifically targeting techniques that involve creating or modifying WMI event consumers and bindings to execute scripts or commands automatically.   "gupt_backdoor_detection": "The YARA rule \"gupt_backdoor_detection\" is designed to identify a specific PowerShell script associated with the Gupt backdoor, which is part of the Nishang framework, by detecting strings indicative of malicious activities such as wireless network reconnaissance, execution of obfuscated commands, and downloading payloads.   "ps_powershell_modify_registry_logon_credentials": "The YARA rule \"ps_powershell_modify_registry_logon_credentials\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell scripts that modify the Windows registry to enable the storage of logon credentials in plaintext, specifically targeting the WDigest authentication protocol settings.   "ps_injection_267": "The YARA rule \"ps_injection_267\" is designed to detect the use of the \"SuspendThread\" function, which is indicative of process injection techniques where a malicious actor manipulates the execution flow of a target process to execute arbitrary code.   "malicious_azure_admin_creation": "The YARA rule \"malicious_azure_admin_creation\" is designed to detect potentially unauthorized or suspicious activities involving the creation of administrator accounts within Azure Active Directory, specifically targeting command-line operations and role assignments that may indicate privilege escalation or lateral movement attempts.   "invoke_portknockbackdoor": "The YARA rule \"invoke_portknockbackdoor\" is designed to detect the presence of a script or command that initiates a port-knocking sequence to establish a covert communication channel, typically used to bypass firewall restrictions and gain unauthorized access to a system.   "generic_powershell_fd1e7_456": "The YARA rule \"generic_powershell_fd1e7_456\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell scripts that employ dynamic execution and process injection techniques, which are indicative of malicious activities such as code execution and privilege escalation.   "sharpsniper_powershell_script": "The YARA rule \"sharpsniper_powershell_script\" is designed to detect the execution of a PowerShell script associated with the SharpSniper tool, which is indicative of a code injection or execution technique leveraging PowerShell for malicious purposes.   "powerview_recon_analysis": "The YARA rule \"powerview_recon_analysis\" is designed to detect the execution of PowerView, a PowerShell tool used for network reconnaissance and privilege escalation in Active Directory environments, by identifying specific command patterns associated with domain enumeration, trust mapping, and Kerberoasting activities.   "generic_powershell_aaebf_194": "The YARA rule \"generic_powershell_aaebf_194\" is designed to detect the use of obfuscated or potentially malicious PowerShell scripts that employ specific invocation patterns, such as \"invoke-drycberamsi,\" \"invoke-drycberetw,\" and \"invoke-webrev,\" which may indicate the execution of scripts related to command and scripting interpreter activities.   "powershell_credman_modified": "The YARA rule \"powershell_credman_modified\" is designed to detect modifications of the PowerShell script \"credman.ps1,\" which is potentially used for credential enumeration and manipulation, indicating unauthorized access or credential theft attempts.   "malicious_tcp_shell_244b1": "The YARA rule \"malicious_tcp_shell_244b1\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell scripts that establish TCP connections for remote command execution, leveraging .NET classes such as `TcpClient`, `StreamReader`, and `StreamWriter` to facilitate communication between a compromised host and an attacker-controlled server.   "suspicious_powershell_cleanup": "The YARA rule \"suspicious_powershell_cleanup\" is designed to detect potentially malicious activity involving the use of PowerShell commands to manipulate Azure resources, specifically targeting role assignments, user accounts, and resource groups, which may indicate unauthorized cleanup or removal actions consistent with the MITRE technique T1059.001 (PowerShell).   "potential_disable_realtime_monitoring": "The YARA rule \"potential_disable_realtime_monitoring\" is designed to detect attempts to disable real-time monitoring in Microsoft Defender by identifying the use of the \"Set-MpPreference\" command with the \"-DisableRealtimeMonitoring\" parameter set to \"$true,\" which aligns with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, indicating potential PowerShell-based script execution for security evasion.",
  "generic_powershell_54ee3_253": "The YARA rule \"generic_powershell_54ee3_253\" is designed to detect the use of the \"invoke-snaffler\" command, which is associated with the execution of potentially malicious PowerShell scripts.   "malicious_powershell_activity_07fbf": "The YARA rule \"malicious_powershell_activity_07fbf\" is designed to detect potentially malicious PowerShell activity indicative of code execution and file manipulation, specifically targeting behaviors associated with the abuse of system libraries and cryptographic functions, as well as unauthorized installation processes.   "powershell_amsipatch_451a9": "The YARA rule \"powershell_amsipatch_451a9\" is designed to detect the manipulation of the AMSI (Antimalware Scan Interface) by identifying attempts to patch or modify the amsi.dll file, specifically targeting the \"amsiscanbuffer\" function, which is a technique used to disable or bypass antimalware scanning capabilities in PowerShell scripts.   "suspicious_process_termination": "The YARA rule \"suspicious_process_termination\" is designed to detect scripts that repeatedly terminate processes using PowerShell commands, indicative of potential abuse of scripting for process manipulation.   "generic_powershell_adb0e_132": "The YARA rule \"generic_powershell_adb0e_132\" is designed to detect potentially malicious PowerShell scripts that utilize various \"invoke-\" prefixed functions, indicative of scripting techniques for dynamic execution, web requests, and command retries, which align with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001 for PowerShell.   "rdp_persistent_cache": "The YARA rule \"rdp_persistent_cache\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell commands indicative of script-based attacks or malicious activity, specifically targeting the execution of commands related to system enumeration and manipulation, such as \"get-childitem\" and \"remove-variable,\" which align with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001 (PowerShell).   "malicious_dh_powershell": "The YARA rule \"malicious_dh_powershell\" is designed to detect potentially malicious PowerShell scripts that implement Diffie-Hellman key exchange, often used in conjunction with obfuscation techniques such as base64 encoding and RC4 decryption, indicative of command and control activities or data exfiltration.   "ps_obfuscation_273": "The YARA rule \"ps_obfuscation_273\" is designed to detect obfuscated PowerShell scripts, specifically targeting the use of the technique where property names are manipulated or concatenated in a non-standard manner, as indicated by the pattern \"$_.name-clike'\".   "powershell_keylogger_61ded": "The YARA rule \"powershell_keylogger_61ded\" is designed to detect a malicious PowerShell script exhibiting keylogger capabilities, characterized by its ability to capture and log keystrokes to a specified file, implement delays using \"start-sleep,\" and potentially exfiltrate logged data via SMTP.   "malicious_ad_object_permission": "The YARA rule \"malicious_ad_object_permission\" is designed to detect potentially malicious PowerShell scripts that manipulate Active Directory object permissions, which may indicate unauthorized privilege escalation or lateral movement within a network.   "ps_art_ttp_19": "The YARA rule \"ps_art_ttp_19\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell's \"invoke-expression\" command, which is often leveraged for executing arbitrary code, alongside the presence of \"dynamiccompile.exe\" and references to MITRE technique T1027.004, indicating potential obfuscation or packing methods.   "malicious_powershell_uac_bypass": "The YARA rule \"malicious_powershell_uac_bypass\" is designed to detect the execution of the Nishang \"Invoke-PSUACme\" script, which is used to bypass User Account Control (UAC) in Windows systems by leveraging PowerShell commands to execute potentially malicious payloads with elevated privileges.   "ps_art_ttp_25": "The YARA rule \"ps_art_ttp_25\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell scripts that employ the \"invoke-expression\" command, which is commonly associated with executing arbitrary code, and references to \"dumper.ps1,\" indicating potential data exfiltration or credential dumping activities.   "powershell_malware_deletekeyregister": "The YARA rule \"powershell_malware_deletekeyregister\" is designed to detect malicious PowerShell scripts that attempt to delete registry keys, potentially indicating privilege escalation or unauthorized system modifications.   "ps_shadowcopy_deletion_327": "The YARA rule \"ps_shadowcopy_deletion_327\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell commands that target the deletion of Windows Volume Shadow Copies, a technique often employed by adversaries to hinder data recovery and evade forensic analysis, corresponding to MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001.   "bypassexecpolicy_detection": "The YARA rule \"bypassexecpolicy_detection\" is designed to identify the use of PowerShell scripts that circumvent execution policies, a technique often employed by adversaries to execute unauthorized scripts or commands on a system.   "malicious_powershell_mocking": "The YARA rule \"malicious_powershell_mocking\" is designed to detect the instantiation of suspicious PowerShell objects that may indicate the use of PowerShell for malicious purposes, specifically targeting techniques related to object manipulation and initialization without proper security checks.   "generic_powershell_b9eca_378": "The YARA rule \"generic_powershell_b9eca_378\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell scripts that implement clipboard logging and command-and-control functionalities, as indicated by the presence of strings such as \"invoke-clipboardlogger,\" \"invoke-clipboardc2v,\" and \"invoke-clipboardc2c.\" This rule targets the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the use of PowerShell for malicious activities, specifically focusing on the unauthorized capture and exfiltration of clipboard data.",
  "generic_powershell_2da2a_369": "The YARA rule \"generic_powershell_2da2a_369\" is designed to detect the use of potentially malicious PowerShell scripts, specifically those invoking the \"invoke-chungus\" function, which may indicate the execution of obfuscated or unauthorized scripts.   "malicious_powershell_meterpreter": "The YARA rule \"malicious_powershell_meterpreter\" is designed to detect the presence of a PowerShell script that utilizes the msfvenom tool to generate and deploy a Meterpreter payload, specifically targeting Windows systems with a reverse TCP connection.   "cred_popper_detection": "The YARA rule \"cred_popper_detection\" is designed to identify the execution of the \"cred-popper\" tool within PowerShell scripts, which is indicative of credential harvesting activities.   "detect_progid_search": "The YARA rule \"detect_progid_search\" identifies potentially malicious PowerShell scripts that enumerate CLSIDs and ProgIDs within the Windows Registry, a technique often used by adversaries to discover and exploit COM objects for executing arbitrary code.   "powershell_maliciousscript_detection_543e9": "The YARA rule \"powershell_maliciousscript_detection_543e9\" is designed to detect obfuscated PowerShell scripts that attempt to bypass or disable AMSI (Antimalware Scan Interface) by manipulating memory and invoking methods to evade detection, aligning with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which pertains to the use of PowerShell for execution of malicious code.   "generic_powershell_dea53_530": "The YARA rule \"generic_powershell_dea53_530\" is designed to detect the use of obfuscated or potentially malicious PowerShell scripts, specifically targeting the technique of command and scripting interpreter abuse as outlined in MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001.   "malicious_powershell_activity_54524": "The YARA rule \"malicious_powershell_activity_54524\" is designed to detect PowerShell scripts exhibiting malicious behavior through the use of command-line obfuscation techniques, including base64 encoding, and network activity indicative of downloading potentially harmful content from external sources.   "powershell_passwordspray_detection": "The YARA rule \"powershell_passwordspray_detection\" is designed to identify scripts that perform password spraying attacks using PowerShell, a technique where an attacker attempts to gain unauthorized access by systematically trying a single password against multiple accounts.   "nishangpersistencecleaner": "The YARA rule \"nishangpersistencecleaner\" is designed to detect the use of Nishang scripts, specifically targeting the removal of persistence mechanisms through WMI events and registry modifications, as indicated by the presence of strings related to command execution and registry paths.   "invoke_psgcatagent": "The YARA rule \"invoke_psgcatagent\" is designed to detect the use of the PowerShell command \"invoke-psgcatagent,\" which is associated with the execution of scripts or commands via PowerShell, a technique categorized under MITRE ATT&CK's T1059.001.   "ps_invoke_dcsyncth_1": "The YARA rule \"ps_invoke_dcsyncth_1\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell scripts that invoke the \"DCSync\" technique, which is associated with credential theft by simulating domain controller behavior to extract account credentials from Active Directory.   "ps_staging_local_certificates_via_export_certificate": "The YARA rule \"ps_staging_local_certificates_via_export_certificate\" is designed to detect the malicious behavior of exporting local certificates into a compressed archive, potentially for exfiltration or staging purposes, as indicated by the presence of strings related to certificate export and ZIP file compression.   "ps_injection_266": "The YARA rule \"ps_injection_266\" is designed to detect the presence of process injection techniques, specifically targeting PowerShell scripts, as indicated by the MITRE ATT&CK tag T1059.001.   "decode_base64_nishang": "The YARA rule \"decode_base64_nishang\" is designed to detect scripts or code snippets that utilize Nishang, a post-exploitation framework, to decode Base64-encoded data, often indicative of PowerShell-based obfuscation techniques associated with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001 (PowerShell).   "powershell_winenum_script": "The YARA rule \"powershell_winenum_script\" is designed to detect the execution of a suspicious PowerShell enumeration script that performs extensive system reconnaissance, including gathering information on Active Directory group memberships, password change history, network connections, and firewall configurations.   "ps_eventlog_t1654": "The YARA rule \"ps_eventlog_t1654\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell commands that interact with Windows event logs, specifically targeting the retrieval of logs from the \"security\" and \"system\" categories, which may indicate unauthorized or suspicious activity related to MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001 (PowerShell).   "wmi_backdoor_detection": "The YARA rule \"wmi_backdoor_detection\" is designed to identify the creation or removal of a backdoor utilizing WMI (Windows Management Instrumentation) event subscriptions, which can be leveraged by attackers to execute arbitrary code or commands persistently on a compromised system.   "generic_powershell_d4df4_340": "The YARA rule \"generic_powershell_d4df4_340\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell scripts that employ the \"invoke-powershelltcp\" pattern, indicative of a technique used for establishing reverse TCP connections, commonly associated with remote code execution and command-and-control activities.   "malicious_azure_deployment_script_27b6e": "The YARA rule \"malicious_azure_deployment_script_27b6e\" is designed to detect the execution of a malicious Azure deployment script that potentially engages in credential theft and privilege escalation activities.   "ps_disable_etw_dotnet": "The YARA rule \"ps_disable_etw_dotnet\" is designed to detect attempts to disable Event Tracing for Windows (ETW) in .NET applications by modifying registry properties, specifically targeting the use of PowerShell commands to set the \"etwenabled\" property to 0 within the \".NET Framework\" registry path.   "ps_obfuscation_302": "The YARA rule \"ps_obfuscation_302\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell obfuscation techniques, specifically targeting scripts that create encrypted data streams and manipulate Microsoft Office file formats such as .docx and .xlsx, which aligns with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001 (Command and Scripting Interpreter: PowerShell).   "invoke_serviceenable": "The YARA rule \"invoke_serviceenable\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell commands that enable or modify system services, which is indicative of the technique \"Command and Scripting Interpreter: PowerShell\" (MITRE ATT&CK T1059.001).   "eventvwr_uacbypass": "The YARA rule \"eventvwr_uacbypass\" is designed to detect attempts to bypass User Account Control (UAC) by exploiting the Event Viewer application, a technique categorized under MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001 (Command and Scripting Interpreter: PowerShell).   "maliciouspowershellwebserver": "The YARA rule \"maliciouspowershellwebserver\" is designed to detect potentially malicious activity involving PowerShell scripts that establish a web server, leveraging social engineering and privilege escalation techniques, specifically targeting behaviors associated with the execution of scripts that bypass security controls and elevate privileges, as indicated by the presence of strings related to UAC bypass and script execution. The rule is tagged with MITRE technique T1059.001, which pertains to the use of PowerShell for command and script execution, highlighting its focus on identifying threats that exploit PowerShell for unauthorized access and control.",
  "reverse_shell_badusb": "The YARA rule \"reverse_shell_badusb\" is designed to detect the presence of a reverse shell implemented in a PowerShell script, typically associated with BadUSB attacks, by identifying specific patterns indicative of network communication setup and data manipulation using PowerShell objects and streams. This rule targets the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the use of scripting for execution, highlighting the potential for unauthorized remote access and control over compromised systems.",
  "invoke_openinboxfinder": "The YARA rule \"invoke_openinboxfinder\" is designed to detect the execution of PowerShell scripts that utilize the \"invoke-openinboxfinder\" command, which is indicative of adversaries leveraging PowerShell for script-based execution to potentially access or manipulate email inboxes. This detection aligns with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, highlighting the use of PowerShell for malicious activities such as unauthorized access or data exfiltration.",
  "invoke_dcsync": "The YARA rule \"invoke_dcsync\" is designed to detect the use of the \"Invoke-DCSync\" command, which is associated with the technique T1059.001 (PowerShell) under the MITRE ATT&CK framework. This command is typically used by attackers to simulate domain controller replication and extract sensitive credentials from Active Directory, posing a significant threat to network security.",
  "ps_wmi_atomic": "The YARA rule \"ps_wmi_atomic\" is designed to detect the use of Windows Management Instrumentation (WMI) for executing scripts or commands, specifically targeting the technique identified by MITRE ATT&CK as T1059.001, which involves leveraging WMI for remote execution or automation of malicious activities. The detection pattern \"$s1 = 'win32_atomic'\" suggests monitoring for specific WMI-related strings indicative of potential script-based attacks or system manipulation.",
  "malicious_powershell_execution_9866d": "The YARA rule \"malicious_powershell_execution_9866d\" is designed to detect the execution of PowerShell scripts that manipulate the current directory path by resetting it to a specified location before executing a block of code, indicative of the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001 (PowerShell). This behavior is often associated with attempts to obfuscate script execution paths or evade detection by altering the environment in which the script runs, potentially signaling malicious activity.",
  "ps_reverse_shell_330": "The YARA rule \"ps_reverse_shell_330\" is designed to detect the presence of a reverse shell command executed via the `ps` utility, characterized by the redirection of standard error to standard output (\"2>&1\"), the use of a port range (\"0..65535\"), and a specific pattern of obfuscated command execution using backticks and a sequence of characters. This rule targets the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves command and scripting interpreter abuse for remote shell access, indicating potential unauthorized remote control or data exfiltration activities.",
  "potentially_malicious_network_script": "The YARA rule \"potentially_malicious_network_script\" is designed to detect PowerShell commands that may indicate malicious activities involving network configuration manipulation and system restart operations, specifically targeting commands such as \"netsh interface,\" \"ipv4 uninstall,\" and \"restart-computer.\" This rule aligns with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the use of scripting for execution, potentially allowing attackers to disrupt network settings or force system reboots as part of their intrusion tactics.",
  "generic_powershell_92b6b_274": "The YARA rule \"generic_powershell_92b6b_274\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell scripts that employ the \"invoke-aesdecryption\" function, indicative of attempts to decrypt data using AES encryption, which aligns with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001 for PowerShell-based command and scripting interpreter abuse. This rule targets potential malicious activities involving encrypted payloads or data exfiltration facilitated through PowerShell.",
  "invoke_decode_malicious_detection": "The YARA rule \"invoke_decode_malicious_detection\" is designed to identify the use of the \"invoke-decode\" script from the Nishang framework, which is often employed by threat actors to decode and execute Base64-encoded payloads, potentially facilitating malicious PowerShell activity. This detection aligns with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, indicating the use of PowerShell for command and script execution, which can be leveraged for unauthorized data manipulation or execution of malicious code.",
  "ps_snmp_scan_2": "The YARA rule \"ps_snmp_scan_2\" is designed to detect PowerShell scripts that utilize SNMP scanning techniques, specifically identifying commands related to network reconnaissance and system status checks, such as \"get-snmpvalue\" and \"display-computerstatus.\" This rule targets the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the use of PowerShell for executing malicious scripts to gather information about network configurations and vulnerabilities.",
  "admingroup_membership": "The YARA rule \"admingroup_membership\" is designed to detect the execution of PowerShell scripts that attempt to enumerate members of the local administrators group on a Windows system, which is indicative of reconnaissance activity associated with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001 (PowerShell).   "suspicious_tcp_connection_activity": "The YARA rule \"suspicious_tcp_connection_activity\" is designed to detect attempts to establish unauthorized or failed TCP connections, potentially indicating command and control activity or lateral movement within a network, as characterized by MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001 (PowerShell).   "ps_compress_archive_t1560": "The YARA rule \"ps_compress_archive_t1560\" is designed to detect the presence of a specific compressed archive file named \"t1560-data-ps.zip,\" which is indicative of data compression for exfiltration purposes, aligning with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001 (Command and Scripting Interpreter: PowerShell).   "malicious_powershell_emailharvesting": "The YARA rule \"malicious_powershell_emailharvesting\" is designed to detect potentially malicious PowerShell scripts that target Exchange ActiveSync (EAS) for email account harvesting and password spraying attacks, as indicated by specific strings related to username harvesting, password spraying, and trust manipulation.   "suspicious_netshare_powershell": "The YARA rule \"suspicious_netshare_powershell\" is designed to detect potentially malicious PowerShell scripts that query open network shares, which may indicate lateral movement or reconnaissance activities within a network.   "powershell_testing_script": "The YARA rule \"powershell_testing_script\" is designed to detect the presence of PowerShell scripts utilizing the Pester testing framework, which may be indicative of adversaries leveraging automated testing scripts to validate or manipulate file contents as part of their operations.   "malicious_tcp_shell_9b0bc": "The YARA rule \"malicious_tcp_shell_9b0bc\" is designed to detect the presence of TCP reverse shell activity within PowerShell scripts, specifically identifying scripts that utilize .NET socket programming to establish remote connections and execute commands, as indicated by the use of patterns such as \"system.net.sockets\" and \"iex $data 2>&1\".   "fakelogonscreen_detection": "The YARA rule \"fakelogonscreen_detection\" is designed to identify a suspicious PowerShell script that employs techniques such as Base64 decoding, console output manipulation, and reflection to potentially execute unauthorized code, indicative of the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001 (PowerShell).   "ps_amsi_bypass_310": "The YARA rule \"ps_amsi_bypass_310\" is designed to detect attempts to bypass the Antimalware Scan Interface (AMSI) in PowerShell scripts, a technique commonly used by attackers to evade detection and execute malicious code.   "ps_amsi_bypass_304": "The YARA rule \"ps_amsi_bypass_304\" is designed to detect attempts to bypass the Antimalware Scan Interface (AMSI) in PowerShell scripts, a technique commonly used by adversaries to evade detection and execute malicious code.   "malicious_powershell_script_detection_3e57d": "The YARA rule \"malicious_powershell_script_detection_3e57d\" is designed to identify potentially malicious PowerShell scripts that utilize remote adapter query capabilities, specifically targeting network adapters through Windows Management Instrumentation (WMI) with elevated privileges.   "powershell_wcmdump_credential_access": "The YARA rule \"powershell_wcmdump_credential_access\" is designed to detect the execution of a PowerShell script that attempts to extract Windows credentials from the Credential Manager, utilizing specific function calls and parameters indicative of credential dumping techniques.   "malicious_ad_module": "The YARA rule \"malicious_ad_module\" is designed to detect activities related to the unauthorized manipulation of Active Directory Certificate Services, specifically targeting the addition and embedding of custom certificates within the directory.   "suspicious_powershell_getcimdirectory": "The YARA rule \"suspicious_powershell_getcimdirectory\" is designed to detect potentially malicious activity involving PowerShell scripts that utilize CIM (Common Information Model) sessions to perform remote directory listings, which may indicate lateral movement or unauthorized access attempts within a network.   "ps_maldoc_atomic": "The YARA rule \"ps_maldoc_atomic\" is designed to detect the presence of PowerShell scripts associated with the execution of malicious documents, specifically targeting the use of the \"invoke-maldoc\" function and references to the \"atomic-red-team\" framework, which are indicative of adversaries leveraging PowerShell for malicious document execution as part of the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001 (PowerShell).   "powershell_malicious_activity_4d06d": "The YARA rule \"powershell_malicious_activity_4d06d\" is designed to detect malicious PowerShell activity associated with in-memory execution and code injection techniques, as indicated by the presence of strings related to process manipulation and memory protection functions.   "ps_art_ttp_23": "The YARA rule \"ps_art_ttp_23\" is designed to detect the malicious behavior associated with the dumping of LSASS process memory, which is indicative of credential theft techniques.   "tcpforwarder_powershell_script": "The YARA rule \"tcpforwarder_powershell_script\" is designed to detect the presence of a PowerShell script that implements TCP forwarding, a technique often used by attackers to establish covert communication channels or facilitate lateral movement within a network.   "portscan_tool_detection_49d3f": "The YARA rule \"portscan_tool_detection_49d3f\" is designed to detect the execution of a PowerShell-based port scanning tool, specifically Nishang, which is used to identify open ports on target systems, potentially as part of network reconnaissance activities.   "detect_find_fruit_script": "The YARA rule \"detect_find_fruit_script\" is designed to identify the usage of a script that potentially exploits web servers by leveraging PowerShell commands to perform network reconnaissance and interact with web services, as indicated by the presence of strings related to HTTP requests, service point manipulation, and parallel host pinging.   "nishang_execute_ontime": "The YARA rule \"nishang_execute_ontime\" is designed to detect the use of a specific payload from the Nishang framework, which leverages PowerShell scripts for executing tasks at scheduled times, potentially for malicious purposes.   "generic_powershell_5d6bd_78": "The YARA rule \"generic_powershell_5d6bd_78\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell scripts that employ various \"invoke-\" functions indicative of lateral movement and remote execution techniques, such as SMB, WMI, and SSH, which align with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001 (PowerShell).   "ps_obfuscation_274": "The YARA rule \"ps_obfuscation_274\" is designed to detect obfuscated PowerShell scripts, specifically targeting the technique of command and script obfuscation as outlined in MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001.   "suspicious_powershell_regex_replacer": "The YARA rule \"suspicious_powershell_regex_replacer\" is designed to detect PowerShell scripts that perform regex-based manipulation of file content, which may indicate the use of T1059.001 (PowerShell) for potentially malicious activities such as obfuscation or unauthorized data modification.   "invoke_aclscanner": "The YARA rule \"invoke_aclscanner\" is designed to detect the execution of a PowerShell script that potentially abuses the \"Invoke-ACLScanner\" command, which is indicative of adversaries leveraging PowerShell for system discovery and privilege escalation, aligning with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001.   "ps_art_ttp_36": "The YARA rule \"ps_art_ttp_36\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell scripts that employ the \"-erroraction ignore\" parameter, potentially indicating attempts to suppress error messages during execution, alongside references to \"errorhandler.cmd\" and the \"c:\\\\windows\\\\system32\\\\oobe\\\\setup\" path, which may suggest the presence of a script or tool used for persistence or system manipulation.   "simplehttpserver_detection": "The YARA rule \"simplehttpserver_detection\" is designed to identify a PowerShell script that sets up a local HTTP server, potentially for malicious purposes such as hosting files for unauthorized download, which aligns with the MITRE technique T1059.001 (PowerShell).   "ps_memcopy_1059": "The YARA rule \"ps_memcopy_1059\" is designed to detect the use of the \"memcpy\" function, which is commonly associated with the execution of scripts or code injection techniques, specifically targeting the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001 (Command and Scripting Interpreter: PowerShell).   "ps_art_ttp_22": "The YARA rule \"ps_art_ttp_22\" is designed to detect PowerShell scripts that utilize the \"iex (iwr\" command to execute code from the internet, potentially indicating fileless malware execution, and the use of \"get-passwordvaultcredentials\" and \"getcredmancreds.ps1\" to extract stored credentials, aligning with the MITRE technique T1059.001 for PowerShell-based command and scripting interpreter abuse.   "invoke_downgradeaccount": "The YARA rule \"invoke_downgradeaccount\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell scripts that attempt to downgrade user account privileges, which is indicative of the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001 (PowerShell).   "powershell_arm_bypass": "The YARA rule \"powershell_arm_bypass\" is designed to detect PowerShell scripts that attempt to bypass the Antimalware Scan Interface (AMSI) by utilizing techniques such as manipulating memory protection and invoking specific functions like \"getprocaddress\" and \"virtualprotect.\" This rule targets scripts employing the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the use of PowerShell for executing malicious code while evading security mechanisms.",
  "generic_powershell_c3616_270": "The YARA rule \"generic_powershell_c3616_270\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell scripts that perform token manipulation techniques, specifically targeting the execution of commands such as \"invoke-tokenmanipulation,\" \"invoke-impersonateuser,\" and \"invoke-reverttoself,\" which are indicative of attempts to escalate privileges or impersonate users within a system.   "detect_malicious_powershell_script": "The YARA rule \"detect_malicious_powershell_script\" is designed to identify potentially harmful PowerShell scripts that exhibit characteristics associated with process injection or execution manipulation, as indicated by strings such as \"you must specified,\" \"[+] binding executable,\" \"try to run [,\" and \"not exists.\" This detection aligns with MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the use of PowerShell for malicious purposes, including unauthorized code execution and system compromise.",
  "malicious_admin_deletion": "The YARA rule \"malicious_admin_deletion\" is designed to detect scripts that perform unauthorized removal of administrative users and resource groups in Azure environments, potentially indicating a threat actor's attempt to disrupt or gain control over cloud resources.   "powershell_suspiciousdeployment": "The YARA rule \"powershell_suspiciousdeployment\" is designed to detect potentially malicious use of PowerShell scripts that may be involved in unauthorized Azure resource deployment activities, characterized by the presence of specific command patterns such as \"adduseragent,\" \"test-path,\" \"resourcegroup,\" \"login-azaccount,\" and \"switch context.\" This rule targets the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001, which involves the abuse of PowerShell for executing commands that could facilitate lateral movement or privilege escalation within cloud environments.",
  "generic_powershell_af634_469": "The YARA rule \"generic_powershell_af634_469\" is designed to detect the use of PowerShell scripts that employ the \"invoke-smbremoting\" command, which is indicative of remote code execution via SMB protocol, aligning with the MITRE ATT&CK technique T1059.001 (PowerShell).
